📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 综述得出的结论是,HPV16 E2是病毒生命周期中的关键调节蛋白,在DNA复制和基因表达中发挥着核心作用。还值得注意的是,HPV16 E2在低分化宫颈病变细胞中高表达,可能通过抑制E6和E7癌基因来抑制肿瘤形成。
  • 完成 HPV 疫苗接种 系列的青少年比例为 26.3%(95% 置信区间,23.3-29.3)。与完整的 疫苗接种 呈正相关的因素包括母亲受过中等或以上教育、母亲共同或自主决策、之前的 宫颈癌 筛查以及对 HPV 的良好了解。相反,高信息超载和决策疲劳与完成 疫苗接种 时间表的较低几率相关。
  • 该研究鉴定出 6,462 个体细胞 突变 富含癌症相关通路,特别是 PI3K-Akt 信号传导。PIK3CA 是最常发生突变的基因 (42%),其频率显着高于 HPV16 阳性 TCGA-CESC 肿瘤 (26.21%) (p < 0.04)。与 TCGA-CESC 不同,印度 PIK3CA 突变肿瘤富含晚期疾病。与 PIK3CA 野生肿瘤相比,这些肿瘤表现出较低的病毒载量、较高的肿瘤 突变 负荷和更具侵袭性的分子表型。基于 CIBERSORT 的免疫分析显示,PIK3CA 突变肿瘤和正常组织之间的免疫细胞浸润(包括 CD8+ T 细胞)相当,而 PIK3CA 野生肿瘤与正常组织相比,浸润显着增强。
  • 修正:人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 的日语人工智能代理系统信息监视和公共传播:开发和可行性评估在“人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 信息监视和公共传播的日语人工智能代理系统:开发和可行性评估”中,我们确定了几个需要修正的项目。所有更正都不会改变本文的任何发现或结论。署名原文为:刘俊宇,博士;杨斯文,工学学士;马德秀,博士;牛倩,博士;张泽群,博士;永井谷田桃子,博士;Tomoki Aoyama,医学博士、博士 更正的署名现在为:Junyu Liu,ME;杨斯文,工学学士;马德秀,硕士;牛倩,博士;张泽群,硕士;永井谷田桃子,博士;Tomoki Aoyama,医学博士、博士 其次,在“方法”部分的“报告生成评估方法”部分中,更正了以下文本:两个协议均使用基于 LLM 的法官和标准化评分标准,为每个维度生成从 0 到 5 的分数(多媒体附录 4 和 5)。这使得该段落与同一部分的其余部分(“三名志愿者独立阅读并评分每个报告”)和结果部分(“三名评估员独立评分每个报告部分”)保持一致,这是正确的。下面按它们在文章中出现的顺序列出,提供原始发表的文本和更正的文本。首先,在作者署名中,三位作者的学位名称不正确。现在的文字如下: 两个协议均由 3 位领域专家使用多媒体附录 4 和 5 中提供的标准化评分标准进行应用,为每个维度从 0 到 5 的范围分配分数。每位专家独立对报告的每个部分进行评分。表 5 中报告的值和 M。

  • This reporting window highlights the following Human papillomaviruses news developments.
  • A cross-sectional study identified a high prevalence of high-risk HPV (hrHPV) among asymptomatic rural Ugandan women, with 25.3% testing positive. [news-cfa4cd86e5fc82c0].
  • A retrospective study found that HPV16 is associated with the highest cancer risk among women with high-grade cervical cytology, with HPV16-positive individuals showing the highest proportions of precancerous lesions and invasive squamous cell carcinoma.
  • A study found that molecular HPV testing can identify cervical abnormalities missed by Pap smears. The study is reported in the content, but no specific entity or event is confirmed. [news-975827f3854dcf5f].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Korean Journal of Family Medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-27  |  DOI: 10.4082/kjfm.26.0105  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

完成埃塞俄比亚南部青少年的 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 及其决定因素:一项收敛平行混合方法研究。

Completion of human papillomavirus vaccination and its determinants among adolescents in Southern Ethiopia: a convergent parallel mixed-methods study.
作者： Charuai Suwanbamrung, Abdulbasit Sherfa, Dereje Haile
摘要中文翻译背景:宫颈癌 是埃塞俄比亚女性中第二常见的癌症,主要归因于 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染。尽管 HPV 疫苗接种 已被引入,但推荐的两剂方案的完成情况仍然不理想。现有证据主要集中在获取和意识上,而对影响完成的认知、社会文化和母亲决策因素的关注有限。本研究采用混合方法评估了埃塞俄比亚南部青少年第二剂或完全 HPV 疫苗接种 的严重程度和决定因素。方法:采用多阶段抽样方法,对 832 名 9-14 岁青春期女孩的母亲进行了一项基于社区的横断面调查。使用半结构化问卷收集数据并使用 IBM SPSS ver. 进行分析。使用二元逻辑回归模型来识别与 疫苗 完成相关的因素。此外,我们还对 12 位特意挑选的母亲进行了深入访谈,并进行了主题分析,以探讨影响 疫苗 完成的看法。结果:完成 HPV 疫苗接种 系列的青少年比例为 26.3%(95% 置信区间,23.3-29.3)。与完整的 疫苗接种 呈正相关的因素包括母亲受过中等或以上教育、母亲共同或自主决策、之前的 宫颈癌 筛查以及对 HPV 的良好了解。相反,高信息超载和决策疲劳与完成 疫苗接种 时间表的较低几率相关。定性研究结果显示,母亲的决定受到文化信仰、对卫生工作者的信任、同伴和社交媒体的影响以及之前的医疗保健经验的影响。结论:HPV 疫苗接种 完成率较低,并且与相互作用的认知、社会文化和健康系统因素相关。干预措施不应局限于提高认识,还应解决信息负担、加强母亲决策自主权,并通过持续的咨询提高信任。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是埃塞俄比亚女性中第二常见的癌症,主要归因于 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染。现有证据主要集中在获取和意识上,而对影响完成的认知、社会文化和母亲决策因素的关注有限。
设计与对象
本研究采用混合方法评估了埃塞俄比亚南部青少年第二剂或完全 HPV 疫苗接种 的严重程度和决定因素。采用多阶段抽样方法,对 832 名 9-14 岁少女的母亲进行了一项基于社区的横断面调查。
核心方法
使用半结构化问卷收集数据并使用 IBM SPSS ver. 进行分析。使用二元逻辑回归模型来识别与 疫苗 完成相关的因素。此外,我们还对 12 位特意挑选的母亲进行了深入访谈,并进行了主题分析,以探讨影响 疫苗 完成的看法。
主要结果
完成 HPV 疫苗接种 系列的青少年比例为 26.3%(95% 置信区间,23.3-29.3)。与完整的 疫苗接种 呈正相关的因素包括母亲受过中等或以上教育、母亲共同或自主决策、之前的 宫颈癌 筛查以及对 HPV 的良好了解。相反,高信息超载和决策疲劳与完成 疫苗接种 时间表的较低几率相关。
解释与创新
HPV 疫苗接种 完成率较低,并且与相互作用的认知、社会文化和卫生系统因素相关。干预措施不应局限于提高认识,还应解决信息负担、加强母亲决策自主权,并通过持续的咨询提高信任。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施,以提高埃塞俄比亚南部青少年的 HPV 疫苗接种 完成率,重点关注孕产妇决策和卫生系统支持。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。由于依赖自我报告的数据和潜在的回忆偏差,证据的强度中等。

Completion of human papillomavirus vaccination and its determinants among adolescents in Southern Ethiopia: a convergent parallel mixed-methods study.

Authors: Charuai Suwanbamrung, Abdulbasit Sherfa, Dereje Haile
Original abstract (English)
Background: Cervical cancer is the second most common cancer among women in Ethiopia and is primarily attributable to human papillomavirus (HPV) infection. Although HPV vaccination has been introduced, completion of the recommended two-dose schedule remains suboptimal. Existing evidence has largely focused on access and awareness, with limited attention to cognitive, sociocultural, and maternal decision-making factors influencing completion. This study assessed the magnitude and determinants of second-dose or complete HPV vaccination among adolescents in southern Ethiopia using a mixed-methods approach. Methods: A community-based cross-sectional survey was conducted among 832 mothers of adolescent girls aged 9-14 years, selected using a multistage sampling method. Data were collected using a semi-structured questionnaire and analyzed with IBM SPSS ver. A binary logistic regression model was used to identify factors associated with vaccine completion. Additionally, in-depth interviews were conducted with 12 purposively selected mothers and analyzed thematically to explore perceptions influencing vaccine completion. Results: The proportion of adolescents who completed the HPV vaccination series was 26.3% (95% confidence interval, 23.3-29.3). Factors positively associated with complete vaccination included maternal education at the secondary or higher, shared or autonomous maternal decision-making, prior cervical cancer screening and good knowledge of HPV. Conversely, high information overload and decision fatigue were associated with lower odds of completing the vaccination schedule. Qualitative findings revealed that mothers' decisions were shaped by cultural beliefs, trust in health workers, peer and social media influences, and prior healthcare experience. Conclusion: HPV vaccination completion was low and was associated with interacting cognitive, socio-cultural, and health-system factors. Interventions should move beyond awareness creation to address information burden, strengthen maternal decision-making autonomy, and improve trust through consistent counseling.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer is the second most common cancer among women in Ethiopia and is primarily attributable to human papillomavirus (HPV) infection. Existing evidence has largely focused on access and awareness, with limited attention to cognitive, sociocultural, and maternal decision-making factors influencing completion.
Design and population
This study assessed the magnitude and determinants of second-dose or complete HPV vaccination among adolescents in southern Ethiopia using a mixed-methods approach. A community-based cross-sectional survey was conducted among 832 mothers of adolescent girls aged 9-14 years, selected using a multistage sampling method.
Core methods
Data were collected using a semi-structured questionnaire and analyzed with IBM SPSS ver. A binary logistic regression model was used to identify factors associated with vaccine completion. Additionally, in-depth interviews were conducted with 12 purposively selected mothers and analyzed thematically to explore perceptions influencing vaccine completion.
Main results
The proportion of adolescents who completed the HPV vaccination series was 26.3% (95% confidence interval, 23.3-29.3). Factors positively associated with complete vaccination included maternal education at the secondary or higher, shared or autonomous maternal decision-making, prior cervical cancer screening and good knowledge of HPV. Conversely, high information overload and decision fatigue were associated with lower odds of completing the vaccination schedule.
Interpretation and novelty
HPV vaccination completion was low and was associated with interacting cognitive, socio-cultural, and health-system factors. Interventions should move beyond awareness creation to address information burden, strengthen maternal decision-making autonomy, and improve trust through consistent counseling.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted interventions to improve HPV vaccination completion rates among adolescents in southern Ethiopia, focusing on maternal decision-making and health-system support.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on self-reported data and the potential for recall bias.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

基因组 对卢旺达艾滋病毒感染者 人乳头瘤病毒 感染和持续性的全关联研究。

Genome-wide association study of human papillomavirus infection and persistence in women living with HIV in Rwanda.
作者： Naluai ÅT, Schifra Uwamungu, Maria Andersson, Bethelehem Nigussie Zewdie, Claude Mambo Muvunyi, Emile Bienvenu, Bengt Hasséus, Daniel Giglio, Åsa Torinsson Naluai
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 我们在东非进行了一项探索性 基因组 全关联研究 (GWAS),以确定与 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染的获得和持续相关的 宿主 遗传变异。方法 我们对 385 名感染 HIV 的卢旺达妇女进行了基因分型,并使用 qPCR 从生殖器拭子样本中选择了 宿主 生物标志物检测信号。我们对两年内清除 HPV 的女性(操作上定义为持续感染)与未清除的女性进行了比较。还对基因分型阵列上呈现的稀有和低频变异进行了探索性的基于基因的聚合分析。结果 在主要聚类分层 Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel 分析中,PPARGC1B 内的 rs13158830 超过了持续 HPV 感染的传统 基因组 范围显着性阈值。HPV 阳性女性中附近的 MIR378A 基因表达检测信号较高,但无法区分清除率和持续性。结论 我们确定了与持续 HPV 感染和几个其他候选基因座的探索性 宿主 遗传关联。在 Fisher 精确测试和 PC 调整的 Firth 回归中,先导 rs13158830 信号仍然很明显,但携带者数量较少,产生了很大的不确定性。这些发现需要在独立的 队列研究 中进行复制。临床试验注册 不适用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定与东非艾滋病毒感染者 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染的获得和持续相关的 宿主 基因变异。
设计与对象
在卢旺达进行了一项探索性 基因组 全关联研究 (GWAS),涉及 385 名感染艾滋病毒的卢旺达妇女,对两年内清除 HPV 的妇女与未清除 HPV 的妇女进行比较。
核心方法
使用 qPCR 从生殖器拭子样本中选择 宿主 生物标志物检测信号,对 385 名感染 HIV 的卢旺达妇女进行了基因分型。还对罕见和低频变异进行了探索性基于基因的聚合分析。
主要结果
在主要聚类分层 Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel 分析中,PPARGC1B 内的 rs13158830 超过了持续 HPV 感染的传统 基因组 范围显着性阈值。在 Fisher 精确测试和 PC 调整的 Firth 回归中,先导 rs13158830 信号仍然很明显,但携带者数量较少,产生了很大的不确定性。
解释与创新
作者发现了与持续性 HPV 感染和几个其他候选基因座的探索性 宿主 遗传关联,表明了影响该人群中 HPV 持续性的潜在遗传因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来影响 HPV 持久性的 宿主 遗传因素的研究提供信息,并可能有助于针对感染艾滋病毒的女性中的 HPV 相关疾病制定有针对性的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究的携带者数量较少,带来了很大的不确定性,研究结果需要在独立的 队列研究 中进行复制。该证据是探索性的,尚未得到验证。

Genome-wide association study of human papillomavirus infection and persistence in women living with HIV in Rwanda.

Authors: Naluai ÅT, Schifra Uwamungu, Maria Andersson, Bethelehem Nigussie Zewdie, Claude Mambo Muvunyi, Emile Bienvenu, Bengt Hasséus, Daniel Giglio, Åsa Torinsson Naluai
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background We conducted an explorative genome-wide association study (GWAS) in East Africa to identify host genetic variants associated with the acquisition and persistence of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection. Methods We genotyped 385 Rwandan women living with HIV and selected host biomarker assay signals from genital swab samples using qPCR. We compared women who cleared HPV over a two-year interval (operationally defined as persistent infection), with those who did not. Exploratory gene-based aggregation analyses of rare and low-frequency variants represented on the genotyping array were also performed. Results In the primary cluster-stratified Cochran–Mantel–Haenszel analysis, rs13158830 within PPARGC1B exceeded the conventional genome-wide significance threshold for persistent HPV infection. The nearby MIR378A gene-expression assay signal was higher in HPV-positive women but did not distinguish clearance from persistence. Conclusions We identified an exploratory host genetic association with persistent HPV infection and several additional candidate loci. The lead rs13158830 signal remained evident in Fisher exact testing and PC-adjusted Firth regression, but the small number of carriers produced substantial uncertainty. These findings require replication in independent cohorts. Clinical trial registration Not applicable.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify host genetic variants associated with the acquisition and persistence of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection in women living with HIV in East Africa.
Design and population
An explorative genome-wide association study (GWAS) was conducted in Rwanda, involving 385 Rwandan women living with HIV, comparing those who cleared HPV over a two-year interval with those who did not.
Core methods
Genotyping was performed on 385 Rwandan women living with HIV, with host biomarker assay signals selected from genital swab samples using qPCR. Exploratory gene-based aggregation analyses of rare and low-frequency variants were also conducted.
Main results
In the primary cluster-stratified Cochran–Mantel–Haenszel analysis, rs13158830 within PPARGC1B exceeded the conventional genome-wide significance threshold for persistent HPV infection. The lead rs13158830 signal remained evident in Fisher exact testing and PC-adjusted Firth regression, but the small number of carriers produced substantial uncertainty.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors identified an exploratory host genetic association with persistent HPV infection and several additional candidate loci, suggesting potential genetic factors influencing HPV persistence in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform future research on host genetic factors that influence HPV persistence and could contribute to the development of targeted interventions for HPV-related diseases in women living with HIV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's small number of carriers introduced substantial uncertainty, and the findings require replication in independent cohorts. The evidence is exploratory and not yet validated.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 实施后,美国 宫颈癌 阶段分布的人口水平变化。

Population-Level Shifts in Cervical Cancer Stage Distribution in the United States Following Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Vaccine Implementation.
作者： Eva Koshy, Christina Son
摘要中文翻译目的:评估 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的引入是否与 宫颈癌 诊断 的阶段转变相关,并检查社会人口统计学差异和不断发展的成像实践是否影响了这些观察到的趋势。方法:我们分析了 2004 年 1 月至 2022 年 12 月期间从 监测、流行病学 和最终结果 (SEER) 数据库诊断的 103,831 例 宫颈癌 病例。诊断 的分期被分类为非远处或远处疾病,并在社会人口学亚组之间进行比较,包括 诊断 的年龄组(<40 岁与≥40 岁)、种族/民族、收入和城乡状况。使用线性概率模型评估远期疾病的时间趋势,包括 诊断 年份、年龄组和年龄组逐年交互项。结果:在整个 队列研究 (N = 103,831) 中,14,622 名患者 (14.1%) 出现远端疾病。在<40岁的患者中,远处转移疾病的发生率从2004年的5.6%增加到2022年的9.2%,相当于每年增加0.18个百分点(95% CI 0.12-0.23,p < 0.001)。在≥40岁的患者中,远处转移疾病的发生率从2004年的12.6%增加到2022年的20.4%,相当于每年增加0.39个百分点(95% CI 0.34-0.44,p < 0.001)。≥40 岁患者的年增长率增加了 0.21 个百分点(95% CI 0.14-0.29,交互作用 p < 0.001)。结论:在过去的二十年中,美国的远期 宫颈癌 有所增加,≥40 岁女性的增幅明显高于 40 岁以下女性。年轻女性中相对较小的增加与 HPV 疫苗接种 在人口水平上可能的贡献一致;然而,个体疫苗接种状态不可用,并且无法建立因果关系。远程舞台演示中持续存在的种族、社会经济和地理差异强调了公平获取 疫苗接种 和筛选的持续需求。

Population-Level Shifts in Cervical Cancer Stage Distribution in the United States Following Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Vaccine Implementation.

Authors: Eva Koshy, Christina Son
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To assess whether human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine introduction is associated with shifts in stage at cervical cancer diagnosis and to examine whether sociodemographic disparities and evolving imaging practices have influenced these observed trends. METHODS: We analyzed 103,831 cervical cancer cases diagnosed between January 2004 and December 2022 from the Surveillance, Epidemiology, and End Results (SEER) database. Stage at diagnosis was classified as non-distant or distant disease and compared across sociodemographic subgroups, including age group at diagnosis (<40 vs. ≥40 years), race/ethnicity, income, and urban-rural status. Temporal trends in distant-stage disease were evaluated using a linear probability model including year of diagnosis, age group, and an age group-by-year interaction term. RESULTS: Across the entire cohort (N = 103,831), 14,622 patients (14.1%) presented with distant-stage disease. Among patients <40 years, distant disease increased from 5.6% in 2004 to 9.2% in 2022, corresponding to an annual increase of 0.18 percentage points per year (95% CI 0.12-0.23, p < 0.001). Among patients ≥40 years, distant disease increased from 12.6% in 2004 to 20.4% in 2022, corresponding to an annual increase of 0.39 percentage points per year (95% CI 0.34-0.44, p < 0.001). The annual increase was 0.21 percentage points greater among patients ≥40 years (95% CI 0.14-0.29, interaction p < 0.001). CONCLUSION: Over the past two decades, distant-stage cervical cancer increased in the United States, with a significantly steeper increase among women ≥40 years than among women <40 years. The comparatively smaller increase among younger women is consistent with a possible contribution of HPV vaccination at the population level; however, individual vaccination status was unavailable, and a causal relationship cannot be established. Persistent racial, socioeconomic, and geographic disparities in distant-stage presentation emphasize the ongoing need for equitable access to vaccination and screening.

The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations or concrete methodological limitations that would affect the confidence in the results.

基线 HPV16 状态和基因型多重性独立预测 HR-HPV 阳性女性随后进行的高级别宫颈病变,这些女性转诊进行阴道镜检查,细胞学检查呈阴性或低级别:一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究。

Baseline HPV16 status and genotype multiplicity independently predict subsequent high-grade cervical lesions in HR-HPV-positive women referred for colposcopy with negative or low-grade cytology: A prospective cohort study.
作者： Barbara Gardella, Mattia Dominoni, Martina Rita Pano, Adalberto Lovotti, Stefania Cesari, Giacomo Fiandrino, Paola Alberizzi, Arsenio Spinillo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 阳性女性患高度鳞状上皮内病变或更严重 (HSIL+) 的前瞻性风险,评估基线 HPV16 状态、基因型多样性和后续 HPV 持续性的独立和交互影响。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性 队列研究 试验将 2562 名转诊接受阴道镜检查的 HR-HPV 阳性女性分为四组:单一非 HPV16、单一 HPV16、HPV16 加其他 HR 类型或多重非 HPV16。
核心方法
使用 Cox 比例风险回归、36 个月限制平均生存时间 (RMST) 和 Fine-Gray 竞争风险模型分析 HSIL+ 风险。多变量逻辑回归评估了与后续 HPV 持续性相关的并发 HSIL+ 风险。
主要结果
Overall, 691 women (27.0%) developed HSIL+. Infection with HPV16 plus other HR types carried the highest risk (adjusted hazard ratio [aHR] 2.10, 95% CI: 1.69-2.62) and shorter median HSIL+-free survival than single non-HPV16 infection (24 vs. 54 months), corresponding to 5.1 fewer HSIL+-free-months at 36 months (p<0.001). Baseline HPV16 positivity and genotype multiplicity exerted independent, additive effects without multiplicative interaction (adjusted p=0.600). Incorporating multiplicity improved model discrimination beyond entry cytology and HPV16 status (concordance index 0.612 vs.
解释与创新
基线 HPV16 阳性和基因型多重性是 HSIL+ 的独立附加危险因素,其中 HPV16 加上其他 HR 类型标志着风险最高的亚组。基因型多样性与 HPV16 状态一起提供了独特的临床预测价值,而后续 HPV 持续性为基于风险的管理策略中的并发风险评估提供了补充、非冗余信息。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了基线 HPV16 状态和基因型多样性在预测 HSIL+ 发展中的独立和附加作用,以及后续 HPV 持续性对并发风险评估的补充价值,为基于风险的管理策略提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
The study's results remained robust in competing-risk analyses (subdistribution HR 2.03, 95% CI: 1.64-2.52) and after excluding early-treated women (aHR 2.00, 95% CI: 1.52-2.62), suggesting strong evidence despite potential limitations in generalisability and confounding factors.

Baseline HPV16 status and genotype multiplicity independently predict subsequent high-grade cervical lesions in HR-HPV-positive women referred for colposcopy with negative or low-grade cytology: A prospective cohort study.

Authors: Barbara Gardella, Mattia Dominoni, Martina Rita Pano, Adalberto Lovotti, Stefania Cesari, Giacomo Fiandrino, Paola Alberizzi, Arsenio Spinillo
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To evaluate the prospective risk of high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion or worse (HSIL+) in high-risk HPV (HR-HPV)positive women, assessing the independent and interactive effects of baseline HPV16-status, genotype multiplicity, and follow-up HPV persistence. METHODS: A prospective cohort of 2562 HR-HPV-positive women referred for colposcopy were categorized into four groups: single non-HPV16, single HPV16, HPV16 plus other HR types, or multiple non-HPV16. HSIL+ risk was analysed using Cox-proportional-hazards-regression, restricted mean survival time (RMST) at 36months, and Fine-Gray competing-risk models. Multivariable logistic regression evaluated concurrent HSIL+ risk associated with follow-up HPV persistence. RESULTS: Overall, 691 women (27.0%) developed HSIL+. Infection with HPV16 plus other HR types carried the highest risk (adjusted hazard ratio [aHR]2.10, 95%CI: 1.69-2.62) and shorter median HSIL+-free survival than single non-HPV16 infection (24 vs. 54months), corresponding to 5.1 fewer HSIL+-free-months at 36months (p<0.001). Baseline HPV16 positivity and genotype multiplicity exerted independent, additive effects without multiplicative interaction (adjusted p=0.600). Incorporating multiplicity improved model discrimination beyond entry cytology and HPV16 status (concordance index 0.612 vs. Results remained robust in competing-risk analyses (subdistribution HR2.03, 95%CI: 1.64-2.52) and after excluding early-treated women (aHR2.00, 95%CI: 1.52-2.62). Follow-up viral persistence strongly correlated with concurrent HSIL+ (aHR5.71-8.59, p<0.001), reflecting a cross-sectional risk correlation complementary to baseline prognostication. CONCLUSIONS: Baseline HPV16 positivity and genotype multiplicity are independent, additive risk factors for HSIL+, with HPV16 plus other HR types marking the highest-risk subgroup. Genotype multiplicity provides distinct clinical predictive value alongside HPV16 status, while follow-up HPV persistence offers complementary, non-redundant information for concurrent risk evaluation in risk-based management strategies.

Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the prospective risk of high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion or worse (HSIL+) in high-risk HPV (HR-HPV)-positive women, assessing the independent and interactive effects of baseline HPV16-status, genotype multiplicity, and follow-up HPV persistence.

Genotype multiplicity provides distinct clinical predictive value alongside HPV16 status, while follow-up HPV persistence offers complementary, non-redundant information for concurrent risk evaluation in risk-based management strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform risk-based management strategies by highlighting the independent and additive roles of baseline HPV16 status and genotype multiplicity in predicting HSIL+ development, and the complementary value of follow-up HPV persistence for concurrent risk evaluation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results remained robust in competing-risk analyses (subdistribution HR 2.03, 95% CI: 1.64-2.52) and after excluding early-treated women (aHR 2.00, 95% CI: 1.52-2.62), suggesting strong evidence despite potential limitations in generalisability and confounding factors.

综合基因组和免疫分析显示 PIK3CA 突变型 HPV16 阳性 宫颈癌 在印度东部患者中是一种临床上具有侵袭性且免疫学上独特的亚型

Integrated Genomic and Immune Profiling Reveals PIK3CA-Mutant HPV16-Positive Cervical Cancer as a Clinically Aggressive and Immunologically Distinct Subtype in Patients from Eastern India
作者： Abhisikta Ghosh, Abarna Sinha, Sonia Mathai, Jaydip Bhaumik, Asima Mukhopadhyay, Sreyosi Majumder, Arindam Maitra, Nidhan K. Biswas, Koushik Mondal, Partha Pratim Majumder
摘要中文翻译背景:HPV16 驱动的 宫颈癌 (CaCx) 是印度女性中癌症相关 死亡率 的主要原因。识别人群特异性基因组和免疫决定因素可能会改善治疗策略。方法:我们对 49 例 HPV16 阳性宫颈肿瘤和匹配的血液样本进行了全外显子组测序。整合了 49 个肿瘤中 29 个肿瘤的可用 RNA-seq 数据,以识别复发性 突变、改变的通路和免疫微环境特征。结果:我们鉴定了 6,462 个体细胞 突变 富含癌症相关通路,特别是 PI3K-Akt 信号传导。PIK3CA 是最常发生突变的基因 (42%),其频率显着高于 HPV16 阳性 TCGA-CESC 肿瘤 (26.21%) (p < 0.04)。与 TCGA-CESC 中 PIK3CA 突变 与早期疾病相关的情况不同,印度 PIK3CA 突变肿瘤在晚期疾病中丰富。与 PIK3CA 野生肿瘤相比,这些肿瘤表现出较低的病毒载量、较高的肿瘤 突变 负荷和更具侵袭性的分子表型。基于 CIBERSORT 的免疫分析显示,PIK3CA 突变肿瘤和正常组织之间的免疫细胞浸润(包括 CD8+ T 细胞)相当,而 PIK3CA 野生肿瘤与正常组织相比,浸润显着增强。两种肿瘤亚型都比正常组织具有更高水平的成熟三级淋巴结构。与正常组织相比,两种亚型中的 CD274 (PD-L1) 表达均显着升高,而 PDCD1 (PD-1) 上调和 PD-1/PD-L1 转录偶联显着水平仅发生在 PIK3CA 突变肿瘤中。CCL3-CCR1 轴在 PIK3CA 突变肿瘤中被选择性激活,并与细胞毒性、干扰素反应、骨髓和免疫检查点标记物相关,表明存在免疫抑制微环境。结论:PIK3CA 突变 定义了印度患者中具有分子侵袭性、免疫学独特的 HPV16 阳性 CaCx 亚型,强调了其进行靶向治疗干预的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV16 驱动的 宫颈癌 是印度女性中癌症相关 死亡率 的主要原因。识别人群特异性基因组和免疫决定因素可能会改善治疗策略。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 49 个 HPV16 阳性宫颈肿瘤和匹配的血液样本的全外显子组测序。整合 29 个肿瘤的 RNA-seq 数据来分析 突变、通路和免疫特征。
核心方法
对 49 例 HPV16 阳性宫颈肿瘤和匹配的血液样本进行了全外显子组测序。整合了 29 个肿瘤的 RNA-seq 数据,以识别复发性 突变、改变的通路和免疫微环境特征。
主要结果
该研究鉴定出 6,462 个体细胞 突变 富含癌症相关通路,特别是 PI3K-Akt 信号传导。PIK3CA 是最常发生突变的基因 (42%),其频率显着高于 HPV16 阳性 TCGA-CESC 肿瘤 (26.21%) (p < 0.04)。与 TCGA-CESC 不同,印度 PIK3CA 突变肿瘤富含晚期疾病。与 PIK3CA 野生肿瘤相比,这些肿瘤表现出较低的病毒载量、较高的肿瘤 突变 负荷和更具侵袭性的分子表型。基于 CIBERSORT 的免疫分析显示,PIK3CA 突变肿瘤和正常组织之间的免疫细胞浸润(包括 CD8+ T 细胞)相当,而 PIK3CA 野生肿瘤与正常组织相比,浸润显着增强。
解释与创新
作者解释说,PIK3CA 突变 定义了印度患者中 HPV16 阳性 宫颈癌 的分子侵袭性、免疫学独特亚型,强调了它们进行靶向治疗干预的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为印度人群中 HPV16 阳性 宫颈癌 的靶向治疗策略的开发提供信息,特别是针对具有不同免疫学特征的 PIK3CA 突变亚型。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对特定人群的关注和使用单一参考数据集(TCGA-CESC)而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于有限样本量的基因组和免疫分析,可能无法推广到其他人群。

Integrated Genomic and Immune Profiling Reveals PIK3CA-Mutant HPV16-Positive Cervical Cancer as a Clinically Aggressive and Immunologically Distinct Subtype in Patients from Eastern India

Authors: Abhisikta Ghosh, Abarna Sinha, Sonia Mathai, Jaydip Bhaumik, Asima Mukhopadhyay, Sreyosi Majumder, Arindam Maitra, Nidhan K. Biswas, Koushik Mondal, Partha Pratim Majumder
Original abstract (English)
Background: HPV16-driven cervical cancer (CaCx) is a leading cause of cancer-related mortality among Indian women. Identifying population-specific genomic and immune determinants may improve therapeutic strategies. Methods: We performed whole-exome sequencing on 49 HPV16-positive cervical tumors and matched blood samples. Available RNA-seq data from 29 out of 49 tumors were integrated, to identify recurrent mutations, altered pathways, and immune microenvironment features. Results: We identified 6,462 somatic mutations enriched in cancer-related pathways, particularly PI3K-Akt signaling. PIK3CA was the most recurrently mutated gene (42%), significantly higher frequency (p < 0.04) than in HPV16-positive TCGA-CESC tumors (26.21%). Unlike TCGA-CESC, where PIK3CA mutations were associated with early-stage disease, Indian PIK3CA-mutant tumors were enriched in advanced-stage disease. These tumors exhibited lower viral loads, higher tumor mutation burden, and more aggressive molecular phenotypes than PIK3CA-wild tumors. CIBERSORT-based immune profiling showed comparable immune-cell infiltration, including CD8+ T cells, between PIK3CA-mutant tumors and normal tissues, whereas PIK3CA-wild tumors showed significantly enhanced infiltration, compared to normal tissues. Both tumor subtypes portrayed mature tertiary lymphoid structures at higher levels than normal tissues. Compared to normal tissues, CD274 (PD-L1) expression was elevated significantly in both subtypes, while PDCD1 (PD-1) upregulation and PD-1/PD-L1 transcriptional coupling at significant levels, occurred exclusively in PIK3CA-mutant tumors. The CCL3-CCR1 axis was selectively activated in PIK3CA-mutant tumors and correlated with cytotoxic, interferon-response, myeloid, and immune checkpoint markers, indicating an immunosuppressive microenvironment. Conclusion: PIK3CA mutations define a molecularly aggressive, immunologically distinct subtype of HPV16-positive CaCx in Indian patients, underscoring their potential for targeted therapeutic interventions.

These tumors exhibited lower viral loads, higher tumor mutation burden, and more aggressive molecular phenotypes than PIK3CA-wild tumors. CIBERSORT-based immune profiling showed comparable immune-cell infiltration, including CD8+ T cells, between PIK3CA-mutant tumors and normal tissues, whereas PIK3CA-wild tumors showed significantly enhanced infiltration, compared to normal tissues.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that PIK3CA mutations define a molecularly aggressive, immunologically distinct subtype of HPV16-positive cervical cancer in Indian patients, highlighting their potential for targeted therapeutic interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of targeted therapeutic strategies for HPV16-positive cervical cancer in Indian populations, particularly for PIK3CA-mutant subtypes with distinct immunological profiles.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a specific population and the use of a single reference dataset (TCGA-CESC). The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on genomic and immune profiling of a limited sample size and may not be generalizable to other populations.

高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 的首次无效尿液检测:三种分析前方法的 队列研究 内比较。

First-void urine testing for high-risk human papillomavirus: A within-cohort comparison of three preanalytical approaches.
作者： Sümeyra Ebru Menekşe Yurt, Parkan ÖM, Seda Şahin, Yüksek Ş, Ertuğrul Şen, Selma Gökahmetoğlu, Ömür Mustafa Parkan, Şahin Yüksek, Ertugrul Sen
摘要中文翻译目的:比较基于首次排尿 (FVU) 的高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) DNA 检测与配对宫颈拭子 (CS) 样本的三种分析前方法。方法:这项横断面观察性研究包括 69 名先前 hrHPV 筛查结果呈阳性的参与者以及配对的 CS 和 FVU 样本。从每个 FVU 样本中,生成三种尿液制剂:未保存的尿液、用于 DNA 稳定的 EDTA 处理尿液、用尿液保存介质缓冲的尿液(UCM;Novosanis,Wijnegem,比利时)。所有样本均使用 QIAscreen HPV PCR 检测(Qiagen,希尔登,德国)进行分析。评估了配对 CS 样本的一致性、基因型特异性一致性、配对 CS 周期阈值 (Ct) 值以及参与者报告的尿液采样感知。结果:在所有三种方法中,尿液和 CS 样本之间的总体一致性中等,未保存尿液的 Cohen κ 值为 0.566,EDTA 处理尿液为 0.538,UCM 缓冲尿液为 0.581。hrHPV DNA 阳性尿液样本的配对 CS Ct 值低于 hrHPV DNA 阴性尿液样本的参与者,这与宫颈 hrHPV DNA 水平较高时尿液可检出率较高一致。HPV16 和 HPV18 的基因型特异性一致性比合并的非 16/18 hrHPV 更强。参与者报告的数据表明尿液采样的可接受性良好。结论:这三种方法显示出彼此大致相似的结果,而所有方法与配对 CS 样本的一致性中等。这些发现支持进一步评估基于尿液的 hrHPV 检测作为一种非侵入性采样选择,有助于扩大检测范围,特别是在低收入和其他资源有限的环境中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在比较基于首次排尿 (FVU) 的高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) DNA 检测与配对宫颈拭子 (CS) 样本的三种分析前方法,解决对非侵入性替代方案的需求,以改善 hrHPV 筛查的机会。
设计与对象
一项横断面观察性研究纳入了 69 名先前 hrHPV 筛查结果呈阳性的参与者以及配对的 CS 和 FVU 样本,旨在评估基于尿液的 hrHPV 检测方法的有效性。
核心方法
从每个 FVU 样本中生成三种尿液制剂:未保存的尿液、用于 DNA 稳定的 EDTA 处理尿液以及用尿液保存介质(UCM;Novosanis,Wijnegem,Belgium)缓冲的尿液。所有样本均使用 QIAscreen HPV PCR 检测(Qiagen,希尔登,德国)进行分析。
主要结果
在所有三种方法中,尿液和 CS 样本之间的总体一致性中等,未保存尿液的 Cohen κ 值为 0.566,EDTA 处理尿液为 0.538,UCM 缓冲尿液为 0.581。hrHPV DNA 阳性尿液样本的参与者的配对 CS 周期阈值 (Ct) 低于 hrHPV DNA 阴性尿液样本的参与者。
解释与创新
研究发现,三种分析前方法显示出大致相似的结果,而与配对 CS 样本的一致性中等,表明基于尿液的 hrHPV 检测可能是宫颈拭子的可行替代方案。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持进一步评估基于尿液的 hrHPV 检测作为一种非侵入性采样选择,有助于扩大检测范围,特别是在低收入和其他资源有限的环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于适度的样本量和观察设计,这可能会限制研究结果的普遍性。由于缺乏随机对照且参与者报告的数据可能存在偏差,证据强度中等。

First-void urine testing for high-risk human papillomavirus: A within-cohort comparison of three preanalytical approaches.

Authors: Sümeyra Ebru Menekşe Yurt, Parkan ÖM, Seda Şahin, Yüksek Ş, Ertuğrul Şen, Selma Gökahmetoğlu, Ömür Mustafa Parkan, Şahin Yüksek, Ertugrul Sen
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: To compare three preanalytical approaches for first-void urine (FVU)-based high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) DNA detection against paired cervical swab (CS) specimens. METHODS: This cross-sectional observational study included 69 participants with a previous positive hrHPV screening result and paired CS and FVU specimens. From each FVU specimen, three urine preparations were generated: unpreserved urine, EDTA-treated urine for DNA stabilization, urine buffered with urine conservation medium (UCM; Novosanis, Wijnegem, Belgium). All specimens were analyzed using the QIAscreen HPV PCR Test (Qiagen, Hilden, Germany). Agreement with paired CS specimens, genotype-specific agreement, paired CS cycle threshold (Ct) values, and participant-reported perceptions of urine sampling were assessed. RESULTS: Overall agreement between urine and CS specimens was moderate across all three approaches, with Cohen's κ values of 0.566 for unpreserved urine, 0.538 for EDTA-treated urine, and 0.581 for UCM-buffered urine. Paired CS Ct values were lower in participants with hrHPV DNA-positive urine specimens than in those with hrHPV DNA-negative urine specimens, consistent with greater urinary detectability at higher cervical hrHPV DNA levels. Genotype-specific agreement was stronger for HPV16 and HPV18 than for pooled non-16/18 hrHPV. Participant-reported data suggested good acceptability of urine sampling. CONCLUSIONS: The three approaches showed broadly similar results to one another, while agreement with paired CS specimens was moderate across all approaches. These findings support further evaluation of urine-based hrHPV testing as a non-invasive sampling option that could help expand access to testing, particularly in low-income and other resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to compare three preanalytical approaches for first-void urine (FVU)-based high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) DNA detection against paired cervical swab (CS) specimens, addressing the need for non-invasive alternatives to improve access to hrHPV screening.
Design and population
A cross-sectional observational study included 69 participants with a previous positive hrHPV screening result and paired CS and FVU specimens, conducted to evaluate the effectiveness of urine-based hrHPV testing methods.
Core methods
Three urine preparations were generated from each FVU specimen: unpreserved urine, EDTA-treated urine for DNA stabilization, and urine buffered with urine conservation medium (UCM; Novosanis, Wijnegem, Belgium). All specimens were analyzed using the QIAscreen HPV PCR Test (Qiagen, Hilden, Germany).
Main results
Overall agreement between urine and CS specimens was moderate across all three approaches, with Cohen's κ values of 0.566 for unpreserved urine, 0.538 for EDTA-treated urine, and 0.581 for UCM-buffered urine. Paired CS cycle threshold (Ct) values were lower in participants with hrHPV DNA-positive urine specimens than in those with hrHPV DNA-negative urine specimens.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that the three preanalytical approaches showed broadly similar results, while agreement with paired CS specimens was moderate, suggesting that urine-based hrHPV testing could be a viable alternative to cervical swabs.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the further evaluation of urine-based hrHPV testing as a non-invasive sampling option that could help expand access to testing, particularly in low-income and other resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on a moderate sample size and observational design, which may limit the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of randomized control and potential for bias in participant-reported data.

与爱荷华州青少年 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 覆盖率相关的多层次因素:来自全州免疫登记处的证据。

Multilevel factors associated with human papillomavirus vaccination coverage in Iowa youth: Evidence from a statewide immunization registry.
作者： Allison Hill, Margaret Carrel, Milkie Vu, Whitney E Zahnd, Emily Janio, Heather M Brandt, Jennifer Sukalski, Amanda R Kahl, Zenawi Hagos Gufue, Natoshia M Askelson
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 对于预防宫颈癌、外阴癌、阴道癌、肛门癌、直肠癌和口咽癌至关重要。这项研究调查了爱荷华州青少年中与 HPV 疫苗接种 相关的个人层面和地区层面的因素。
设计与对象
我们分析了爱荷华州免疫登记信息系统中 9-17 岁青少年的数据,并与邮政编码制表区的社会经济和人口统计指标相关联。该研究主要针对爱荷华州青少年,并检查了与 HPV 疫苗接种 相关的因素。
核心方法
分层贝叶斯空间模型估计了 HPV 疫苗接种 在任何年龄的启动、完成和按时完成。该研究使用了爱荷华州免疫登记信息系统的数据,并将其与邮政编码制表区的社会经济和人口统计指标联系起来。
主要结果
Receipt of school-required and other routinely recommended vaccines was strongly associated with HPV vaccination. Compared with youth who received both vaccine types, those who received only school-required vaccines or only other routinely recommended vaccines had lower HPV vaccination odds (ORs = 0.01-0.42, 95% CrIs = [0.01, 0.43]).
解释与创新
个体水平和区域水平特征与 HPV 疫苗接种 相关。在解决地理差异的同时促进预防性护理和常规青少年 疫苗接种 的干预措施可能会提高 疫苗 的覆盖率并降低 HPV 相关癌症风险。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,解决地理差异和促进预防保健可以提高 HPV 疫苗接种 的覆盖率。这可能有助于降低爱荷华州青少年患 HPV 相关癌症的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对登记数据的依赖和潜在的地理差异而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为它基于观察数据并且没有建立因果关系。

Multilevel factors associated with human papillomavirus vaccination coverage in Iowa youth: Evidence from a statewide immunization registry.

Authors: Allison Hill, Margaret Carrel, Milkie Vu, Whitney E Zahnd, Emily Janio, Heather M Brandt, Jennifer Sukalski, Amanda R Kahl, Zenawi Hagos Gufue, Natoshia M Askelson
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination at the earliest opportunity is critical for preventing cervical, vulvar, vaginal, anal, rectal, and oropharyngeal cancers. This study examined individual-level and area-level factors associated with HPV vaccination among Iowa youth. METHODS: We analyzed Iowa Immunization Registry Information System data for youth aged 9-17 years, linked to ZIP Code Tabulation Area socioeconomic and demographic measures. Hierarchical Bayesian spatial models estimated HPV vaccination initiation, completion at any age, and on-time completion. RESULTS: Receipt of school-required and other routinely recommended vaccines was strongly associated with HPV vaccination. Compared with youth who received both vaccine types, those who received only school-required vaccines or only other routinely recommended vaccines had lower HPV vaccination odds (ORs = 0.01-0.42, 95% CrIs = [0.01, 0.43]). Female youth, insured youth, and those of Hispanic or Latino ethnicity had higher vaccination odds. Youth in rural, small town, and micropolitan areas had lower odds than those in metropolitan areas (ORs = 0.89-0.93, 95% CrIs = [0.85, 0.97]). Area-level education, housing, and local HPV vaccination rates were associated with vaccination. CONCLUSIONS: Individual-level and area-level characteristics were associated with HPV vaccination. Interventions promoting preventive care and routine youth vaccination while addressing geographic disparities may improve vaccine coverage and reduce HPV-related cancer risk.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination at the earliest opportunity is critical for preventing cervical, vulvar, vaginal, anal, rectal, and oropharyngeal cancers. This study examined individual-level and area-level factors associated with HPV vaccination among Iowa youth.
Design and population
We analyzed Iowa Immunization Registry Information System data for youth aged 9-17 years, linked to ZIP Code Tabulation Area socioeconomic and demographic measures. The study focused on Iowa youth and examined factors associated with HPV vaccination.
Core methods
Hierarchical Bayesian spatial models estimated HPV vaccination initiation, completion at any age, and on-time completion. The study used data from the Iowa Immunization Registry Information System and linked it to ZIP Code Tabulation Area socioeconomic and demographic measures.
Main results
Receipt of school-required and other routinely recommended vaccines was strongly associated with HPV vaccination. Compared with youth who received both vaccine types, those who received only school-required vaccines or only other routinely recommended vaccines had lower HPV vaccination odds (ORs = 0.01-0.42, 95% CrIs = [0.01, 0.43]).
Interpretation and novelty
Individual-level and area-level characteristics were associated with HPV vaccination. Interventions promoting preventive care and routine youth vaccination while addressing geographic disparities may improve vaccine coverage and reduce HPV-related cancer risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that addressing geographic disparities and promoting preventive care could improve HPV vaccination coverage. This may help reduce the risk of HPV-related cancers in Iowa youth.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on registry data and potential geographic disparities. The evidence is moderate as it is based on observational data and does not establish causality.

持续的 HPV16 E7 表达建立了一种预处理的上皮状态,并揭示了女性口腔癌发生过程中转录反应的放大

Persistent HPV16 E7 Expression Establishes a Preconditioned Epithelial State and Reveals Amplified Transcriptional Responses in Females During Oral Carcinogenesis
作者： Kalani Hettiarachchi, Minwoo Kim, RLPR Liyanage, Avi-Hai Hovav, Arutha Kulasinghe, Ian Frazer, Kelvin Tuong, Debottam Sinha, Janin Chandra
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关且致癌物驱动的头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 通过不同的生物学途径产生,但每种病因如何塑造癌发生开始的组织状态仍不清楚。我们研究了 HPV16 E7 癌蛋白表达和生物性别如何影响致癌物暴露之前和期间的口腔上皮反应。使用流式细胞术、组织病理学、免疫组织化学和 RNA 测序,对 4-硝基喹啉-1-氧化物 (4NQO) 诱导口腔癌发生前后的雄性和雌性野生型 (WT) 和表达 HPV16 E7 的转基因 (K14E7) 小鼠的口腔上皮进行分析,并将转录程序与人 HPV + 和 HPV - HNSCC 进行比较。持续的 HPV16 E7 表达建立了上皮增生和细胞周期、MYC、干扰素反应和炎症程序丰富的基线转录状态,并伴随着 T 细胞和骨髓细胞的选择性重塑。HPV16 E7 驱动的转录重塑在女性中更为明显,尽管通路富集在性别之间是保守的。4NQO暴露后,WT上皮经历了转录重塑,重演了未经处理的K14E7上皮中已经活跃的程序,而K14E7上皮显示出有限的额外转录变化,尽管病理终点相似。跨物种分析显示,小鼠 WT 和 K14E7 4NQO 模型与人类 HPV - 和 HPV + HNSCC 之间存在一致性,其中性别相关途径显示出显着(尽管较为温和)的跨物种一致性。总之,这些发现表明 HPV16 E7 建立了一种预条件增殖和炎症上皮状态,并具有早期免疫重塑,从而改变了对致癌损伤的反应。HPV16 E7可能会在致癌过程中重定向而不是加速上皮重塑,女性对癌基因表达和致癌物暴露的转录反应作为上皮适应的一个特征而出现。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 HPV16 E7 癌蛋白表达和生物性别如何影响致癌物暴露之前和期间的口腔上皮反应。背景强调,HPV 相关且致癌物驱动的头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 通过不同的生物学途径产生,但每种病因形成的组织状态仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究使用雄性和雌性野生型 (WT) 和表达 HPV16 E7 的转基因 (K14E7) 小鼠的口腔上皮,对 4-硝基喹啉-1-氧化物 (4NQO) 诱导口腔癌发生前后的口腔上皮进行分析。该人群包括两种性别和两种遗传模型,并与人类 HPV + 和 HPV - HNSCC 进行比较。
核心方法
这些方法涉及流式细胞术、组织病理学、免疫组织化学和 RNA 测序来分析小鼠的口腔上皮。将转录程序与人类 HPV + 和 HPV - HNSCC 进行比较,以评估生物反应的差异。
主要结果
持续的 HPV16 E7 表达建立了上皮增生和丰富细胞周期、MYC、干扰素反应和炎症程序的基线转录状态。这些变化伴随着 T 细胞和骨髓细胞的选择性重塑。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HPV16 E7 通过早期免疫重塑建立了一种预处理的增殖和炎症上皮状态,改变了对致癌损伤的反应。这表明HPV16 E7可能在癌变过程中重定向而不是加速上皮重塑。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了有关 HPV16 E7 影响口腔癌发生进展的分子机制的见解,这可以为未来有关 HPV 相关癌症的研究和靶向治疗的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠模型,并不能直接转化为人类结果。然而,跨物种分析显示小鼠模型与人类 HPV - 和 HPV + HNSCC 之间的一致性,支持对研究结果的中等可信度。

Persistent HPV16 E7 Expression Establishes a Preconditioned Epithelial State and Reveals Amplified Transcriptional Responses in Females During Oral Carcinogenesis

Authors: Kalani Hettiarachchi, Minwoo Kim, RLPR Liyanage, Avi-Hai Hovav, Arutha Kulasinghe, Ian Frazer, Kelvin Tuong, Debottam Sinha, Janin Chandra
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated and carcinogen-driven head and neck squamous cell carcinomas (HNSCC) arise through distinct biological routes, yet how each aetiology shapes the tissue state in which carcinogenesis begins remains unclear. We investigated how HPV16 E7 oncoprotein expression and biological sex influence the oral epithelial response before and during carcinogen exposure. Oral epithelium from male and female wild-type (WT) and HPV16 E7-expressing transgenic (K14E7) mice was analysed before and following 4-nitroquinoline-1-oxide (4NQO) induced oral carcinogenesis using flow cytometry, histopathology, immunohistochemistry, and RNA sequencing, with transcriptional programmes compared with human HPV + and HPV − HNSCC. Persistent HPV16 E7 expression established epithelial hyperplasia and a baseline transcriptional state enriched for cell-cycle, MYC, interferon response and inflammatory programmes, accompanied by selective remodelling of T cell and myeloid cells. HPV16 E7-driven transcriptional remodelling was more pronounced in females, although pathway enrichment was conserved between sexes. Following 4NQO exposure, WT epithelium underwent transcriptional remodelling that recapitulated programmes already active in untreated K14E7 epithelium, whereas K14E7 epithelium showed limited additional transcriptional change despite similar pathological endpoints. Cross-species analyses showed concordance between the murine WT and K14E7 4NQO model and human HPV − and HPV + HNSCC, with sex-associated pathways demonstrating significant, albeit more modest, cross-species concordance. Together, these findings indicate that HPV16 E7 establishes a preconditioned proliferative and inflammatory epithelial state with early immune remodelling that alters responses to carcinogenic injury. HPV16 E7 may redirect rather than accelerate epithelial remodelling during carcinogenesis, with female transcriptional responsiveness to both oncogene expression and carcinogen exposure emerging as a feature of epithelial adaptation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how HPV16 E7 oncoprotein expression and biological sex influence the oral epithelial response before and during carcinogen exposure. The background highlights that HPV-associated and carcinogen-driven head and neck squamous cell carcinomas (HNSCC) arise through distinct biological routes, yet the tissue state shaped by each aetiology remains unclear.
Design and population
The study used oral epithelium from male and female wild-type (WT) and HPV16 E7-expressing transgenic (K14E7) mice, analysing them before and following 4-nitroquinoline-1-oxide (4NQO) induced oral carcinogenesis. The population included both sexes and two genetic models, with comparisons made to human HPV + and HPV − HNSCC.
Core methods
The methods involved flow cytometry, histopathology, immunohistochemistry, and RNA sequencing to analyse oral epithelium from mice. Transcriptional programmes were compared with human HPV + and HPV − HNSCC to assess differences in biological responses.
Main results
Persistent HPV16 E7 expression established epithelial hyperplasia and a baseline transcriptional state enriched for cell-cycle, MYC, interferon response and inflammatory programmes. These changes were accompanied by selective remodelling of T cell and myeloid cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HPV16 E7 establishes a preconditioned proliferative and inflammatory epithelial state with early immune remodelling, altering responses to carcinogenic injury. This indicates that HPV16 E7 may redirect rather than accelerate epithelial remodelling during carcinogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the molecular mechanisms by which HPV16 E7 influences the progression of oral carcinogenesis, which could inform future research on HPV-associated cancers and the development of targeted therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to mouse models and does not directly translate to human outcomes. However, cross-species analyses showed concordance between the murine model and human HPV − and HPV + HNSCC, supporting a moderate confidence in the findings.

人乳头瘤病毒 L1 衣壳蛋白的组装途径以静电相互作用编码。

The assembly pathway for human papillomavirus L1 capsid protein is encoded in electrostatic interactions.
作者： Yuan X, K N Nand, J C Patel, Jin A, Bystroff C
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 L1 蛋白自发折叠并组装成 60 nm 病毒样颗粒 (VLP),在高盐浓度下具有 T = 7d 二十面体对称性,但在低盐浓度下则不然。在组装过程中从低盐到高盐的快速增加会产生质量较差的 VLP,而在长达 48 小时内缓慢增加盐会产生高质量的 VLP。对这种行为的深入了解是通过基于 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 VLP 低温电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 结构的计算模型得出的。使用线性化泊松-玻尔兹曼方程计算静电势能垒。在单体最初快速寡聚成对称五聚体(壳粒)后,这些单元以能量顺序形成成对相互作用,最低势垒首先。但在高盐浓度下,无论是在模拟还是在实验中,能量势垒或多或少都是相等的,并且粒子形式不规则。相互作用界面由 突变 修改,以支持更小的 30 nm T = 1 二十面体对称性,而不支持 T = 7d 对称性。结果是更小的颗粒。通过仔细控制离子强度和/或通过设计突变界面残基,可以直接从未纯化的细菌裂解物中制备高质量、高免疫原性的VLP,而不需要使用拆卸/重新组装步骤。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 人乳头瘤病毒 L1 衣壳蛋白的组装途径以及盐浓度如何影响病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 的形成。这项研究的动机是需要提高 疫苗 开发的 VLP 质量。
设计与对象
该研究使用基于 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 VLP 低温电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 结构的计算模型来研究组装途径。该模型模拟了不同盐浓度对 VLP 形成的影响。
核心方法
使用计算方法对组装过程进行建模,并使用线性化泊松-玻尔兹曼方程计算静电势能垒。引入 突变 来修改交互界面并测试其对 VLP 对称性的影响。
主要结果
结果表明,盐浓度的快速增加会导致VLP质量较差,而在48小时内缓慢增加盐浓度会产生高质量的VLP。发现 突变 有利于较小的 30 nm T = 1 二十面体对称性,而不利于 T = 7d 对称性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,组装途径受静电相互作用控制,控制离子强度或突变特定残基可以导致直接从细菌裂解物中形成高质量、免疫原性的 VLP。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对 HPV 疫苗 的 VLP 生产具有影响,因为它们表明,仔细控制盐浓度和靶向 突变 可以提高 VLP 的质量,而无需拆卸和重新组装步骤。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于计算模型和实验验证,在充分捕捉体内组装过程的复杂性方面可能存在局限性。由于依赖模拟且缺乏直接的临床结果,证据是中等的。

The assembly pathway for human papillomavirus L1 capsid protein is encoded in electrostatic interactions.

Authors: Yuan X, K N Nand, J C Patel, Jin A, Bystroff C
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus L1 protein spontaneously folds and assembles into 60 nm virus-like particles (VLP), with T = 7d icosahedral symmetry at high salt concentrations but not in low salt. Rapid increase from low to high salt during the assembly process gives poor-quality VLPs, while slowly increasing salt, over as many as 48 h, gives rise to high-quality VLPs. Insight into this behavior is drawn from a computational model based on the cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM) structure of the human papillomavirus Type 16 VLP. Electrostatic potential energy barriers were calculated using the linearized Poisson-Boltzmann equation. After initial, rapid oligomerization of monomers into symmetrical pentamers (capsomeres), these units form pairwise interactions in energetic order, lowest barrier first. But at high salt concentrations, the energetic barriers are more or less equal and irregular particles form, both in simulation and in experiments. The interaction interfaces were modified by mutation to favor the smaller, 30 nm T = 1 icosahedral symmetry and disfavor T = 7d symmetry. The results were smaller particles. By carefully controlling ionic strength, and/or by mutating interface residues by design, it is possible to make high quality, highly immunogenic VLPs directly from unpurified bacterial lysate, without requiring the use of a disassembly/reassembly step.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the assembly pathway of human papillomavirus L1 capsid protein and how salt concentration affects the formation of virus-like particles (VLPs). The research was motivated by the need to improve the quality of VLPs for vaccine development.
Design and population
The study used a computational model based on the cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM) structure of the human papillomavirus Type 16 VLP to investigate the assembly pathway. The model simulated the effects of varying salt concentrations on the formation of VLPs.
Core methods
The assembly process was modeled using computational methods, and electrostatic potential energy barriers were calculated using the linearized Poisson-Boltzmann equation. Mutations were introduced to modify interaction interfaces and test their impact on VLP symmetry.
Main results
The results showed that rapid increases in salt concentration led to poor-quality VLPs, while slow increases over 48 hours produced high-quality VLPs. Mutations were found to favor the smaller, 30 nm T = 1 icosahedral symmetry and disfavor T = 7d symmetry.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the assembly pathway is governed by electrostatic interactions and that controlling ionic strength or mutating specific residues can lead to the formation of high-quality, immunogenic VLPs directly from bacterial lysate.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have implications for the production of VLPs for HPV vaccines, as they suggest that careful control of salt concentration and targeted mutations can improve the quality of VLPs without the need for disassembly and reassembly steps.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on computational modeling and experimental validation, which may have limitations in fully capturing the complexity of in vivo assembly processes. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on simulations and the absence of direct clinical outcomes.

Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the feasibility of a Japanese-language AI agent system for HPV vaccine infoveillance and public communication, addressing the need for accurate and culturally relevant health information dissemination.
Design and population
The study involved the development and feasibility evaluation of an AI agent system for HPV vaccine-related infoveillance, with data collected from chat conversations and social media posts in Japanese and English, involving 139,803 unique documents.
Core methods
The system used a large language model (LLM) for stance classification, with three raters evaluating interrater reliability using metrics such as MAD, Spearman ρ, and ICC. The evaluation included single-turn and multiturn conversations, with results based on statistical analysis of scores across different dimensions.
Main results
The study corrected several errors, including the number of conversations used for multiturn evaluation (from 11 to 8), the language distribution of chat documents (from 59.3% Japanese to 1.4% Japanese), and inconsistencies in the “Score of ≤3, %” column of Table 5. The ICC for context memory was 1.000, indicating perfect interrater reliability, while topic centering and identity showed lower reliability.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the feasibility of the AI agent system for HPV vaccine infoveillance, with the correction of errors enhancing the accuracy of reported results and reliability metrics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of AI tools for HPV vaccine communication and public health surveillance, highlighting the importance of accurate data representation and reliability in health-related infoveillance systems.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results were based on corrected data, which may affect the interpretation of reliability metrics. The evidence is moderate due to the focus on feasibility and the limited scope of the corrections.

科研与公卫意义
该研究深入探讨了 miR-129 作为 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 的诊断和预后生物标志物的潜在作用。它还强调了 BMSC 和 宫颈癌 细胞之间的相互作用,这可以为未来肿瘤微环境和治疗策略的研究提供信息。

Study on the molecular mechanism of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells inducing epithelial-mesenchymal transition in HPV-associated cervical cancer through activation of miR-129 .

Authors: Nieying Ma, Kaijie Xu, Zhize Wang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: HPV-associated cervical cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related death in women worldwide. The role of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells (BMSCs) and miR-129 in tumor progression remains unclear. OBJECTIVES: To investigate miR-129 expression and its clinical significance in HPV-associated cervical cancer and to explore whether BMSCs regulate malignant phenotypes through miR-129. METHODS: miR-129 expression was measured by qRT-PCR in 77 cervical cancer and 30 normal cervical tissues. Associations with clinicopathological features and overall survival (OS) were analyzed. Diagnostic value was assessed by ROC curve. Rat BMSCs were co-cultured with SiHa and HeLa cells. Following miR-129 overexpression or knockdown, cell proliferation (CCK-8, colony formation), invasion (Transwell) and expression of E-cadherin, N-cadherin and MRP1 (Western blot) were examined. RESULTS: miR-129 expression was significantly higher in cervical cancer tissues than in normal controls (3.24±0.56 vs. High miR-129 expression correlated with tumor size ≥3 cm (P=0.009), FIGO stage III-IV (P=0.0034) and lymph node metastasis (P=0.038). High expression was associated with shorter OS (median: 28.4 vs. 46.2 months; HR=2.944, 95%CI:1.484-5.842, P=0.002). The AUC was 0.788 (95%CI:0.693-0.884), with a cut-off of 1.85 (sensitivity:76.9%, specificity:70.4%). BMSC co-culture upregulated miR-129 expression (SiHa: 3.52±0.42 vs. miR-129 overexpression promoted proliferation, colony formation and invasion, while knockdown suppressed these phenotypes. Overexpression of miR-129 reduced E-cadherin (0.42±0.05 vs. 1.00±0.08, P<0.01) and increased N-cadherin (2.85±0.22 vs. 1.00±0.10, P<0.01) and MRP1 (2.46±0.18 vs. Knockdown of miR-129 partially reversed BMSC-induced changes in these proteins. CONCLUSION: miR-129 is significantly upregulated in HPV-associated cervical cancer and shows potential as a diagnostic and prognostic biomarker. BMSCs upregulate miR-129 in cervical cancer cells and miR-129 modulates proliferation, invasion, EMT marker expression and MRP1 expression. These findings suggest that the BMSCs/miR-129 axis may contribute to cervical cancer progression, but further in-vivo and mechanistic studies are needed.

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the potential role of miR-129 as a diagnostic and prognostic biomarker for HPV-associated cervical cancer. It also highlights the interaction between BMSCs and cervical cancer cells, which could inform future research on tumor microenvironment and therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and abstract-only data, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single study with no direct clinical outcomes reported.

厄瓜多尔男性 人乳头瘤病毒 相关癌症的住院治疗和 死亡率 负担:一项全国范围的人口研究

Hospitalization and mortality burden of human papillomavirus-associated cancers among men in Ecuador: a nationwide population-based study
作者： Xavier Sánchez, Ruth Jimbo-Sotomayor, Zira Mendes da Silva, Paula Cristina Pungartnik, Juan Carlos Orengo, Cintia Irene Parellada, Z. Dasilva
摘要中文翻译简介 男性中与 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关的癌症对癌症相关发病率和 死亡率 有很大影响,但来自拉丁美洲的基于人群的证据仍然有限,特别是在厄瓜多尔。材料和方法利用厄瓜多尔 2015-2019 年全国出院数据和 死亡率 数据库,这项回顾性二次数据分析量化了男性 HPV 相关癌症的负担,并使用已发布的特定地点归因分数估计了 HPV 归因负担。计算粗略比率和特定年龄比率,并使用世界卫生组织 (WHO) 世界标准人口获得年龄标准化比率 (ASR)。结果 2015年至2019年间,记录了3,442例因HPV相关癌症住院(ASR:每10万名男性中有9.7例),其中头颈癌占住院治疗的66.4%,其次是阴茎癌(24.0%)和肛门癌(9.6%)。尽管住院率和 死亡率 率随着年龄的增长而显着增加,但口咽癌、阴茎癌和肛门癌的住院率和约三分之一的死亡发生在年龄 <60 岁的男性中。应用已发布的特定部位归因分数,估计有 1,002 例住院治疗与 HPV 相关(ASR:每 100,000 名男性中有 2.8 例),其中阴茎癌所占比例最大。总共确定了 575 例因 HPV 相关癌症死亡(ASR:每 100,000 名男性 1.7 例),其中估计有 134 例与 HPV 相关(ASR:每 100,000 名男性 0.4 例)。死亡率 比率随着年龄的增长而逐渐增加。结论 HPV 相关癌症给厄瓜多尔男性带来了沉重的住院治疗和 死亡率 负担。尽管在老年人中观察到负担最高,但在 60 岁以下的男性中也存在相当大的负担。这些发现为 监测 提供了国家基线,并可能为未来 HPV 预防策略的评估提供信息,同时努力实现高且公平的 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率、早期 诊断、及时 治疗 以及改善癌症护理的可及性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在量化厄瓜多尔男性中 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关癌症的负担,解决拉丁美洲(尤其是厄瓜多尔)基于人口的有限证据。
设计与对象
一项基于厄瓜多尔 2015 年至 2019 年出院数据和 死亡率 数据库的全国性人口研究,量化了男性中 HPV 相关癌症的负担,并使用已发布的特定地点归因分数估计了 HPV 归因负担。
核心方法
计算粗略比率和特定年龄比率,并使用世界卫生组织 (WHO) 世界标准人口获得年龄标准化比率 (ASR)。
主要结果
During 2015–2019, 3,442 hospitalizations due to HPV-associated cancers were recorded (ASR: 9.7 per 100,000 men), with head and neck cancers accounting for 66.4% of hospitalizations, followed by penile cancer (24.0%) and anal cancer (9.6%). Mortality rates increased progressively with age, and more than half of hospitalizations and approximately one-third of deaths from oropharyngeal, penile, and anal cancers occurred among men aged <60 years.
解释与创新
研究结果为 监测 提供了国家基线,并可能为未来 HPV 预防策略的评估提供信息,同时努力实现高且公平的 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率、早期 诊断、及时 治疗 以及改善获得癌症护理的机会。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了厄瓜多尔男性中 HPV 相关癌症的大量住院治疗和 死亡率 负担,强调需要有针对性的公共卫生干预措施,特别是针对年轻男性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到二手数据的使用和对已发表的特定地点归因分数的依赖的限制,这可能会引入偏差。证据强度中等。

Hospitalization and mortality burden of human papillomavirus-associated cancers among men in Ecuador: a nationwide population-based study

Authors: Xavier Sánchez, Ruth Jimbo-Sotomayor, Zira Mendes da Silva, Paula Cristina Pungartnik, Juan Carlos Orengo, Cintia Irene Parellada, Z. Dasilva
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated cancers in men contribute substantially to cancer-related morbidity and mortality, yet population-based evidence from Latin America remain limited, particularly in Ecuador. Materials and methods Using nationwide hospital discharge and mortality databases from Ecuador for 2015–2019, this retrospective secondary data analysis quantified the burden of HPV-associated cancers among men and estimated the HPV-attributable burden using published site-specific attributable fractions. Crude and age-specific rates were calculated, and age-standardized rates (ASRs) were obtained using the World Health Organization (WHO) World Standard Population. Results During 2015–2019, 3,442 hospitalizations due to HPV-associated cancers were recorded (ASR: 9.7 per 100,000 men), with head and neck cancers accounting for 66.4% of hospitalizations, followed by penile cancer (24.0%) and anal cancer (9.6%). Although hospitalization and mortality rates increased markedly with age, more than half of hospitalizations and approximately one-third of deaths from oropharyngeal, penile, and anal cancers occurred among men aged <60 years. Applying published site-specific attributable fractions, 1,002 hospitalizations were estimated to be HPV-attributable (ASR: 2.8 per 100,000 men), with penile cancer contributing the largest share. A total of 575 deaths due to HPV-associated cancers were identified (ASR: 1.7 per 100,000 men), of which 134 were estimated to be HPV-attributable (ASR: 0.4 per 100,000 men). Mortality rates increased progressively with age. Conclusion HPV-associated cancers impose a substantial hospitalization and mortality burden among men in Ecuador. Although the highest burden was observed among older adults, a considerable burden was also evident among men aged <60 years. These findings provide a national baseline for surveillance and may inform future evaluations of HPV prevention strategies, alongside efforts to achieve high and equitable HPV vaccination coverage, earlier diagnosis, timely treatment, and improved access to cancer care.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to quantify the burden of human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated cancers among men in Ecuador, addressing the limited population-based evidence from Latin America, particularly in Ecuador.
Design and population
A nationwide population-based study using hospital discharge and mortality databases from 2015 to 2019 in Ecuador, quantifying the burden of HPV-associated cancers among men and estimating the HPV-attributable burden using published site-specific attributable fractions.
Core methods
Crude and age-specific rates were calculated, and age-standardized rates (ASRs) were obtained using the World Health Organization (WHO) World Standard Population.
Main results
During 2015–2019, 3,442 hospitalizations due to HPV-associated cancers were recorded (ASR: 9.7 per 100,000 men), with head and neck cancers accounting for 66.4% of hospitalizations, followed by penile cancer (24.0%) and anal cancer (9.6%). Mortality rates increased progressively with age, and more than half of hospitalizations and approximately one-third of deaths from oropharyngeal, penile, and anal cancers occurred among men aged <60 years.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a national baseline for surveillance and may inform future evaluations of HPV prevention strategies, alongside efforts to achieve high and equitable HPV vaccination coverage, earlier diagnosis, timely treatment, and improved access to cancer care.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the substantial hospitalization and mortality burden of HPV-associated cancers among men in Ecuador, emphasizing the need for targeted public health interventions, particularly for younger men.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of secondary data and the reliance on published site-specific attributable fractions, which may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate.

患病率,埃塞俄比亚东部孕妇高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 的基因型分布和相关因素:基于人群的 队列研究 研究中的横断面分析

Prevalence, genotype distribution and associated factors of high-risk human papillomavirus among pregnant women in Eastern Ethiopia: a cross-sectional analysis nested within a population-based cohort study
作者： Fitsum Weldegebreal, Bodine Van Eenooghe, Desalegn Admassu Ayana, Lisa Himschoot, Tara Wilfong, Tesfaye Assebe Yadeta, Merga Dheresa, Akewok Sime Negesa, Tadesse Gure Eticha, Abraham Geremew
摘要中文翻译高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染是宫颈癌前病变和宫颈癌的主要原因。尽管埃塞俄比亚推出了四价 Gardasil ® HPV 疫苗(针对 HPV6、11、16 和 18),但有关 HR-HPV 基因型分布和 疫苗 提供的覆盖范围的数据仍然有限。我们评估了埃塞俄比亚东部表面健康孕妇中 HR-HPV 的 患病率、基因型分布、理论 疫苗 覆盖率和相关母体因素。本研究是 EthiOMICS 队列研究 中的横断面分析,EthiOMICS 队列研究 是一项针对从 Hararghe 健康和人口统计 监测 系统站点招募的孕妇(妊娠 12-22 周)的基于人群的纵向研究。共采集217份阴道拭子,提取基因组DNA,并使用Seegene HR HPV AllplexTM检测针对14种高危基因型进行HR-HPV检测。使用 SPSS 28 版对数据进行分析。在 217 名孕妇中,HR-HPV 患病率 为 12.9%(95% CI:8.8–18.1)。HPV16 和 HPV66 是最常见的基因型(各 21.4%),其次是 HPV31、HPV58 和 HPV51(各 17.9%);35.7% HPV 阳性女性携带多种 HR-HPV 基因型。HR-HPV 阳性女性中四价 疫苗 的参与者水平基于理论基因型的覆盖率为 25.0%,9 价 疫苗 的覆盖率为 71.4%。在调整后的探索性分析中,微小解脲支原体和人型支原体的检测与 HR-HPV 相关。超过八分之一的孕妇携带 HR-HPV,其中超过三分之一的女性携带多种高危基因型。目前在埃塞俄比亚使用的四价 疫苗 在 HR-HPV 阳性女性中的理论参与者水平基于基因型的覆盖率为 25.0%。这些发现凸显了埃塞俄比亚东部孕妇中循环 HR-HPV 基因型的多样性,并强调了持续分子 监测 的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估埃塞俄比亚东部孕妇的 患病率、基因型分布和高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 的相关因素,解决该地区 HR-HPV 基因型分布和 疫苗 覆盖率数据有限的问题。
设计与对象
对从埃塞俄比亚东部 Hararghe 健康和人口统计 监测 系统站点招募的 217 名孕妇(妊娠 12-22 周)进行了横断面分析。
核心方法
从参与者身上收集阴道拭子,并提取基因组 DNA,使用针对 14 种高危基因型的 Seegene HR HPV AllplexTM 检测进行 HR-HPV 检测。使用 SPSS 28 版分析数据。
主要结果
HR-HPV 的 患病率 为 12.9%(95% CI:8.8-18.1),其中 HPV16 和 HPV66 是最常见的基因型(各 21.4%),其次是 HPV31、HPV58 和 HPV51(各 17.9%)。35.7% HPV 阳性女性携带多种 HR-HPV 基因型。HR-HPV 阳性女性中,四价 疫苗 的理论参与者水平基于基因型的覆盖率为 25.0%,9 价 疫苗 的覆盖率为 71.4%。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了埃塞俄比亚东部孕妇中循环 HR-HPV 基因型的多样性,并强调了持续分子 监测 的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为有针对性的 疫苗接种 策略和公共卫生干预措施的设计提供信息,以减轻该地区 HPV 相关疾病的负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计和样本量的限制,横断面设计没有建立因果关系,样本量可能无法代表埃塞俄比亚东部孕妇的全部人口。

Prevalence, genotype distribution and associated factors of high-risk human papillomavirus among pregnant women in Eastern Ethiopia: a cross-sectional analysis nested within a population-based cohort study

Authors: Fitsum Weldegebreal, Bodine Van Eenooghe, Desalegn Admassu Ayana, Lisa Himschoot, Tara Wilfong, Tesfaye Assebe Yadeta, Merga Dheresa, Akewok Sime Negesa, Tadesse Gure Eticha, Abraham Geremew
Original abstract (English)
High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection is the main cause of cervical precancer and cancer. Although Ethiopia introduced the quadrivalent Gardasil ® HPV vaccine (targeting HPV6, 11, 16, and 18), data on the distribution of HR-HPV genotype and the coverage provided by the vaccine remains limited. We assessed the prevalence, genotype distribution, theoretical vaccine coverage, and associated maternal factors for HR-HPV among apparently healthy pregnant women in eastern Ethiopia. This study was a cross-sectional analysis nested within the EthiOMICS cohort, a population based longitudinal study of pregnant women (12–22 weeks of gestation) recruited from the Hararghe Health and Demographic Surveillance System Site. A total of 217 vaginal swabs were collected, genomic DNA was extracted, and HR-HPV detection was performed using the Seegene HR HPV AllplexTM assay targeting 14 high-risk genotypes. Data were analyzed using SPSS version 28. Among the 217 pregnant women, HR-HPV prevalence was 12.9% (95% CI: 8.8–18.1). HPV16 and HPV66 were the most common genotypes (21.4% each), followed by HPV31, HPV58, and HPV51 (17.9% each); 35.7% of HPV-positive women harbored multiple HR-HPV genotypes. Participant-level theoretical genotype-based coverage among HR-HPV-positive women was 25.0% for the quadrivalent vaccine and 71.4% for the 9-valent vaccine. Detection of Ureaplasma parvum and Mycoplasma hominis was associated with HR-HPV in the adjusted exploratory analysis. More than one in eight pregnant women harbored HR-HPV, and more than one-third of these women had multiple high-risk genotypes. Theoretical participant-level genotype-based coverage of the quadrivalent vaccine currently used in Ethiopia was 25.0% among HR-HPV-positive women. These findings highlight the diversity of circulating HR-HPV genotypes among pregnant women in eastern Ethiopia and underscore the importance of continued molecular surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the prevalence, genotype distribution, and associated factors of high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) among pregnant women in eastern Ethiopia, addressing the limited data on HR-HPV genotype distribution and vaccine coverage in the region.
Design and population
A cross-sectional analysis was conducted among 217 pregnant women (12–22 weeks of gestation) recruited from the Hararghe Health and Demographic Surveillance System Site in eastern Ethiopia.
Core methods
Vaginal swabs were collected from the participants, and genomic DNA was extracted for HR-HPV detection using the Seegene HR HPV AllplexTM assay targeting 14 high-risk genotypes. Data were analyzed using SPSS version 28.
Main results
The prevalence of HR-HPV was 12.9% (95% CI: 8.8–18.1), with HPV16 and HPV66 being the most common genotypes (21.4% each), followed by HPV31, HPV58, and HPV51 (17.9% each). 35.7% of HPV-positive women harbored multiple HR-HPV genotypes. The theoretical participant-level genotype-based coverage among HR-HPV-positive women was 25.0% for the quadrivalent vaccine and 71.4% for the 9-valent vaccine.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the diversity of circulating HR-HPV genotypes among pregnant women in eastern Ethiopia and underscore the importance of continued molecular surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform the design of targeted vaccination strategies and public health interventions to reduce the burden of HPV-associated diseases in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which does not establish causality, and the sample size, which may not be representative of the entire population of pregnant women in eastern Ethiopia.

跨期偏好和社会信号如何驱动父母的选择? 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 上的离散选择实验

How do intertemporal preference and social signals drive parental choices? a discrete choice experiment on human papillomavirus vaccination
作者： Yuan Hao He, Tian-Qi Chen, Qing Shen, Zi-Xuan Zhang, Ji-Zhong He, Guo-Feng Yue
摘要中文翻译父母对 疫苗 的犹豫限制了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 的吸收。然而,跨期偏好和社会信号与这种犹豫之间的关系仍有待探索。一项在线调查整合了离散选择实验(DCE)和跨期选择任务,用于量化属性偏好、货币贴现率和影响父母决策的因素。使用混合 Logit 模型估计主要属性偏好,而潜在类别分析则确定偏好异质性。此外,还利用交互项来研究跨期偏好如何调节这些选择。经验数据显示,保护期限和 疫苗 有效性是父母选择的主要决定因素,而表明医疗保健专业人员的孩子已接种疫苗的社会参考信息也很重要。疫苗 起源没有表现出明显的影响。潜在类别分析将 队列研究 分为接受的大多数 (79.4%) 和犹豫的少数 (20.6%)。交互模型表明,货币贴现与偏好结构共同相关,尽管没有个体属性交互能够在多重测试的调整中幸存下来。父母对 HPV 疫苗接种 的选择反映了临床属性、社会参考信息和跨期偏好。保护期限和 疫苗 的有效性最重要,当父母被告知医疗保健专业人员的孩子已经接种疫苗时,他们更经常选择 疫苗接种。较高的货币贴现率与接受多数的成员资格相关,尽管在多次测试调整后属性级别的相互作用是探索性的。降低自付费用和传达可信的社会参考信息作为干预策略值得测试。鉴于样本主要是城市和受过高等教育的人,这些研究结果应谨慎推广。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
父母对 疫苗 的犹豫限制了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 的吸收。然而,跨期偏好和社会信号与这种犹豫之间的关系仍有待探索。
设计与对象
一项在线调查整合了离散选择实验(DCE)和跨期选择任务,用于量化属性偏好、货币贴现率和影响父母决策的因素。该研究将 队列研究 分为接受的大多数 (79.4%) 和犹豫的少数 (20.6%)。
核心方法
使用混合 Logit 模型估计主要属性偏好,而潜在类别分析则确定偏好异质性。交互项被用来检查跨期偏好如何调节这些选择。
主要结果
经验数据显示,保护期限和 疫苗 有效性是父母选择的主要决定因素,而表明医疗保健专业人员的孩子已接种疫苗的社会参考信息也很重要。较高的货币贴现率与接受多数的成员资格有关。
解释与创新
父母对 HPV 疫苗接种 的选择反映了临床属性、社会参考信息和跨期偏好。保护期限和 疫苗 的有效性最重要,当父母被告知医疗保健专业人员的孩子已经接种疫苗时,他们更经常选择 疫苗接种。
科研与公卫意义
降低自付费用和传达可信的社会参考信息作为干预策略值得测试。鉴于样本主要是城市和受过高等教育的人,这些研究结果应谨慎推广。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告与研究设计或统计方法相关的具体限制。由于多次测试调整后属性级交互的探索性,证据强度中等。

How do intertemporal preference and social signals drive parental choices? a discrete choice experiment on human papillomavirus vaccination

Authors: Yuan Hao He, Tian-Qi Chen, Qing Shen, Zi-Xuan Zhang, Ji-Zhong He, Guo-Feng Yue
Original abstract (English)
Parental vaccine hesitancy constrains human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination uptake. However, how intertemporal preferences and social signals relate to this hesitancy remains underexplored. An online survey integrating a discrete choice experiment (DCE) and an intertemporal choice task was deployed to quantify attribute preferences, monetary discount rates, and the factors shaping parental decisions. Primary attribute preferences were estimated using a mixed logit model, while latent class analysis identified preference heterogeneity. Additionally, interaction terms were utilized to examine how intertemporal preferences modulate these choices. Empirical data reveal that protection duration and vaccine effectiveness were the primary determinants of parental choice, while social-reference information indicating that healthcare professionals’ children had been vaccinated was also valued. Vaccine origin showed no significant effect. Latent class analysis segmented the cohort into a receptive majority (79.4%) and a hesitant minority (20.6%). Interaction models indicated that monetary discounting was jointly associated with the structure of preferences, although no individual attribute interaction survived adjustment for multiple testing. Parental choices about HPV vaccination reflected clinical attributes, social reference information, and intertemporal preferences. Protection duration and vaccine effectiveness mattered most, and parents more often chose vaccination when told that healthcare professionals’ children had been vaccinated. Higher monetary discount rates were associated with membership in the receptive majority, although the attribute-level interactions were exploratory after adjustment for multiple testing. Reducing out-of-pocket costs and communicating credible social-reference information are worth testing as intervention strategies. Given the predominantly urban and highly educated sample, these findings should be generalized with caution.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Parental vaccine hesitancy constrains human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination uptake. However, how intertemporal preferences and social signals relate to this hesitancy remains underexplored.
Design and population
An online survey integrating a discrete choice experiment (DCE) and an intertemporal choice task was deployed to quantify attribute preferences, monetary discount rates, and the factors shaping parental decisions. The study segmented the cohort into a receptive majority (79.4%) and a hesitant minority (20.6%).
Core methods
Primary attribute preferences were estimated using a mixed logit model, while latent class analysis identified preference heterogeneity. Interaction terms were utilized to examine how intertemporal preferences modulate these choices.
Main results
Empirical data reveal that protection duration and vaccine effectiveness were the primary determinants of parental choice, while social-reference information indicating that healthcare professionals’ children had been vaccinated was also valued. Higher monetary discount rates were associated with membership in the receptive majority.
Interpretation and novelty
Parental choices about HPV vaccination reflected clinical attributes, social reference information, and intertemporal preferences. Protection duration and vaccine effectiveness mattered most, and parents more often chose vaccination when told that healthcare professionals’ children had been vaccinated.
Scientific and public-health significance
Reducing out-of-pocket costs and communicating credible social-reference information are worth testing as intervention strategies. Given the predominantly urban and highly educated sample, these findings should be generalized with caution.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations related to the study design or statistical methods. The evidence strength is moderate due to the exploratory nature of the attribute-level interactions after adjustment for multiple testing.

口腔健康专业人员和 人乳头瘤病毒 预防:紧急行动呼吁

Oral Health Professionals and Human Papillomavirus Prevention: An Urgent Call to Action
作者： Lakshman Samaranayake, Saman Warnakulasuriya, Lakshman Perera Samaranayake
摘要中文翻译口腔健康专家
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究强调了口腔健康专业人员在预防 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染和相关疾病方面的关键作用,强调需要提高这一领域的认识和采取积极措施。
设计与对象
该研究是《国际牙科杂志》的社论,发表于 2026-12-01,重点关注口腔健康专业人员在 HPV 预防中的责任。
核心方法
该社论概述了将 HPV 预防策略纳入常规牙科护理的重要性,包括教育、筛查以及与其他医疗保健提供者的合作。
主要结果
该社论报道称,口腔健康专业人员有独特的机会通过早期检测和患者教育为 HPV 预防做出贡献,但没有提供具体的数字结果。
解释与创新
作者解释说,将 HPV 预防纳入牙科实践是减少 HPV 相关疾病 发病率 的一种新方法,特别是在以口腔健康专业人员为主要接触点的人群中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,口腔健康专业人员可以在 HPV 监测 和预防中发挥关键作用,这对旨在减轻 HPV 相关癌症和感染负担的公共卫生战略具有重大影响。
局限与证据强度
作为一篇社论,该研究没有提供原始数据,其结论是基于现有文献和专家意见,这将证据的强度限制在中等。

Oral Health Professionals and Human Papillomavirus Prevention: An Urgent Call to Action

Authors: Lakshman Samaranayake, Saman Warnakulasuriya, Lakshman Perera Samaranayake
Original abstract (English)
Oral health professionals
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study highlights the critical role of oral health professionals in preventing human papillomavirus (HPV) infections and associated diseases, emphasizing the need for increased awareness and proactive measures in this field.
Design and population
The study is an editorial in the International Dental Journal, published on 2026-12-01, focusing on the responsibilities of oral health professionals in HPV prevention.
Core methods
The editorial outlines the importance of integrating HPV prevention strategies into routine dental care, including education, screening, and collaboration with other healthcare providers.
Main results
The editorial reports that oral health professionals have a unique opportunity to contribute to HPV prevention through early detection and patient education, though no specific numerical results are provided.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the integration of HPV prevention into dental practice is a novel approach to reducing the incidence of HPV-related diseases, particularly in populations where oral health professionals are the primary point of contact.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that oral health professionals can play a pivotal role in HPV surveillance and prevention, which has significant implications for public health strategies aimed at reducing the burden of HPV-associated cancers and infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
As an editorial, the study does not present original data, and its conclusions are based on existing literature and expert opinion, which limits the strength of the evidence to moderate.
