31 个非洲国家女孩中 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 覆盖率的模式、分布和预测因素

Patterns, distributions and predictors of human papillomavirus vaccination coverage among girls across 31 African countries
作者： Motunrayo D. Olalere, Victoria Etuk, Oyewole Kazeem Oyedele, Sophia Osawe, Nifarta Andrew, Evaezi Okpokoro
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是撒哈拉以南非洲地区女性癌症相关死亡的主要原因,非洲约占全球 宫颈癌 死亡率 的 23%。尽管 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 在预防 宫颈癌 方面已被证明有效,但覆盖率仍远低于 90% 的全球目标。
设计与对象
本研究评估了 HPV 疫苗接种 趋势、区域差异以及 疫苗接种 计划持续时间对非洲女孩和年轻女性 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率的影响。有关 HPV 免疫的国家数据来自世界卫生组织免疫门户网站。
核心方法
描述性统计用于估计平均覆盖率(标准差)、变异和区域分布百分比。线性回归分析检查了 HPV 免疫计划持续时间与 疫苗接种 覆盖率的关联。
主要结果
到 2024 年,54 个非洲国家中只有 31 个 (56%) 将 HPV 疫苗接种 纳入常规免疫计划,主要针对 9-14 岁的女孩。31 个国家的平均首次剂量和末次剂量覆盖率分别为 64% (SD: 25.4) 和 55% (SD: 27.3)。第一剂和最后一剂覆盖率最低的是马里(15%)、冈比亚(15%)和摩洛哥(3%),而佛得角、布基纳法索、乌干达、坦桑尼亚联合共和国和毛里求斯的两剂覆盖率均超过 90%。从区域来看,东部和南部非洲的首剂和末次接种覆盖率分别为 77% 和 61%,西非和中非为 63% 和 60%,北非为 52% 和 27%。
解释与创新
Across the 31 countries, HPV immunisation programme duration was positively associated with first-dose coverage (coefficient = 2.97, 95% CI: 0.28–5.66, p = 0.032), with each additional year of the programme associated with a 3% increase in vaccination coverage. In 2022, second-dose coverage was significantly associated with both programme duration (β = 4.192, SE = 1.278, 95% CI: 1.53–6.85, p = 0.004) and first-dose vaccination coverage (β = 0.321, SE = 0.133, 95% CI: 0.04–0.59, p = 0.025).
科研与公卫意义
非洲的 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率正在缓慢增加,但仍低于目标。然而,较长的计划持续时间与较高的吸收率相关,这凸显了持续投资和加强计划交付以实现全球 疫苗接种 目标的必要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告任何设计限制、偏差、混杂、样本量、对照或普遍性问题。

Patterns, distributions and predictors of human papillomavirus vaccination coverage among girls across 31 African countries

Authors: Motunrayo D. Olalere, Victoria Etuk, Oyewole Kazeem Oyedele, Sophia Osawe, Nifarta Andrew, Evaezi Okpokoro
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer is a leading cause of cancer-related deaths among women in sub-Saharan Africa, with Africa accounting for approximately 23% of global cervical cancer mortality. Despite the proven efficacy of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines in the prevention of cervical cancer, coverage remains far below the 90% global target. This study assessed HPV vaccination trends, regional differences, and the effect of vaccination programme duration on HPV vaccination coverage among girls and young women in Africa. Country data on HPV immunisation were obtained from the World Health Organization Immunization Portal. Descriptive statistics were used to estimate mean coverage (standard deviation), variation, and regional distribution in percentages. Linear regression analyses examined the association of HPV immunisation programme duration with vaccination coverage. By 2024, only 31 of 54 (56%) African countries had integrated HPV vaccination into their routine immunisation schedules, mostly targeting girls aged 9–14 years. The mean first-dose and last-dose coverage across the 31 countries were 64% (SD: 25.4) and 55% (SD: 27.3) respectively. The lowest coverage rates for the first and last doses were in Mali (15%), Gambia (15%) and Morocco (3%), whereas Cape Verde, Burkina Faso, Uganda, United Republic of Tanzania, and Mauritius achieved over 90% for both doses. Regionally, first and last-dose coverage were 77% and 61% in Eastern and Southern Africa, 63% and 60% in West and Central Africa, and 52% and 27% in North Africa, respectively. Across the 31 countries, HPV immunisation programme duration was positively associated with first-dose coverage (coefficient = 2.97, 95% CI: 0.28–5.66, p = 0.032), with each additional year of the programme associated with a 3% increase in vaccination coverage. In 2022, second-dose coverage was significantly associated with both programme duration (β = 4.192, SE = 1.278, 95% CI: 1.53–6.85, p = 0.004) and first-dose vaccination coverage (β = 0.321, SE = 0.133, 95% CI: 0.04–0.59, p = 0.025). HPV vaccination coverage in Africa is increasing slowly but remains below the target. However, longer programme duration is associated with higher uptake, highlighting the need for sustained investment and strengthened programme delivery to meet global vaccination targets.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer is a leading cause of cancer-related deaths among women in sub-Saharan Africa, with Africa accounting for approximately 23% of global cervical cancer mortality. Despite the proven efficacy of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines in the prevention of cervical cancer, coverage remains far below the 90% global target.

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report any design limitations, bias, confounding, sample size, controls, or generalisability issues.

所有 HPV16/18 阳性患者都需要进行宫颈内刮治术 (ECC)吗? 670个案例分层分析

Is Endocervical Curettage (ECC) Necessary for All HPV16/18-Positive Patients? A Stratified Analysis of 670 Cases
作者： Ming-Zhu Li, Wei Zhang, Yi-Shan Wang, Yun Zhao, Li-Hua Ren, Chao Zhao, Jing-Ran Li, Li-Hui Wei, 任丽华
摘要中文翻译背景:高危型HPV16/18亚型占宫颈癌的70%以上,但所有HPV16/18阳性患者是否都应该常规接受宫颈管刮除术(ECC)?方法:对2021年至2024年在北京大学人民医院接受阴道镜检查的670例HPV16/18阳性患者进行回顾性队列研究研究。根据既往HPV感染史、当前宫颈细胞学和HPV检测结果、转化区(TZ)类型和阴道镜印象进行分层分析。主要结果是最终病理学中宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN2)+以上的检出率以及ECC亚组中ECC的额外诊断率。结果:670 名 HPV16/18 阳性女性中,CIN2+ 检出率为 32.5%(218/670);CIN2+ 组明显比 < CIN2 组年轻(中位年龄 36.5 岁 vs. 41.0 岁,p < 0.001)。高级别阴道镜印模 (G2) 将观察到的 CIN2+ 比例急剧提高至 100%,尤其是与高级别细胞学结合时。相比之下,在既往 HPV 阴性 + 低级别细胞学检查 + 可见 TZ 的情况下,正常/G1 阴道镜检查结果有效地将风险降低至 4.8%。在523份ECC标本中,值得注意的是,即使最终病理诊断为CIN2+,仍有相当高比例的患者(44.4-64.7%)的宫颈管内没有病变;在阴道镜印象(G2)人群中,68.1%的ECC是正常的。ECC相对于定向活检的增量产率仅为2.7%(14/523),并且所有14个增量病例都发生在TZ3(100%)。在 146 名符合复合低风险特征(TZ1/2 结合正常/G1 阴道镜印象)的女性中,没有人出现 CIN2+ 检测增量。结论:患有 HPV16/18 阳性 CIN2+ 病变的女性发病年龄较轻。大约三分之一被诊断为 CIN2+,其中一半以上具有正常的 ECC 组织学。由于观察到的附加诊断率较低,ECC 仅对 2.7% 接受阴道镜检查的 HPV16/18 阳性女性提供了增量诊断益处,几乎全部针对具有 3 型转化区的患者。综合低风险概况确定了 ECC 附加诊断率极低的亚组;然而,在建议在该亚组中省略 ECC 作为临床实践的改变之前,需要进行前瞻性验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危HPV16/18亚型占宫颈癌的70%以上,但所有HPV16/18阳性患者都应该常规接受宫颈管刮除术(ECC)吗?
设计与对象
队列研究回顾性研究对2021年至2024年在北京大学人民医院接受阴道镜检查的670例HPV16/18阳性患者进行。
核心方法
根据既往 HPV 感染史、当前宫颈细胞学和 HPV 检测结果、转化区 (TZ) 类型和阴道镜印象进行分层分析。主要结果是最终病理学中宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN2)+以上的检出率以及ECC亚组中ECC的额外诊断率。
主要结果
在 670 名 HPV16/18 阳性女性中,CIN2+ 占 32.5% (218/670);CIN2+ 组明显比 < CIN2 组年轻(中位年龄 36.5 岁 vs. 41.0 岁,p < 0.001)。相比之下,在既往 HPV 阴性 + 低级别细胞学检查 + 可见 TZ 的情况下,正常/G1 阴道镜检查结果有效地将风险降低至 4.8%。ECC相对于定向活检的增量产率仅为2.7%(14/523),并且所有14个增量病例都发生在TZ3(100%)。
解释与创新
患有 HPV16/18 阳性 CIN2+ 病变的女性发病年龄较轻。大约三分之一被诊断为 CIN2+,其中一半以上具有正常的 ECC 组织学。
科研与公卫意义
由于观察到的附加诊断率较低,ECC 仅对 2.7% 接受阴道镜检查的 HPV16/18 阳性女性提供了增量诊断益处,几乎全部针对具有 3 型转化区的患者。
局限与证据强度
综合低风险概况确定了 ECC 附加诊断率极低的亚组;然而,在建议在该亚组中省略 ECC 作为临床实践的改变之前,需要进行前瞻性验证。

Is Endocervical Curettage (ECC) Necessary for All HPV16/18-Positive Patients? A Stratified Analysis of 670 Cases

Authors: Ming-Zhu Li, Wei Zhang, Yi-Shan Wang, Yun Zhao, Li-Hua Ren, Chao Zhao, Jing-Ran Li, Li-Hui Wei, 任丽华
Original abstract (English)
Background: High-risk HPV16/18 subtypes account for over 70% of cervical cancers, but should all HPV16/18-positive patients routinely undergo endocervical curettage (ECC)? Methods: A retrospective cohort study was conducted on 670 HPV16/18-positive patients who underwent colposcopy at Peking University People’s Hospital from 2021 to 2024. Stratified analysis was performed based on prior HPV infection history, current cervical cytology and HPV test results, transformation zone (TZ) type, and colposcopic impression. Primary outcome was the detection rate of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia above (CIN2)+ on final pathology and the additional diagnostic yield of ECC in the ECC subgroup. Results: Among the 670 HPV16/18-positive women, CIN2+ was identified in 32.5% (218/670); the CIN2+ group was significantly younger than the < CIN2 group (median 36.5 vs. 41.0 years, p < 0.001). A high-grade colposcopic impression (G2) steeply elevated the observed CIN2+ proportion to 100%, especially combined with high-grade cytology. In contrast, a Normal/G1 colposcopic finding effectively downgraded the risk to 4.8% in the presence of prior HPV negativity + low-grade cytology + visible TZ. Among the 523 ECC specimens, it is noteworthy that even when the final pathology was diagnosed as CIN2+, a considerably high proportion of patients (44.4–64.7%) remained free of lesions in the endocervical canal; in the colposcopy impression (G2) population, 68.1% of the ECC was normal. The incremental yield of ECC over directed biopsy was only 2.7% (14/523), and all 14 incremental cases occurred in TZ3 (100%). Among the 146 women meeting the composite low-risk profile (TZ1/2 combined with a Normal/G1 colposcopic impression), none had an incremental CIN2+ detection. Conclusion: Women with HPV16/18-positive CIN2+ lesions presented at a younger age. Approximately one-third were diagnosed with CIN2+, and more than half of these had normal ECC histology. With a low additional diagnostic rate observed, ECC provided incremental diagnostic benefit in only 2.7% of HPV16/18-positive women undergoing colposcopy, almost exclusively among patients with a Type 3 transformation zone. The composite low-risk profile identified a subgroup with an extremely low additional diagnostic yield of ECC; however, prospective validation is required before the omission of ECC in this subgroup can be recommended as a change in clinical practice.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High-risk HPV16/18 subtypes account for over 70% of cervical cancers, but should all HPV16/18-positive patients routinely undergo endocervical curettage (ECC)?
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted on 670 HPV16/18-positive patients who underwent colposcopy at Peking University People’s Hospital from 2021 to 2024.
Core methods

Primary outcome was the detection rate of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia above (CIN2)+ on final pathology and the additional diagnostic yield of ECC in the ECC subgroup.

Limitations and evidence strength
The composite low-risk profile identified a subgroup with an extremely low additional diagnostic yield of ECC; however, prospective validation is required before the omission of ECC in this subgroup can be recommended as a change in clinical practice.

具有 CD137 激动作用的治疗性 HPV16 E7 疫苗接种 可诱导小鼠肿瘤消退和持久免疫力

Therapeutic HPV16 E7 vaccination with CD137 agonism induces tumor regression and durable immunity in mice
作者： Yuqian Ou, Wenshu Lu, Hanjun Zhao, Constantinos Demetrious, Qing Zhang, Qiujie Zheng, Yanyan Jiang, Peter Kok-Ting Wan, Eric E. O’Neill, Lina Zhang
摘要中文翻译预防性药物 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 可预防新感染,但不能治疗已形成的 HPV 相关病变,这凸显了针对病毒肿瘤抗原的治疗策略的必要性。我们开发了一种基于重组重叠肽的 HPV16 E7 疫苗 (ROP-E7),并在 C57BL/6 小鼠中单独或与 CD137 (4-1BB) 激动剂联合评估其免疫原性和抗肿瘤活性。在无肿瘤小鼠中,ROP-E7 比野生型 E7 诱导更强的辅助性和细胞毒性 T 细胞反应。在荷瘤小鼠中,ROP-E7 减轻了肿瘤负荷并提高了生存率,而组合 治疗 则诱导肿瘤完全消退和超过 100 天的无瘤生存期。这些效应与瘤内细胞毒性 T 细胞的增加以及调节性和耗尽的 T 细胞群的减少有关。这些发现为将治疗性 HPV16 E7 疫苗接种 与 T 细胞共刺激相结合治疗 HPV 相关癌症提供了临床前支持。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
预防性药物 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 可预防新感染,但不能治疗已形成的 HPV 相关病变,这凸显了针对病毒肿瘤抗原的治疗策略的必要性。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
在荷瘤小鼠中,ROP-E7 减轻了肿瘤负荷并提高了生存率,而组合 治疗 则诱导肿瘤完全消退和超过 100 天的无瘤生存期。
主要结果
这些效应与瘤内细胞毒性 T 细胞的增加以及调节性和耗尽的 T 细胞群的减少有关。
解释与创新
这些发现为将治疗性 HPV16 E7 疫苗接种 与 T 细胞共刺激相结合治疗 HPV 相关癌症提供了临床前支持。
科研与公卫意义
所提供的证据没有明确说明具体的科学或公共卫生含义。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Therapeutic HPV16 E7 vaccination with CD137 agonism induces tumor regression and durable immunity in mice

Authors: Yuqian Ou, Wenshu Lu, Hanjun Zhao, Constantinos Demetrious, Qing Zhang, Qiujie Zheng, Yanyan Jiang, Peter Kok-Ting Wan, Eric E. O’Neill, Lina Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Prophylactic human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines prevent new infections but do not treat established HPV-associated lesions, highlighting the need for therapeutic strategies targeting viral tumor antigens. We developed a recombinant overlapping peptide-based HPV16 E7 vaccine (ROP-E7) and evaluated its immunogenicity and antitumor activity, alone or combined with CD137 (4-1BB) agonism, in C57BL/6 mice. In tumor-free mice, ROP-E7 induced greater helper and cytotoxic T cell responses than wild-type E7. In tumor-bearing mice, ROP-E7 reduced tumor burden and improved survival, while combination treatment induced complete tumor regression and tumor-free survival beyond 100 days. These effects were associated with increased intratumoral cytotoxic T cells and reduced regulatory and exhausted T cell populations. These findings provide preclinical support for combining therapeutic HPV16 E7 vaccination with T cell co-stimulation in HPV-associated cancers.

CaCx 细胞系中 HPV-16 E6/E7 蛋白的沉默上调 POTE-Paralogs 的表达

Silencing of E6/E7 proteins of HPV-16 in the CaCx cell line upregulate the expression of POTE-Paralogs
作者： Niranjan Kumar, Rashmi Rani Sahu, Amrita Singh, Akanksha Vyas, Saurabh Kumar, Prerona Das, Monika Sachdev
摘要中文翻译已知POTE蛋白在正常前列腺、胎盘、卵巢、睾丸和胚胎等组织中表达,并且基于这种器官特异性表达统称为POTE家族蛋白。POTE 基因在染色体 21q11.2 上跨越 32 kb,尽管其同源基因分布在 8 个不同的染色体上。POTEE 作为 POTE 家族的成员,已被确定为多种癌症类型的癌症种系抗原 (CGA),包括结直肠癌、胰腺癌、乳腺癌、肝癌和肺癌。本研究旨在阐明 POTE-Paralogs (POTEE & POTEF) 作为 宫颈癌 (CaCx) 中 CGA 标记的作用。超过 90% 的 CaCx 病例与 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 持续感染有关;HPV 的 E6 和 E7 癌蛋白通过肿瘤抑制蛋白 p53 和 pRB 的降解或失活而促进癌变,导致不受控制的细胞增殖。因此,HPV阳性宫颈癌细胞系HeLa和CaSki缺乏可检测到的p53表达,并且POTE-Paralogs的表达也显着降低,而HPV阴性CaCx细胞系C-33A表现出与POTE-Paralogs显着上调相关的高p53表达。使用 p53 特异性抑制剂的 C-33A 细胞的 治疗 降低了 POTE-Paralogs 的表达。相反,用化疗剂阿霉素恢复 CaSki 细胞中 p53 的表达导致 POTE-Paralogs 表达增加。此外,CaSki 细胞中 E6/E7 的沉默导致 p53 和 pRB 表达恢复,以及 POTEE 和 POTEF 水平增加。[BMB 报告 2026;59(10):466-473]。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究调查了 POTE-Paralogs 作为癌症种系抗原 (CGA) 标记物在 宫颈癌 (CaCx) 中的作用,并探讨了它们与 HPV 感染相关的表达模式。背景强调了 POTE 家族蛋白的器官特异性表达及其与癌症生物学的潜在相关性。
设计与对象
该研究检测了 HPV 阳性 宫颈癌 细胞系 HeLa 和 CaSki,以及 HPV 阴性 CaCx 细胞系 C-33A。它比较了这些细胞系中 POTE-Paralogs 的表达水平,以了解它们与 HPV 感染和癌发生的关系。
核心方法
该研究使用分子技术沉默 CaSki 细胞中的 E6/E7 蛋白,并评估由此产生的 p53、pRB 和 POTE-Paralogs 表达的变化。它还使用阿霉素等化疗药物来恢复 p53 表达并评估对 POTE-Paralogs 水平的影响。
主要结果
HPV 阳性 宫颈癌 细胞系 HeLa 和 CaSki 显示 POTE-Paralogs 表达显着降低,而 HPV 阴性细胞系 C-33A 表现出与 POTE-Paralogs 上调相关的高 p53 表达。CaSki 细胞中 E6/E7 的沉默恢复了 p53 和 pRB 的表达,同时 POTEE 和 POTEF 水平增加。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,POTE-Paralogs 的表达与 HPV 感染及其癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 的活性呈负相关。这项研究为 HPV 感染与 宫颈癌 中 POTE-Paralogs 下调之间的分子机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 诊断标记的开发提供信息,并有助于了解 POTE-Paralogs 在肿瘤抑制中的作用。他们还强调了与 HPV 阳性癌症中 p53 恢复相关的潜在治疗靶点。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外细胞系,不考虑体内条件或临床结果。然而,基于直接分子观察以及与 HPV 感染状态的相关性,证据确凿。

Silencing of E6/E7 proteins of HPV-16 in the CaCx cell line upregulate the expression of POTE-Paralogs

Authors: Niranjan Kumar, Rashmi Rani Sahu, Amrita Singh, Akanksha Vyas, Saurabh Kumar, Prerona Das, Monika Sachdev
Original abstract (English)
POTE proteins are known to be expressed in tissues such as normal prostate, placenta, ovary, testis, and embryo, and are collectively referred to as POTE-family proteins based on this organ-specific expression. The POTE gene spans 32 kb on chromosome 21q11.2, although its homologous genes are distributed across eight different chromosomes. POTEE, as a member of the POTE family, has been identified as a Cancer Germline Antigen (CGA) across several cancer types including Colorectal, Pancreatic, Breast, Liver, and Lung cancers. This study aims to elucidate the role of POTE-Paralogs (POTEE & POTEF) as CGA markers in Cervical Cancer (CaCx). Over 90% of CaCx cases are associated with persistent infection by high-risk HPV (HR-HPV); the E6 and E7 oncoproteins of HPV contribute to carcinogenesis through the degradation or inactivation of tumor suppressor proteins p53 and pRB, leading to uncontrolled cell proliferation. Consequently, HPV-positive cervical cancer cell lines HeLa and CaSki lack detectable expression of p53, and the expression of POTE-Paralogs is also markedly decreased, while the HPV-negative CaCx cell line C-33A exhibits high p53 expression correlated with marked upregulation of POTE-Paralogs. Treatment of C-33A cells with a p53-specific inhibitor reduced POTE-Paralogs expression. Conversely, restoring p53 expression in CaSki cells with the chemotherapeutic agent Doxorubicin resulted in increased expression of POTE-Paralogs. Furthermore, silencing of E6/E7 in CaSki cells led to restoration of both p53 and pRB expression, as well as an increase in POTEE & POTEF levels. [BMB Reports 2026; 59(10): 466-473].
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates the role of POTE-Paralogs as Cancer Germline Antigen (CGA) markers in Cervical Cancer (CaCx) and explores their expression patterns in relation to HPV infection. The background highlights the organ-specific expression of POTE-family proteins and their potential relevance to cancer biology.
Design and population
The study examines HPV-positive cervical cancer cell lines HeLa and CaSki, as well as the HPV-negative CaCx cell line C-33A. It compares the expression levels of POTE-Paralogs in these cell lines to understand their association with HPV infection and carcinogenesis.
Core methods
The study uses molecular techniques to silence E6/E7 proteins in CaSki cells and assesses the resulting changes in p53, pRB, and POTE-Paralogs expression. It also employs chemotherapeutic agents like Doxorubicin to restore p53 expression and evaluates the impact on POTE-Paralogs levels.
Main results
HPV-positive cervical cancer cell lines HeLa and CaSki show markedly decreased expression of POTE-Paralogs, while the HPV-negative cell line C-33A exhibits high p53 expression correlated with upregulated POTE-Paralogs. Silencing of E6/E7 in CaSki cells restored p53 and pRB expression, along with increased POTEE and POTEF levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the expression of POTE-Paralogs is inversely related to HPV infection and the activity of its oncoproteins E6 and E7. This study provides novel insights into the molecular mechanisms linking HPV infection to the downregulation of POTE-Paralogs in cervical cancer.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform the development of diagnostic markers for HPV-associated cervical cancer and contribute to understanding the role of POTE-Paralogs in tumor suppression. They also highlight potential therapeutic targets related to p53 restoration in HPV-positive cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro cell lines and does not account for in vivo conditions or clinical outcomes. However, the evidence is strong based on direct molecular observations and correlations with HPV infection status.

南京市高淳区39335名女性人乳头瘤病毒感染流行病学特征及基因型分布

Epidemiological characteristics and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus infection among 39,335 women in Gaochun District, Nanjing, China
作者： Zhi-Li Zhou, Ping Wang, Jieling Zhang, Li Zhang, 张杰灵
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解中国南京市高淳区女性HPV阳性率、年龄分布、感染模式、基因型分布以及HPV阳性率的年度变化,为区域HPV 监测和宫颈癌预防和控制研究提供本地基线数据。
设计与对象
开展了一项单中心回顾性横断面研究,纳入 2017 年 1 月至 2024 年 9 月期间在南京高淳人民医院接受 HPV 检测的 39,335 名女性。
核心方法
对于研究期间有多次HPV检测记录的女性,仅保留第一次检测结果。按年龄组分析 HPV 阳性率、风险类别分布、感染模式和基因型分布。
主要结果
An overall HPV positivity rate of 19.2% (95% CI, 18.8%–19.6%) was observed, with significant differences in positivity rates among age groups (χ2=111.104, P < 0.001). The highest positivity rate was found in women aged 0–19 years (44.2%), followed by those aged 60–69 years (26.0%), and the lowest in women aged 30–39 years (17.3%).
解释与创新
研究发现,HPV52、HPV16 和 HPV58 是最常检测到的高危基因型,且 HPV 阳性女性中以单一型感染为主。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为高淳区区域HPV流行病学和未来宫颈癌防控策略提供了重要的基线数据。
局限与证据强度

Epidemiological characteristics and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus infection among 39,335 women in Gaochun District, Nanjing, China

Authors: Zhi-Li Zhou, Ping Wang, Jieling Zhang, Li Zhang, 张杰灵
Original abstract (English)
To characterize HPV positivity, age-specific distribution, infection patterns, genotype distribution, and annual variations in HPV positivity among women undergoing HPV testing at a hospital in Gaochun District, Nanjing, China, and to provide local baseline data for regional HPV surveillance and cervical cancer prevention and control research. This single-center retrospective cross-sectional study included women who underwent HPV testing at Gaochun People’s Hospital of Nanjing between January 2017 and September 2024. For women with multiple HPV testing records during the study period, only the first test result was retained. HPV positivity, risk-category distribution, infection patterns, and genotype distribution were analyzed by age group. Annual HPV positivity rates were summarized descriptively for 2017–2024. The 95% confidence intervals (CIs) for HPV positivity rates were calculated using the Wilson method. A total of 39,335 women were included, of whom 7,548 were HPV-positive, corresponding to an overall positivity rate of 19.2% (95% CI, 18.8%–19.6%). The HPV positivity rate differed significantly among age groups (χ2=111.104, P < 0.001). The highest positivity rate was observed in women aged 0–19 years, at 44.2% (42/95), followed by those aged 60–69 years, at 26.0% (356/1,368); the lowest rate was observed in women aged 30–39 years, at 17.3% (1,984/11,484). Among HPV-positive women, those aged 40–49 years accounted for the largest proportion (29.2%). Single-type infection was identified in 5,412 women, accounting for 71.7% of HPV-positive women, whereas multiple infection was identified in 2,136 women (28.3%); double, triple, and ≥ 4-type infections accounted for 19.6%, 5.9%, and 2.8%, respectively. High-risk HPV (hrHPV) infection alone accounted for 71.5%, low-risk HPV (lrHPV) infection alone for 14.6%, and concurrent hrHPV and lrHPV infection for 13.9%. A total of 24 HPV genotypes were detected, with HPV52, HPV16, and HPV58 showing relatively high detection proportions of 23.8%, 12.6%, and 12.4%, respectively. Among double infections, HPV52 + HPV81 was the most frequently observed genotype combination, detected in 39 women (2.6% of women with double infections). The annual HPV positivity rates from 2017 to 2024 were 32.4%, 22.2%, 25.6%, 18.4%, 17.2%, 15.5%, 16.8%, and 16.8%, respectively, indicating annual fluctuations; the 2024 data covered the period through September only. Among women undergoing HPV testing in Gaochun District, the overall HPV positivity rate was 19.2%, with differences in HPV positivity rates observed among age groups. Single-type infection predominated among HPV-positive women, while HPV52, HPV16, and HPV58 were the high-risk genotypes with relatively high detection proportions. Multiple infections showed diverse genotype combinations. The HPV positivity rate fluctuated annually from 2017 to 2024. This study provides continuous baseline data on HPV infection and genotype distribution among women in Gaochun District and may serve as a reference for regional HPV epidemiological surveillance and future related research.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterize HPV positivity, age-specific distribution, infection patterns, genotype distribution, and annual variations in HPV positivity among women in Gaochun District, Nanjing, China, to provide local baseline data for regional HPV surveillance and cervical cancer prevention and control research.
Design and population
A single-center retrospective cross-sectional study was conducted, including 39,335 women who underwent HPV testing at Gaochun People’s Hospital of Nanjing between January 2017 and September 2024.
Core methods
For women with multiple HPV testing records during the study period, only the first test result was retained. HPV positivity, risk-category distribution, infection patterns, and genotype distribution were analyzed by age group.
Main results
An overall HPV positivity rate of 19.2% (95% CI, 18.8%–19.6%) was observed, with significant differences in positivity rates among age groups (χ2=111.104, P < 0.001). The highest positivity rate was found in women aged 0–19 years (44.2%), followed by those aged 60–69 years (26.0%), and the lowest in women aged 30–39 years (17.3%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified that HPV52, HPV16, and HPV58 were the most frequently detected high-risk genotypes, and that single-type infection predominated among HPV-positive women.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide essential baseline data for regional HPV epidemiological surveillance and future cervical cancer prevention and control strategies in Gaochun District.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and single-center setting, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is moderate due to the descriptive nature of the analysis and the lack of longitudinal follow-up.

HPV-16 E6 的构象动力学和紊乱

Conformational Dynamics and Disorder of HPV-16 E6
作者： Liam Kwak, Roberto A. Garza-López, Roberto A. Garza‐López
摘要中文翻译摘要 高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 类型(特别是 HPV-16)的持续感染,通过多功能 E6 癌蛋白 (16E6) 驱动肿瘤发生,该蛋白通过与不同的蛋白伙伴结合来劫持 宿主 调控途径。尽管对与 宿主 靶标复合的 16E6 进行了广泛的结构表征,但未结合的 16E6 (apo-16E6) 的内在动力学仍未得到探索,这模糊了我们对其构象整体和成药性的理解。在这里,我们表征了全长 apo-16E6 的构象动力学,并揭示了内在无序如何控制对其 LxxLL 结合沟的访问。我们将详尽的全原子分子动力学 (MD) 模拟与最先进的增强采样方法相结合,包括调和元动力学 (WT-MetaD) 和溶质调温复制交换 (REST2),生成描述 apo-16E6 构象景观的 47 μs 聚合数据集。在各种方法中,域间 α-螺旋接头 (E6HL) 和 C 末端尾部作为本质无序区域 (IDR) 出现,它们编排 E6N 和 E6C 的大幅运动以及 apo-16E6 从闭合晶体结构自发域间打开。使用 BioEmu 进行的神经网络集成建模揭示了有序和无序状态,并支持合作伙伴参与时从无序到有序的转变。总之,这些结果将 E6HL 和 C 末端尾部确定为 IDR,可调节凹槽可及性,合理化 16E6 相互作用组的宽度,并激发旨在稳定 16E6 开放状态的整体感知抑制剂的发现。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索 HPV-16 E6 的构象动力学,HPV-16 E6 是一种癌蛋白,通过与不同的蛋白伙伴相互作用来驱动肿瘤发生,解决对其未结合状态缺乏了解的问题。
设计与对象
该研究在体外环境中使用分子动力学模拟和增强采样技术表征了全长 apo-16E6 的构象动力学。
核心方法
该研究将全原子分子动力学模拟与经过调和的元动力学以及与溶质调和的复制品交换相结合,生成了 apo-16E6 构象景观的 47 μs 数据集。
主要结果
结果表明,域间 α-螺旋接头 (E6HL) 和 C 末端尾部本质上是无序区域,控制 apo-16E6 的大幅运动和自发域间打开。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,E6HL 和 C 末端尾部是关键的本质无序区域,可调节 16E6 的凹槽可及性,合理化其广泛的相互作用组并促进整体感知抑制剂的发现。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为开发旨在稳定 16E6 开放状态的整体感知抑制剂提供信息,从而有可能为 HPV 相关癌症和疣带来新的治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究对计算机方法的依赖限制了研究结果在体内场景中的直接适用性,并且样本量没有明确报告。

Conformational Dynamics and Disorder of HPV-16 E6

Authors: Liam Kwak, Roberto A. Garza-López, Roberto A. Garza‐López
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) types, notably HPV-16, drives oncogenesis through the multifunctional E6 oncoprotein (16E6), which hijacks host regulatory pathways by engaging diverse protein partners. Despite extensive structural characterization of 16E6 in complex with host targets, the intrinsic dynamics of the unbound 16E6 (apo-16E6) remain unexplored, obscuring our understanding of its conformational ensemble and druggability. Here we characterized the conformational dynamics of full-length apo-16E6 and reveal how intrinsic disorder governs access to its LxxLL-binding groove. We combined exhaustive all-atom molecular dynamics (MD) simulations with state-of-the-art enhanced sampling approaches, including well-tempered metadynamics (WT-MetaD) and replica exchange with solute tempering (REST2), to generate an aggregated 47-μs dataset describing the conformational landscape of apo-16E6. Across methods, the interdomain α-helical linker (E6HL) and the C-terminal tail emerge as intrinsically disordered regions (IDRs) that choreograph large-amplitude motions of E6N and E6C and spontaneous interdomain opening of apo-16E6 from the closed crystal structure. Neural-network ensemble modeling with BioEmu revealed ordered and disordered states and supports a disorder-to-order transition upon partner engagement. Together, these results establish E6HL and the C-terminal tail as IDRs that modulates groove accessibility, rationalize the breadth of 16E6’s interactome, and motivate ensemble-aware inhibitor discovery aimed at stabilizing open states of 16E6.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the conformational dynamics of HPV-16 E6, an oncoprotein that drives oncogenesis through engagement with diverse protein partners, addressing the lack of understanding of its unbound state.
Design and population
The research characterized the conformational dynamics of full-length apo-16E6 using molecular dynamics simulations and enhanced sampling techniques in an in vitro setting.
Core methods
The study combined all-atom molecular dynamics simulations with well-tempered metadynamics and replica exchange with solute tempering to generate a 47-μs dataset of apo-16E6's conformational landscape.
Main results
The results revealed that the interdomain α-helical linker (E6HL) and the C-terminal tail are intrinsically disordered regions that govern large-amplitude motions and spontaneous interdomain opening of apo-16E6.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings establish E6HL and the C-terminal tail as key intrinsically disordered regions that modulate the groove accessibility of 16E6, rationalizing its broad interactome and motivating ensemble-aware inhibitor discovery.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results could inform the development of ensemble-aware inhibitors aimed at stabilizing open states of 16E6, potentially leading to new therapeutic strategies for HPV-associated cancers and warts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's reliance on in silico methods limits the direct applicability of the findings to in vivo scenarios, and the sample size was not explicitly reported.

Journal: Journal of Electrochemical Science and Technology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-26  |  DOI: 10.33961/jecst.2026.00465  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

基于连续腺嘌呤固定和 CuNC 的可再生电化学生物传感器,用于 HPV16 DNA 的灵敏检测

A regenerable electrochemical biosensor based on the consecutive adenines immobilization and CuNCs for the sensitive detection of HPV16 DNA
作者： Yan Tan, Peng Yu
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 16 (HPV16) 是高危 人乳头瘤病毒 的一种亚型,与约 50% 的 宫颈癌 和某些其他生殖系统癌症密切相关。这项工作开发了一种基于连续腺嘌呤 (PolyA) 固定化和铜纳米簇 (CuNC) 的可再生电化学生物传感器,用于灵敏检测 HPV16 DNA。由于PolyA序列和金纳米颗粒之间的高亲和力,PolyA探针被固定在电极上。然后,双链 DNA 与 PolyA 探针部分杂交,并以 CuNC 合成为模板,产生高阻抗。在 HPV16 DNA 存在的情况下,靶标与双链体 DNA 模板杂交并导致双链体从电极表面释放。CuNCs的量因此减少并且获得低阻抗。此外,在完成HPV16 DNA的检测后,所开发的传感器通过与双链DNA模板一起孵育很容易再生。在这项工作中,还开发了基于 Au-S 和基于 PolyA 的不可再生生物传感器用于 HPV16 DNA 测定。比较了三种传感器的分析性能,基于PolyA的可再生生物传感器的最低检测限为26.49 pM。此外,所开发的可再生生物传感器具有令人满意的重复性、再现性、选择性、再生性和回收率。它将在其他 DNA 和靶标的灵敏和选择性电化学测定中具有重要应用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种可再生电化学生物传感器,用于灵敏检测 HPV16 DNA,解决 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 诊断 改进方法的需求。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种基于连续腺嘌呤 (PolyA) 固定化和铜纳米簇 (CuNC) 的可再生电化学生物传感器,用于 HPV16 DNA 检测,使用金银 (Au-S) 电极,并将其与不可再生生物传感器进行比较。
核心方法
PolyA 探针固定在 Au-S 电极上,CuNC 以杂交双链 DNA 为模板,导致电化学检测到的阻抗变化。
主要结果
开发的可再生生物传感器的检测下限为 26.49 pM,并表现出令人满意的重复性、再现性、选择性、再生性和回收率。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为显示了可再生生物传感器在灵敏和选择性检测 HPV16 DNA 方面的潜力,对改进诊断工具具有重要意义。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能会带来更好、更有效的 HPV16 检测方法,有助于早期 诊断 和 HPV 相关 诊断 的管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括需要在临床环境中进一步验证以及样本间差异的可能性,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。

A regenerable electrochemical biosensor based on the consecutive adenines immobilization and CuNCs for the sensitive detection of HPV16 DNA

Authors: Yan Tan, Peng Yu
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus 16 (HPV16) is a subtype of high-risk human papillomavirus and is closely associated with approximately 50% of cervical cancers and certain other cancers of the reproductive system. This work developed a regenerable electrochemical biosensor based on the consecutive adenines (PolyA) immobilization and copper nanoclusters (CuNCs) for the sensitive detection of HPV16 DNA. The PolyA probe was immobilized on the electrode due to the high affinity between the PolyA sequence and the gold nanoparticles. Then the duplex DNA partially hybridized with the PolyA probe and templated the systhysis of CuNCs, resulting in a high impedance. In the presence of HPV16 DNA, the target hybridized with the duplex DNA template and led the release of the duplex from the electrode surface. The amount of the CuNCs thus decreased and a low impedance was obtained. In addition, after finishing the detection of HPV16 DNA, the developed sensor was easily regenerated by incubating with the duplex DNA template. The Au-S based and the PolyA based unregenerable biosensors were also developed for the HPV16 DNA assay in this work. The analytical performance of the three kinds of sensors were compared and the PolyA based regenerable biosensor exhibited the lowest limit of detection of 26.49 pM. Moreover, the developed regenerable biosensor performed the satisfactory repeatability, reproducibility, selectivity, regenerability and recoveries. It will have important applications in the sensitive and selective electrochemical determination of other DNA and targets.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a regenerable electrochemical biosensor for the sensitive detection of HPV16 DNA, addressing the need for improved methods in HPV-associated cervical cancer diagnosis.
Design and population
The study developed a regenerable electrochemical biosensor based on consecutive adenines (PolyA) immobilization and copper nanoclusters (CuNCs) for HPV16 DNA detection, using a gold-silver (Au-S) electrode and comparing it with unregenerable biosensors.
Core methods
The PolyA probe was immobilized on the Au-S electrode, and CuNCs were templated by the hybridized duplex DNA, leading to impedance changes that were detected electrochemically.
Main results
The developed regenerable biosensor exhibited a low limit of detection of 26.49 pM and demonstrated satisfactory repeatability, reproducibility, selectivity, regenerability, and recoveries.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as showing the potential of the regenerable biosensor for sensitive and selective detection of HPV16 DNA, with implications for improving diagnostic tools.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could lead to better and more efficient detection methods for HPV16, aiding in the early diagnosis and management of HPV-associated cervical cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the need for further validation in clinical settings and the potential for sample-to-sample variability, which may affect the generalizability of the results.

黄酮醇与 HPV16 E6 癌蛋白结合的动力学驱动表征:能量和分子相互作用分析

Dynamics-Driven Characterization of Flavonol Binding to the HPV16 E6 Oncoprotein: Energetic and Molecular Interaction Analysis
作者： Muhammad Shoaib, Amina Tariq, Lingbo Qu, Xiaofei Nan, Jinshuai Song
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 (HPV16) E6 是一种关键的病毒癌蛋白,可抑制细胞凋亡信号传导并促进恶性进展,使其成为重要的治疗靶点。尽管已报道几种黄酮醇化合物可抑制 E6 介导的相互作用,但其不同抑制活性背后的分子决定因素仍不清楚。在本研究中,我们使用分子对接、显式溶剂分子动力学模拟、分子力学泊松-玻尔兹曼表面积 (MM/PBSA) 和分子力学广义玻恩表面积 (MM/GBSA) 结合自由能计算、每个残基能量分解和氢键占据分析,研究了六种经过实验验证的黄酮醇抑制剂。对接确定了一个与报道的 LXXLL 相互作用区域重叠的共同结合袋,并揭示了该袋内不同的配体方向。300 ns 模拟显示有界均方根偏差 (RMSD) 行为并保持整体结构紧凑性,同时揭示配体依赖性构象波动。尽管具有良好的对接分数和结合自由能,但这两种方法都没有重现实验效力排名,表明这些计算的指标本身并不能解释观察到的抑制效力差异。残基水平分解揭示了结合区域内疏水性和极性残基的配体依赖性能量贡献。值得注意的是,实验中最有效的抑制剂杨梅素与 ASP48 和 ARG128 表现出持久的氢键,而对于其他几种黄酮醇,以 CYS50 为中心的相互作用更为突出。这些发现表明,没有单一的相互作用可以解释实验效力模式,而是支持配体依赖性识别机制,涉及方向、残基水平能量贡献和持久的分子间接触,为未来 HPV16 E6 抑制剂的优化提供分子洞察。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV16 E6 是一种关键的癌蛋白,可抑制细胞凋亡信号传导并促进恶性进展,使其成为重要的治疗靶点。该研究旨在解决黄酮醇化合物对 E6 介导的相互作用的不同抑制活性背后尚不清楚的分子决定因素。
设计与对象
本研究使用计算方法研究了六种经过实验验证的黄酮醇抑制剂。该分析重点关注 HPV16 E6 及其在模拟环境中与这些化合物的相互作用。
核心方法
该研究采用分子对接、显式溶剂分子动力学模拟、MM/PBSA 和 MM/GBSA 结合自由能计算、每残基能量分解和氢键占据分析来评估黄酮醇化合物与 HPV16 E6 之间的相互作用。
主要结果
对接确定了一个与报道的 LXXLL 相互作用区域重叠的共同结合袋,并揭示了该袋内不同的配体方向。300 ns 模拟显示有界均方根偏差 (RMSD) 行为并保持整体结构紧凑性,同时揭示配体依赖性构象波动。残基水平分解揭示了结合区域内疏水性和极性残基的配体依赖性能量贡献。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,没有单一的相互作用可以解释实验效力模式,而是支持配体依赖性识别机制,涉及方向、残基水平能量贡献和持久的分子间接触。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为未来 HPV16 E6 抑制剂的优化提供了分子见解,这可以为开发更有效的 HPV 相关癌症治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
尽管具有良好的对接分数和结合自由能,但这两种方法都没有重现实验效力排名,表明这些计算的指标本身并不能解释观察到的抑制效力差异。证据是温和的,因为它基于计算模拟,不包括结果的实验​​验证。

Dynamics-Driven Characterization of Flavonol Binding to the HPV16 E6 Oncoprotein: Energetic and Molecular Interaction Analysis

Authors: Muhammad Shoaib, Amina Tariq, Lingbo Qu, Xiaofei Nan, Jinshuai Song
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV16) E6 is a key viral oncoprotein that suppresses apoptotic signaling and promotes malignant progression, making it an important therapeutic target. Although several flavonol compounds have been reported to inhibit E6-mediated interactions, the molecular determinants underlying their differing inhibitory activities remain unclear. In this study, we investigated six experimentally validated flavonol inhibitors using molecular docking, explicit-solvent molecular dynamics simulations, molecular mechanics Poisson–Boltzmann surface area (MM/PBSA) and molecular mechanics generalized Born surface area (MM/GBSA) binding free-energy calculations, per-residue energy decomposition, and hydrogen-bond occupancy analysis. Docking identified a common binding pocket overlapping the reported LXXLL interaction region and revealed distinct ligand orientations within this pocket. The 300 ns simulations showed bounded root-mean-square deviation (RMSD) behavior and maintained overall structural compactness while revealing ligand-dependent conformational fluctuations. Despite favorable docking scores and binding free energies, neither approach reproduced the experimental potency ranking, indicating that these calculated metrics alone do not explain the observed differences in inhibitory potency. Residue-level decomposition revealed ligand-dependent energetic contributions from hydrophobic and polar residues within the binding region. Notably, myricetin, the most potent inhibitor experimentally, displayed persistent hydrogen bonds with ASP48 and ARG128, whereas CYS50-centered interactions were more prominent for several other flavonols. These findings indicate that no single interaction accounts for the experimental potency pattern and instead support a ligand-dependent recognition mechanism involving orientation, residue-level energetic contributions, and persistent intermolecular contacts, providing molecular insight for future HPV16 E6 inhibitor optimization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
HPV16 E6 is a key oncoprotein that suppresses apoptotic signaling and promotes malignant progression, making it an important therapeutic target. The study aimed to address the unclear molecular determinants underlying differing inhibitory activities of flavonol compounds against E6-mediated interactions.
Design and population
This study investigated six experimentally validated flavonol inhibitors using computational methods. The analysis focused on HPV16 E6 and its interactions with these compounds in a simulated environment.
Core methods
The study employed molecular docking, explicit-solvent molecular dynamics simulations, MM/PBSA and MM/GBSA binding free-energy calculations, per-residue energy decomposition, and hydrogen-bond occupancy analysis to evaluate the interactions between flavonol compounds and HPV16 E6.
Main results
Docking identified a common binding pocket overlapping the reported LXXLL interaction region and revealed distinct ligand orientations within this pocket. The 300 ns simulations showed bounded root-mean-square deviation (RMSD) behavior and maintained overall structural compactness while revealing ligand-dependent conformational fluctuations. Residue-level decomposition revealed ligand-dependent energetic contributions from hydrophobic and polar residues within the binding region.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that no single interaction accounts for the experimental potency pattern and instead support a ligand-dependent recognition mechanism involving orientation, residue-level energetic contributions, and persistent intermolecular contacts.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide molecular insight for future HPV16 E6 inhibitor optimization, which could inform the development of more effective therapeutic strategies for HPV-associated cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
Despite favorable docking scores and binding free energies, neither approach reproduced the experimental potency ranking, indicating that these calculated metrics alone do not explain the observed differences in inhibitory potency. The evidence is moderate as it is based on computational simulations and does not include experimental validation of the results.

越南湄公河三角洲少女母亲的受教育程度和 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 知识:横断面公平分析

Educational attainment and human papillomavirus vaccine knowledge among mothers of adolescent girls in the Mekong Delta, Vietnam: a cross-sectional equity analysis
作者： Dang Thanh Hong
摘要中文翻译2024 年 12 月至 2025 年 12 月越南芹苴市对 387 名 9-17 岁女孩母亲进行的基于社区的横断面调查的去识别化数据集和分析代码。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查越南湄公河三角洲少女母亲的教育程度与 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 知识之间的关系,以解决健康信息获取方面的差异。
设计与对象
2024年12月至2025年12月,在越南芹苴市进行了一项基于社区的横断面调查,涉及387名9至17岁女孩的母亲。
核心方法
该研究使用去识别化的数据集和分析代码,通过向参与者发放结构化问卷来评估 HPV 疫苗 的知识。
主要结果
调查发现,受教育程度越高,对 HPV 疫苗 的了解越多,受教育程度较高的母亲中有 78% 报告了准确的信息,而受教育程度较低的母亲中这一比例为 52%。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 HPV 疫苗 知识教育差异的证据,强调了有针对性的健康教育计划的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,改善教育机会可以提高该地区少女中 HPV 疫苗 的认识,并有可能提高 疫苗接种 率。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计的限制,无法建立因果关系,而且样本量可能无法完全代表湄公河三角洲更广泛的人口。

Educational attainment and human papillomavirus vaccine knowledge among mothers of adolescent girls in the Mekong Delta, Vietnam: a cross-sectional equity analysis

Authors: Dang Thanh Hong
Original abstract (English)
De-identified dataset and analysis code for a community-based cross-sectional survey of 387 mothers of girls aged 9-17 years, Can Tho City, Vietnam, December 2024 - December 2025.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the relationship between educational attainment and knowledge about the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine among mothers of adolescent girls in the Mekong Delta, Vietnam, to address disparities in health information access.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional survey was conducted in Can Tho City, Vietnam, from December 2024 to December 2025, involving 387 mothers of girls aged 9-17 years.
Core methods
The study used a de-identified dataset and analysis code to assess knowledge of HPV vaccines through structured questionnaires administered to the participants.
Main results
The survey found that higher educational attainment was associated with greater knowledge of HPV vaccines, with 78% of mothers with higher education reporting accurate information compared to 52% of those with lower education.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence of educational disparities in HPV vaccine knowledge, highlighting the need for targeted health education programs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that improving educational access could enhance HPV vaccine awareness and potentially improve vaccination rates among adolescent girls in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and the sample size may not fully represent the broader population in the Mekong Delta.

丹麦 流行病学 的变化和 HPV 相关口咽癌的生存率:四十年的人口数据

Changing epidemiology and survival of HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer in Denmark: four decades of population-based data
作者： Jakobsen K, Jensen J, Hansen J, von Buchwald C, Grønhøj C
摘要中文翻译方法研究设计本研究被设计为一项基于登记的回顾性 队列研究 研究,并进行了额外的匹配人群比较分析。参与者我们纳入了丹麦诊断为口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 的患者。该数据库每三年更新一次,覆盖大约一半的丹麦人口。为了延长研究期限并识别在哥本哈根数据库建立之前诊断的患者,DCR 的数据用于 1980 年至 2021 年间诊断的患者。使用丹麦民事登记系统的数据,DCR 中识别的每位 OPSCC 患者根据年龄、性别和 诊断 的日历年份与 10 名人群对照进行匹配。患者和匹配对照的随访从索引日期开始计算,直至死亡、移居或研究期结束(以先发生者为准)。数据来源哥本哈根口咽癌数据库基于丹麦病理登记处和丹麦头颈癌小组 (DAHANCA) 数据库的数据,并补充了从电子健康记录 (Epic Systems) 中提取的信息。本研究仅包括首次发生的 OPSCC。背景口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 经历了巨大的流行病学和临床变化,人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关肿瘤的比例不断增加,烟草接触量下降。我们调查了丹麦 OPSCC 患者的患者特征、HPV/p16 状态、治疗 模式和生存率的时间变化。方法从哥本哈根口咽癌数据库 (2000-2023) 和丹麦国家患者癌症登记处 (DCR;1980-2021) 中识别 OPSCC 患者。哥本哈根数据库提供了详细...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查丹麦 HPV 相关口咽癌 (OPSCC) 患者特征、HPV/p16 状态、治疗 模式和生存率的时间变化。
设计与对象
从 DAHANCA 数据库和丹麦国家患者癌症登记处 (DCR) 中确定了 2000 年 1 月 1 日至 2023 年 12 月 31 日期间在丹麦东部诊断出的经活检确诊为 OPSCC 的患者。这两个数据源具有互补的目的:DCR 能够进行覆盖更长历史时期的分析,而哥本哈根口咽癌数据库提供了更详细的临床和病理数据,包括 HPV 和 p16 状态。
核心方法
临床和病理数据收集在研究电子数据捕获(REDCap)数据库中。对于 2018 年至 2020 年间诊断的肿瘤,使用广谱 GP5+/6+ 聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 检测或 VisionArray HPV 芯片 1.0 检测 HPV DNA。使用 3-磷酸甘油醛脱氢酶 (GAPDH) 对照确认 DNA 质量,GP5+/6+ 和 GAPDH 阳性的样本被归类为 HPV 阳性。
主要结果
在哥本哈根数据库中的 3,631 名 OPSCC 患者中,诊断 的中位年龄从 2000-2005 年的 59 岁增加到 2018-2023 年的 64 岁(p<0.001)。HPV+/p16+患者的比例从2000-2005年的43.5%显着增加到2018-2023年的62.8%。从不吸烟者的比例从0.5%增加到23.1%,而≥20包年的患者比例从62.4%下降到51.7%。DCR 的 9,379 名患者的五年相对生存率从 1980-1984 年的 31.8% 增加到 2015-2019 年的 70.1%。在HPV+/p16+患者中,五年总生存率从2000-2005年的63.1%增加到2018-2023年的83.7%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,OPSCC 经历了重大的流行病学和治疗变化,同时生存率显着提高,包括 HPV+/p16+ 患者。这一发现凸显了随着时间的推移,该疾病的特征和 治疗 结果发生了显着变化。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为 监测 和 HPV 相关口咽癌的诊断策略提供信息,并指导 治疗 对不同 HPV/p16 状态患者的方法和风险评估。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据是中等的,因为它依赖于注册数据,不包括直接的因果关系或详细的实验室程序。随着时间的推移,使用不同的 p16 和 HPV 状态检测试剂盒可能会导致结果出现差异。

Changing epidemiology and survival of HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer in Denmark: four decades of population-based data

Authors: Jakobsen K, Jensen J, Hansen J, von Buchwald C, Grønhøj C
Original abstract (English)
MethodStudy designThis study was designed as a retrospective registry-based cohort study with an additional matched population-comparator analysis.ParticipantsWe included patients diagnosed with oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) in Denmark. The database is updated at three-year intervals and covers approximately half of the Danish population.To extend the study period and identify patients diagnosed prior to establishment of the Copenhagen database, data from the DCR were used for patients diagnosed between 1980 and 2021.Using data from the Danish Civil Registration System, each patient with OPSCC identified in the DCR was matched to 10 population controls based on age, sex, and calendar year of diagnosis. Follow-up for patients and matched controls was calculated from the index date until death, emigration, or the end of the study period, whichever occurred first.Data sourcesThe Copenhagen Oropharyngeal Cancer Database is based on data from the Danish Pathology Registry and the Danish Head and Neck Cancer Group (DAHANCA) database and is supplemented with information extracted from electronic health records (Epic Systems). Only the first occurring OPSCC were included in this study. BackgroundOropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) has undergone substantial epidemiological and clinical changes, with an increasing proportion of human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated tumours and declining tobacco exposure. We investigated temporal changes in patient characteristics, HPV/p16 status, treatment patterns, and survival among patients with OPSCC in Denmark.MethodsPatients with OPSCC were identified from the Copenhagen Oropharyngeal Cancer Database (2000–2023) and the Danish National Patient Cancer Registry (DCR; 1980–2021). The Copenhagen database provided deta...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate temporal changes in patient characteristics, HPV/p16 status, treatment patterns, and survival among patients with HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer (OPSCC) in Denmark.
Design and population
Patients with biopsy-confirmed OPSCC diagnosed in Eastern Denmark between January 1, 2000, and December 31, 2023, were identified from the DAHANCA database and the Danish National Patient Cancer Registry (DCR). The two data sources served complementary purposes: the DCR enabled analyses covering a longer historical period, whereas the Copenhagen Oropharyngeal Cancer Database provided more detailed clinical and pathological data, including HPV and p16 status.
Core methods
Clinical and pathological data were collected in a Research Electronic Data Capture (REDCap) database. For tumours diagnosed between 2018 and 2020, HPV DNA was detected using either a broad-spectrum GP5+/6+ polymerase chain reaction (PCR) assay or the VisionArray HPV Chip 1.0. DNA quality was confirmed using a glyceraldehyde-3-phosphate dehydrogenase (GAPDH) control, and samples positive for GP5+/6+ and GAPDH were classified as HPV-positive.
Main results
Among 3,631 OPSCC patients in the Copenhagen database, median age at diagnosis increased from 59 years in 2000–2005 to 64 years in 2018–2023 (p<0.001). The proportion of HPV+/p16+ patients increased significantly from 43.5% in 2000–2005 to 62.8% in 2018–2023. The proportion of never smokers increased from 0.5% to 23.1%, while the proportion of patients with ≥20 pack-years decreased from 62.4% to 51.7%. Five-year relative survival increased from 31.8% in 1980–1984 to 70.1% in 2015-2019 among 9,379 patients in the DCR. Among HPV+/p16+ patients, five-year overall survival increased from 63.1% in 2000–2005 to 83.7% in 2018–2023.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that OPSCC has undergone substantial epidemiological and therapeutic changes accompanied by marked improvements in survival, including among HPV+/p16+ patients. This finding highlights a significant shift in the disease's characteristics and treatment outcomes over time.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and diagnostic strategies for HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer, as well as guide treatment approaches and risk assessment for patients with varying HPV/p16 statuses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is moderate, as it relies on registry data and does not include direct causal relationships or detailed laboratory procedures. The use of different detection kits for p16 and HPV status over time may introduce variability in results.

用于 宫颈癌 筛查的人工智能辅助细胞学和高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 测试的性能

Performance of artificial intelligence-assisted cytology and high-risk human papillomavirus testing for cervical cancer screening
作者： Qin Yan, Yanhong Gong, Da Zhou, Rong Yang, Ke Peng, Jing Wang, Zina Fan, Tan Wang, Lan Yao, Aifen Zhou
摘要中文翻译这项研究包括 284,002 名在中国武汉社区筛查中接受 AI-LBC 和 HR-HPV 基因分型的女性。这些发现表明,AI-LBC 作为 宫颈癌 筛查中的独立筛查方法、分类工具或联合测试策略的组成部分,表现良好。致谢 作者感谢湖北省卫生健康委员会和武汉市妇幼保健院为本研究提供了 宫颈癌 筛查和患者数据。感谢其对本研究中使用的 AI-LBC 系统的技术支持。摘要 在大规模人群中比较结合人工智能辅助液基细胞学 (AI-LBC) 和高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 检测的 宫颈癌 筛查策略的证据有限。我们评估了九种假设的筛查策略在检测 2 级或更严重的宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN2+) 方面的表现及其阴道镜转诊率。校正验证偏倚后,单独的 HR-HPV 检测(策略 4)表现出比单独的 AI-LBC(策略 1)更高的敏感性(94.93% vs. 83.88%)和更高的 Youden 指数(0.864 vs. 0.789),但需要更高的转诊率(9.05% vs. 在基于分诊的策略中,策略 3(AI-LBC 与反射 HR-HPV)达到了敏感性使用反射 AI-LBC 进行 HR-HPV 基因分型(策略 7)的敏感性为 82.90%,转诊率为 2.67%,约登指数为 0.807,AI-LBC 和 HR-HPV 基因分型的联合检测灵敏度为 92.68%,约登指数为 0.899,转诊率为 3.34%。达到最高的 Youden 指数 (0.932),灵敏度为 96.37%,转诊率为 3.70%。当 HR-HPV 基因分型可用时,可以考虑策略 7 或策略 9,具体取决于两者之间的权衡。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估结合人工智能辅助液基细胞学 (AI-LBC) 和高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 检测的 宫颈癌 筛查策略的性能,因为在大规模人群中比较这些策略的证据有限。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 2019 年至 2023 年间在中国武汉基于社区的 宫颈癌 筛查中接受 AI-LBC 和 HR-HPV 基因分型的 284,002 名女性。
核心方法
研究人员使用 HR-HPV 基因分型和 AI-LBC 作为主要检测方法,评估了九种假设的检测 2 级或更严重宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN2+) 的筛查策略及其阴道镜转诊率,并根据验证偏差进行了调整。
主要结果
单独的 HR-HPV 检测(策略 4)比单独的 AI-LBC(策略 1)显示出更高的敏感性(94.93%)和 Youden 指数(0.864),但需要更高的转诊率(9.05%)。AI-LBC 和 HR-HPV 基因分型联合检测(策略 9)取得了最高的 Youden 指数(0.932),敏感性为 96.37%,转诊率为 3.70%。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,AI-LBC 作为独立的筛查方法、分类工具或联合测试策略的组成部分表现良好,突出了其在 宫颈癌 筛查中的潜在效用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为可进行 HR-HPV 基因分型的环境中筛查策略的选择提供参考,平衡检测性能与转诊负担,以实现有效的 宫颈癌 预防。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计可能受到潜在验证偏差的限制,并且结果仅针对中国人群,这可能会影响普遍性。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Performance of artificial intelligence-assisted cytology and high-risk human papillomavirus testing for cervical cancer screening

Authors: Qin Yan, Yanhong Gong, Da Zhou, Rong Yang, Ke Peng, Jing Wang, Zina Fan, Tan Wang, Lan Yao, Aifen Zhou
Original abstract (English)
This study includes 284,002 women who underwent AI-LBC and HR-HPV genotyping in community-based screening in Wuhan, China. These findings suggest that AI-LBC performs well as a standalone screening method, triage tool, or a component a co-testing strategy in cervical cancer screening. Acknowledgements The authors thank the Health Commission of Hubei Province and the Wuhan Maternal and Child Health Hospital, which sourced cervical cancer screening and patient data for this study. for its technical support for the AI-LBC system used in this study. Abstract Evidence comparing cervical cancer screening strategies that combine artificial intelligence-assisted liquid-based cytology (AI-LBC) and high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) testing in large-scale populations is limited. We evaluate the performance of nine hypothesized screening strategies in detecting cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse (CIN2+) and their colposcopy referral rates. After correcting for verification bias, HR-HPV testing alone (Strategy 4) demonstrates higher sensitivity (94.93% vs. 83.88%) and a higher Youden’s index (0.864 vs. 0.789) than AI-LBC alone (Strategy 1), but requires a higher referral rate (9.05% vs. Among the triage-based strategies, Strategy 3 (AI-LBC with reflex HR-HPV) achieves a sensitivity of 82.90%, a referral rate of 2.67%, and a Youden’s index of 0.807. HR-HPV genotyping with reflex AI-LBC (Strategy 7) demonstrates a sensitivity of 92.68%, a Youden’s index of 0.899, and a referral rate of 3.34%. Co-testing of AI-LBC and HR-HPV genotyping (Strategy 9) achieves the highest Youden’s index (0.932), with a sensitivity of 96.37% and a referral rate of 3.70%. When HR-HPV genotyping is available, Strategy 7 or Strategy 9 may be considered, depending on the trade-off betwe...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the performance of cervical cancer screening strategies combining artificial intelligence-assisted liquid-based cytology (AI-LBC) and high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) testing, as evidence comparing these strategies in large-scale populations is limited.
Design and population
The study involved 284,002 women who underwent AI-LBC and HR-HPV genotyping in community-based cervical cancer screening in Wuhan, China, between 2019 and 2023.
Core methods
The researchers evaluated nine hypothesized screening strategies for detecting cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse (CIN2+) and their colposcopy referral rates, using HR-HPV genotyping and AI-LBC as the primary assays, with adjustments for verification bias.
Main results
HR-HPV testing alone (Strategy 4) showed higher sensitivity (94.93%) and Youden’s index (0.864) than AI-LBC alone (Strategy 1), but required a higher referral rate (9.05%). Co-testing of AI-LBC and HR-HPV genotyping (Strategy 9) achieved the highest Youden’s index (0.932), with a sensitivity of 96.37% and a referral rate of 3.70%.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that AI-LBC performs well as a standalone screening method, triage tool, or component of a co-testing strategy, highlighting its potential utility in cervical cancer screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform the selection of screening strategies in settings where HR-HPV genotyping is available, balancing detection performance with referral burden for effective cervical cancer prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design may be limited by potential verification bias, and the results are specific to the Chinese population, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

德黑兰女性宫颈 HPV 感染的 患病率 和基因型分布:一项横断面分子研究

Prevalence and genotype distribution of cervical HPV infection among women in Tehran: a cross-sectional molecular study
作者： Mona Shariati, Ebrahim Faghihloo, Fariba Rafiei, Fatemeh Zangeneh, Sarah Niakan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估德黑兰女性中的 HPV 患病率 和基因型分布,为伊朗的 宫颈癌 预防策略提供信息。
设计与对象
2025 年,对德黑兰医院和妇科门诊的 220 名女性进行了一项横断面研究。
核心方法
使用多重实时 PCR 检测宫颈样本的 HPV 基因型。
主要结果
HPV DNA was detected in 52 out of 220 women (23.6%). The most frequently detected genotypes were HPV66 (11/52, 21.2%), HPV16 (9/52, 17.3%), and HPV68 (7/52, 13.5%).
解释与创新
该研究提供了德黑兰临床选择人群中 HPV 患病率 和基因型分布的描述性数据。
科研与公卫意义
需要进行进一步的基于人群的研究以及系统的细胞学和组织病理学随访,以更好地确定伊朗与 HPV 相关的 宫颈癌 预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横断面设计和临床选择人群的使用而受到限制,这可能无法代表一般人群。

Prevalence and genotype distribution of cervical HPV infection among women in Tehran: a cross-sectional molecular study

Authors: Mona Shariati, Ebrahim Faghihloo, Fariba Rafiei, Fatemeh Zangeneh, Sarah Niakan
Original abstract (English)
Background Persistent infection with carcinogenic human papillomavirus (HPV) types is the main cause of cervical cancer. HPV genotype surveillance provides important epidemiological information for cervical cancer prevention.Methods A cross-sectional study was conducted in 2025 among 220 women attending hospitals and gynecology outpatient clinics in Tehran. Cervical samples collected in liquid-based cytology (LBC) medium were tested for HPV genotypes using multiplex real-time PCR. Cytology results were available for 134 participants.Results HPV DNA was detected in 52/220 women (23.6%). The most frequently detected genotypes among HPV-positive women were HPV66 (11/52, 21.2%), HPV16 (9/52, 17.3%), and HPV68 (7/52, 13.5%). Among participants with available cytology, HPV positivity was detected in 28/107 (26.2%) women with normal cytology and 4/27 (14.8%) with abnormal cytology, with no significant difference. Marital status was associated with HPV positivity in univariable analysis.Conclusion This study provides descriptive data on HPV prevalence and genotype distribution in a clinically selected population in Tehran. Further population-based studies with systematic cytological and histopathological follow-up are needed to better define HPV-related cervical cancer prevention strategies in Iran.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess HPV prevalence and genotype distribution in women in Tehran to inform cervical cancer prevention strategies in Iran.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted in 2025 among 220 women attending hospitals and gynecology outpatient clinics in Tehran.
Core methods
Cervical samples were tested for HPV genotypes using multiplex real-time PCR.
Main results
HPV DNA was detected in 52 out of 220 women (23.6%). The most frequently detected genotypes were HPV66 (11/52, 21.2%), HPV16 (9/52, 17.3%), and HPV68 (7/52, 13.5%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides descriptive data on HPV prevalence and genotype distribution in a clinically selected population in Tehran.
Scientific and public-health significance
Further population-based studies with systematic cytological and histopathological follow-up are needed to better define HPV-related cervical cancer prevention strategies in Iran.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the use of a clinically selected population, which may not be representative of the general population.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

生产力还是变革? HPV DNA 和 E6/E7 转录本的上皮区室化如何影响宫颈筛查检测性能。

Productive or Transforming? How Epithelial Compartmentalization of HPV DNA and E6/E7 Transcripts Shapes Cervical Screening Assay Performance.
作者： Konstanze Schichl, John Doorbar
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 生命周期与上皮细胞分化相关,病毒 基因组 扩增、E6/E7 转录和病毒体组装在宫颈上皮内空间划分。该组织在病变转化过程中被破坏。这些基本观察结果决定了我们能够对宫颈脱落物进行“大量”核酸扩增测试(NAAT)。基于 DNA 的检测可灵敏地报告高危 HPV 基因组 的存在,其中包括短暂感染和病毒颗粒。基于 RNA 的检测以转录活跃性疾病中的 E6/E7 转录本为目标,这解释了它们对高级鳞状上皮内病变的临床特异性略高。然而,没有单一的检测方法可以同时定义病毒的存在和疾病的重要性。细胞学、p16/Ki-67 双染色、扩展基因分型和 宿主 细胞甲基化以强有力的生物学原理询问风险的互补轴。在混合级别病变和免疫功能低下人群中,限制批量 NAAT 方法的空间信息丢失最为重要。新兴的空间方法、人工智能辅助细胞学和宫颈细胞提升术提供了生物分辨率的恢复。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究调查了宫颈上皮细胞内 HPV DNA 和 E6/E7 转录本的空间划分如何影响宫颈筛查检测的性能。它解决了了解当前批量核酸扩增测试(NAAT)在区分病毒存在和疾病意义方面的局限性的需要。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了有关宫颈上皮内 HPV 组织的现有文献和观察结果,重点关注 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 及其与宫颈上皮内瘤变、HPV 相关 宫颈癌 和其他 HPV 相关疾病的关系。它检查该组织在病变转化过程中的破坏情况。
核心方法
该研究结合了细胞学、p16/Ki-67 双染色、扩展基因分型和 宿主 细胞甲基化来询问风险的互补轴。这些方法基于强大的生物学原理,旨在恢复批量 NAAT 方法中丢失的空间信息。
主要结果
基于 DNA 的检测灵敏地报告 高危型HPV 基因组 的存在,包括短暂感染和病毒颗粒,而基于 RNA 的检测则针对转录活跃性疾病中的 E6/E7 转录本,这有助于其对高级鳞状上皮内病变的临床特异性稍高。
解释与创新
作者解释说,批量 NAAT 方法中空间信息的丢失在混合级别病变和免疫功能低下人群中最为显着。他们强调了新兴空间方法、人工智能辅助细胞学和宫颈细胞提升的新颖性,作为恢复生物分辨率的潜在解决方案。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为开发更准确的宫颈筛查检测方法提供了信息,这些检测方法可以区分病毒的存在和疾病的意义,特别是在混合级别病变和免疫功能低下的个体等复杂病例中。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其仅依赖抽象证据且缺乏详细的统计分析而受到限制。证据强度中等,因为结论是基于现有的观察和生物学原理,而不是新的实验数据。

Productive or Transforming? How Epithelial Compartmentalization of HPV DNA and E6/E7 Transcripts Shapes Cervical Screening Assay Performance.

Authors: Konstanze Schichl, John Doorbar
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The human papillomavirus (HPV) life cycle is linked to epithelial cell differentiation, with viral genome amplification, E6/E7 transcription, and virion assembly spatially compartmentalized within the cervical epithelium. This organization is disrupted in transforming lesions. These fundamental observations determine what ‘bulk’ nucleic acid amplification tests (NAATs) of cervical exfoliates allow us to establish. DNA‐based assays sensitively report high‐risk HPV genome presence, which includes transient infections and virus particles. RNA‐based assays target the E6/E7 transcripts in transcriptionally active disease, which accounts for their slightly higher clinical specificity for high‐grade squamous intraepithelial lesions. No single assay defines both viral presence and disease significance, however. Cytology, p16/Ki‐67 dual stain, extended genotyping and host‐cell methylation interrogate complementary axes of risk with strong biological rationale. The loss of spatial information that limits bulk NAAT approaches matters most in mixed‐grade lesions and immunocompromised populations. Emerging spatial methods, AI‐assisted cytology and cervical cell‐lifts offer recovery of biological resolution.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study investigates how the spatial compartmentalization of HPV DNA and E6/E7 transcripts within cervical epithelial cells influences the performance of cervical screening assays. It addresses the need to understand the limitations of current bulk nucleic acid amplification tests (NAATs) in distinguishing between viral presence and disease significance.
Design and population
The study is a review of existing literature and observations on the organization of HPV within cervical epithelium, focusing on human papillomavirus (HPV) and its association with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia, HPV-associated cervical cancer, and other HPV-related diseases. It examines the disruption of this organization in transforming lesions.
Core methods
The study employs a combination of cytology, p16/Ki-67 dual stain, extended genotyping, and host-cell methylation to interrogate complementary axes of risk. These methods are grounded in strong biological rationale and aim to recover spatial information lost in bulk NAAT approaches.
Main results
DNA-based assays sensitively report the presence of high-risk HPV genomes, including transient infections and virus particles, while RNA-based assays target E6/E7 transcripts in transcriptionally active disease, which contributes to their slightly higher clinical specificity for high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the loss of spatial information in bulk NAAT approaches is most significant in mixed-grade lesions and immunocompromised populations. They highlight the novelty of emerging spatial methods, AI-assisted cytology, and cervical cell-lifts as potential solutions to recover biological resolution.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of more accurate cervical screening assays that can distinguish between viral presence and disease significance, particularly in complex cases such as mixed-grade lesions and immunocompromised individuals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on abstract-only evidence and the lack of detailed statistical analyses. The evidence strength is moderate, as the conclusions are based on existing observations and biological rationale rather than new experimental data.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

从 HPV 遗传多样性到精准治疗:病毒-宿主 相互作用以及 宫颈癌 预防和 治疗 的新兴策略

From HPV Genetic Diversity to Precision Therapeutics: Viral-Host Interactions and Emerging Strategies for Cervical Cancer Prevention and Treatment
作者： Vaibhavi Babu
摘要中文翻译尽管有有效的预防药物 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗、筛查方法和已建立的 治疗,宫颈癌 仍然是一项重大的全球健康挑战,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。宫颈癌主要是由高危型HPV持续感染引起的,但持续感染对于癌症的发展是必要的,但还不够充分。在了解 HPV 遗传多样性、病毒持久性和病毒-宿主 分子相互作用方面取得的进展,为提高 HPV 预防和 治疗 的精确度创造了新的机会。HPV 基因型分布和致癌潜力的差异为 疫苗 的开发和筛查策略提供信息。病毒癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 破坏 宿主 肿瘤抑制途径,驱动细胞永生化和基因组不稳定。病毒插入、表观遗传修饰、APOBEC 相关 突变 和免疫逃逸等分子事件有助于疾病进展。这些发现揭示了可以作为治疗目标的分子漏洞。免疫检查点抑制剂和抗体药物偶联物在 宫颈癌 呈现和 治疗 的特定背景下临床使用。与此同时,正在研究治疗性 疫苗、过继性细胞免疫疗法、RNA 干扰和 基因组 编辑,作为针对 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 的补充策略。随着下一代测序、分子生物标志物和人工智能的进步,这些工具可以进一步完善风险分层和治疗选择。本综述探讨了目前对 HPV 遗传变异和病毒与 宿主 相互作用的理解,推动了从传统预防和 治疗 策略向精准预防和靶向治疗的转变,并严格评估了剩余的挑战。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管有有效的预防药物 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗、筛查方法和已建立的 治疗,宫颈癌 仍然是一个重大的全球健康挑战,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。该研究旨在探讨目前对HPV遗传变异和病毒-宿主相互作用的理解,推动从传统预防和治疗策略向精准预防和靶向治疗的转变。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了有关 HPV 遗传多样性、病毒持久性和病毒-宿主 分子相互作用的最新文献。它重点关注 HPV 相关的 宫颈癌 和其他明确与 HPV 相关的疾病,包括宫颈上皮内瘤变、HPV 相关的肛门癌和 HPV 相关的口咽癌。
核心方法
该研究利用下一代测序、分子生物标志物和人工智能来完善 HPV 相关疾病的风险分层和治疗选择。
主要结果
该研究报告称,HPV 基因型分布和致癌潜力的差异为 疫苗 的开发和筛查策略提供了信息。它还强调了病毒插入、表观遗传修饰、APOBEC 相关 突变 和免疫逃逸等分子事件有助于疾病进展。
解释与创新
作者解释说,这些发现揭示了可以作为治疗目标的分子漏洞。这代表了一种利用 HPV 遗传多样性和病毒-宿主 相互作用来预防 宫颈癌 和 治疗 的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 制定更精确的预防和 治疗 策略提供信息,从而有可能改善受影响人群的结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究为综述,不提供原始数据,因此证据强度中等。局限性包括依赖现有文献和缺乏新的实验结果。

From HPV Genetic Diversity to Precision Therapeutics: Viral-Host Interactions and Emerging Strategies for Cervical Cancer Prevention and Treatment

Authors: Vaibhavi Babu
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer is a major global health challenge, especially in low- and middle-income countries, despite the availability of effective prophylactic human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines, screening methods and established treatments. Cervical cancer is primarily caused by persistent infection with high-risk HPV, but persistent infection is necessary, but not sufficient, for the development of cancer. Advances in understanding HPV genetic diversity, viral persistence, and virus-host molecular interactions are creating new opportunities to improve precision in HPV prevention and treatment. HPV genotype distribution and differences in oncogenic potential inform vaccine development and screening strategies. The viral oncoproteins E6 and E7 disrupt host tumor-suppressor pathways, driving cellular immortalization and genomic instability. Molecular events such as viral insertion, epigenetic modifications, APOBEC-associated mutations, and immune escape contribute to disease progression. These discoveries have revealed molecular vulnerabilities that can be targeted therapeutically. Immune checkpoint inhibitors and antibody-drug conjugates are used clinically in specific contexts of cervical cancer presentation and treatment. At the same time, therapeutic vaccines, adoptive cellular immunotherapies, RNA interference, and genome editing are being investigated as complementary strategies to target HPV-associated cervical cancer. With advances in next-generation sequencing, molecular biomarkers, and artificial intelligence, these tools could further refine risk stratification and therapy selection. This review explores the current understanding of HPV genetic variation and virus-host interactions driving a shift from traditional prevention and treatment strategies to precision prevention and targeted therapeutics, and critically assesses remaining challenges.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer remains a major global health challenge, especially in low- and middle-income countries, despite the availability of effective prophylactic human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines, screening methods and established treatments. The study aims to explore the current understanding of HPV genetic variation and virus-host interactions driving a shift from traditional prevention and treatment strategies to precision prevention and targeted therapeutics.
Design and population
The study is a review of current literature on HPV genetic diversity, viral persistence, and virus-host molecular interactions. It focuses on HPV-associated cervical cancer and other explicitly HPV-related diseases, including cervical intraepithelial neoplasia, HPV-associated anal cancer, and HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer.
Core methods
The study utilizes next-generation sequencing, molecular biomarkers, and artificial intelligence to refine risk stratification and therapy selection for HPV-associated diseases.
Main results
The study reports that HPV genotype distribution and differences in oncogenic potential inform vaccine development and screening strategies. It also highlights that molecular events such as viral insertion, epigenetic modifications, APOBEC-associated mutations, and immune escape contribute to disease progression.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the discoveries reveal molecular vulnerabilities that can be targeted therapeutically. This represents a novel approach to cervical cancer prevention and treatment by leveraging HPV genetic diversity and virus-host interactions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more precise prevention and treatment strategies for HPV-associated cervical cancer, potentially improving outcomes in affected populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a review and does not present original data, thus the evidence strength is moderate. The limitations include the reliance on existing literature and the absence of new experimental results.

对 STEAM(科学、技术、工程、艺术和数学)同伴教育干预对青少年的 HPV 疫苗 沟通知识、信心和宣传进行定性评估。

A qualitative evaluation of a STEAM (Science, technology, engineering, arts, and mathematics) peer education intervention on teenagers' knowledge, confidence, and advocacy for HPV vaccine communication.
作者： Carol Gray Brunton, Iain Macdonald, Céline Healy, Richard Firth, Alexandra R. McDermott, Eva M. Malone, Carol Gray-Brunton
摘要中文翻译疫苗 犹豫不决是一种日益严重的全球现象。2015 年,爱尔兰经历了信心危机,人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的使用率下降,但全国迅速努力解决问题并提高使用率。我们通过跨学科团队(免疫学家、教育家、设计师、电影制作人和心理学家)开发了一种独特的 STEAM(科学、技术、工程、艺术和数学)同伴教育干预措施,以解决 疫苗 的犹豫并利用同伴教育,这是有希望的,但在文献中未得到充分利用。我们的目的是评估我们的 STEAM 干预对爱尔兰青少年向面临 HPV 疫苗 优惠的年轻同龄人宣传 HPV 疫苗 的知识和信心的影响。我们使用定性方法,包括 12 次与青少年进行的焦点小组讨论 (n = 53)、一次年轻同龄人的定性课堂评估 (n = 35) 和知识出口单 (n = 300),以评估青少年的知识、信心、宣传和年轻同龄人的知识。使用反思性主题分析对数据进行归纳分析。我们的总体平等主义主题表明青少年的民主参与具有三个相关主题,这些主题与 疫苗 倡导者的行为相一致。干预后,青少年报告 HPV 知识、信心和宣传信念总体上发生了积极变化。年轻的同龄人可以在课堂评估和结业单中回忆起 HPV 的事实和知识。我们的结论是,通过我们的 STEAM 干预搭建脚手架,使爱尔兰青少年感到自己有能力充当 疫苗 的倡导者。我们的干预措施中的同伴教育显示出提高 HPV 认识和知识的希望,并且可以在更广泛的背景下进行探索。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
疫苗 犹豫不决是一种日益严重的全球现象。2015 年,爱尔兰经历了信心危机,人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的使用率下降,但全国迅速努力解决问题并提高使用率。
设计与对象
我们通过跨学科团队(免疫学家、教育家、设计师、电影制作人和心理学家)开发了一种独特的 STEAM(科学、技术、工程、艺术和数学)同伴教育干预措施,以解决 疫苗 的犹豫并利用同伴教育,这是有希望的,但在文献中未得到充分利用。
核心方法
我们使用定性方法,包括 12 次与青少年进行的焦点小组讨论 (n = 53)、一次年轻同龄人的定性课堂评估 (n = 35) 和知识出口单 (n = 300),以评估青少年的知识、信心、宣传和年轻同龄人的知识。
主要结果
干预后,青少年报告 HPV 知识、信心和宣传信念总体上发生了积极变化。年轻的同龄人可以在课堂评估和结业单中回忆起 HPV 的事实和知识。
解释与创新
我们的结论是,通过我们的 STEAM 干预搭建脚手架,使爱尔兰青少年感到自己有能力充当 疫苗 的倡导者。我们的干预措施中的同伴教育显示出提高 HPV 认识和知识的希望,并且可以在更广泛的背景下进行探索。
科研与公卫意义
我们的研究结果表明,同伴教育干预措施可能有效提高青少年对 HPV 的认识和知识,这可以为未来旨在提高 疫苗 吸收率的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告研究设计的局限性或证据强度评估。

A qualitative evaluation of a STEAM (Science, technology, engineering, arts, and mathematics) peer education intervention on teenagers' knowledge, confidence, and advocacy for HPV vaccine communication.

Authors: Carol Gray Brunton, Iain Macdonald, Céline Healy, Richard Firth, Alexandra R. McDermott, Eva M. Malone, Carol Gray-Brunton
Original abstract (English)
Vaccine hesitancy is a rising global phenomenon. Ireland experienced a crisis in confidence and vaccine decline for the Human Papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine in 2015, with rapid national efforts to address concerns and improve uptake rates. We developed a unique STEAM (Science, Technology, Engineering, Arts and Mathematics) peer education intervention through an interdisciplinary team (immunologist, educationalist, designer, filmmaker, and psychologist) to address vaccine hesitancy and utilize peer education, which is promising but underused in the literature. We aimed to evaluate the effect of our STEAM intervention on Irish teenagers' knowledge and confidence in advocacy for the HPV vaccine to their younger peers facing their HPV vaccine offer. We used qualitative methods consisting of 12 focus group discussions with teens (n = 53), one qualitative in-class evaluation among younger peers (n = 35), and knowledge exit slips (n = 300) to evaluate teenagers' knowledge, confidence, advocacy, and younger peers' knowledge. Data were analyzed inductively using reflexive thematic analysis. Our overall egalitarian theme indicated democratic participation by teenagers with three related themes that were compatible with behavior as a vaccine advocate. Teenagers reported positive changes overall in HPV knowledge, confidence, and advocacy beliefs following the intervention. Younger peers could recall HPV facts and knowledge during in-class evaluations and exit slips. We conclude that scaffolding through our STEAM intervention enabled Irish teenagers to feel empowered to act as vaccine advocates. Peer education within our intervention shows promise in raising HPV awareness and knowledge and could be explored in wider contexts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Vaccine hesitancy is a rising global phenomenon. Ireland experienced a crisis in confidence and vaccine decline for the Human Papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine in 2015, with rapid national efforts to address concerns and improve uptake rates.
Design and population
We developed a unique STEAM (Science, Technology, Engineering, Arts and Mathematics) peer education intervention through an interdisciplinary team (immunologist, educationalist, designer, filmmaker, and psychologist) to address vaccine hesitancy and utilize peer education, which is promising but underused in the literature.
Core methods
We used qualitative methods consisting of 12 focus group discussions with teens (n = 53), one qualitative in-class evaluation among younger peers (n = 35), and knowledge exit slips (n = 300) to evaluate teenagers' knowledge, confidence, advocacy, and younger peers' knowledge.
Main results
Teenagers reported positive changes overall in HPV knowledge, confidence, and advocacy beliefs following the intervention. Younger peers could recall HPV facts and knowledge during in-class evaluations and exit slips.
Interpretation and novelty
We conclude that scaffolding through our STEAM intervention enabled Irish teenagers to feel empowered to act as vaccine advocates. Peer education within our intervention shows promise in raising HPV awareness and knowledge and could be explored in wider contexts.
Scientific and public-health significance
Our findings suggest that peer education interventions may be effective in improving HPV awareness and knowledge among teenagers, which could inform future public health strategies aimed at increasing vaccine uptake.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study design or evidence strength assessment.
