含酚羟基脂质纳米颗粒能够同时递送 HPV 肽抗原和用于癌症免疫治疗的锰佐剂。

Phenolic Hydroxyl-Containing Lipid Nanoparticles Enable Codelivery of HPV Peptide Antigens and Manganese Adjuvant for Cancer Immunotherapy.
作者： Shuang Hou, De-Ling Zhou, Yi-Wei Zhang, You-Ming Li, Dang Fan, Ye-Qin Hu, Li-Yan Li, Chang-Jiang Qin, Ming-Xue Li, Hong Liu
摘要中文翻译摘要 针对 HPV 相关癌症的治疗性肽 疫苗 通常受到免疫原性不足和细胞内递送效率低下的限制。在这里,我们开发了一种含酚羟基的脂质纳米颗粒(EMT@LNP)作为纳米医学平台,用于协调共递送 HPV16 E6/E7 肽抗原和锰(Mn2+)佐剂。与传统的静电负载策略不同,EMT@LNP利用酚羟基和肽抗原之间的氢键相互作用,实现具有不同理化性质的肽的电荷独立负载,而酚部分和Mn2+之间的配位相互作用允许同时掺入免疫刺激佐剂。这种双重相互作用驱动的设计将抗原递送和先天免疫激活集成在单个纳米结构内。从功能上讲,EMT@LNP 增强抗原摄取并促进树突状细胞成熟,同时激活 STING 相关信号传导,如 STING、TBK1 和 IRF3 磷酸化增加所示。在体内,EMT@LNP 有助于有效递送至引流淋巴结,并延长肌肉注射后的抗原保留时间。在治疗性 TC-1 肿瘤模型中,EMT@LNP 引发有效的抗肿瘤 免疫应答,其特点是增强 CD8+ T 细胞浸润和减少免疫抑制细胞群,从而导致显着的肿瘤生长抑制,且没有可观察到的全身毒性。总的来说,这项研究提出了一种纳米生物技术策略,将电荷无关的肽负载与金属离子介导的免疫激活相结合,为 疫苗接种 治疗 HPV 相关恶性肿瘤提供了一个多功能且潜在可转化的平台。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过开发新型纳米医学平台,解决治疗性肽 疫苗 针对 HPV 相关癌症的局限性,特别是免疫原性不足和细胞内递送效率低下。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种含酚羟基的脂质纳米颗粒(EMT@LNP)作为纳米医学平台,用于协同递送 HPV16 E6/E7 肽抗原和锰(Mn2+)佐剂。该平台在治疗性 TC-1 肿瘤模型中进行了测试。
核心方法
EMT@LNP 旨在利用酚羟基和肽抗原之间的氢键相互作用,从而实现具有不同理化性质的肽的电荷独立负载。酚类部分和 Mn2+ 之间的配位相互作用允许同时掺入免疫刺激佐剂。
主要结果
从功能上讲,EMT@LNP 增强了抗原摄取并促进树突状细胞成熟,同时激活 STING 相关信号传导,如 STING、TBK1 和 IRF3 磷酸化增加所示。在体内,它有助于有效递送至引流淋巴结并延长肌肉注射后的抗原保留时间。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为一种新颖的纳米生物技术策略的证据,该策略将电荷无关的肽负载与金属离子介导的免疫激活相结合,为 疫苗接种 治疗 HPV 相关恶性肿瘤提供了一个多功能平台。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,EMT@LNP 可以为针对 HPV 相关癌症开发更有效的治疗药物 疫苗 提供信息,从而有可能改善免疫原性和 治疗 结局。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象分析,证据基于体外和体内模型,可能无法完全代表临床情况。证据强度中等。

Phenolic Hydroxyl-Containing Lipid Nanoparticles Enable Codelivery of HPV Peptide Antigens and Manganese Adjuvant for Cancer Immunotherapy.

Authors: Shuang Hou, De-Ling Zhou, Yi-Wei Zhang, You-Ming Li, Dang Fan, Ye-Qin Hu, Li-Yan Li, Chang-Jiang Qin, Ming-Xue Li, Hong Liu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Therapeutic peptide vaccines against HPV-associated cancers are often limited by insufficient immunogenicity and inefficient intracellular delivery. Here, we develop a phenolic hydroxyl-containing lipid nanoparticle (EMT@LNP) as a nanomedicine platform for the coordinated codelivery of HPV16 E6/E7 peptide antigens and manganese (Mn2+) adjuvant. Distinct from conventional electrostatic loading strategies, EMT@LNP exploits hydrogen-bonding interactions between phenolic hydroxyl groups and peptide antigens, enabling charge-independent loading of peptides with diverse physicochemical properties, while coordination interactions between phenolic moieties and Mn2+ allow the simultaneous incorporation of immunostimulatory adjuvants. This dual-interaction-driven design integrates antigen delivery and innate immune activation within a single nanostructure. Functionally, EMT@LNP enhances antigen uptake and promotes dendritic cell maturation, accompanied by activation of STING-associated signaling, as indicated by increased phosphorylation of STING, TBK1, and IRF3. In vivo, EMT@LNP facilitates efficient delivery to draining lymph nodes and prolongs antigen retention following intramuscular administration. In a therapeutic TC-1 tumor model, EMT@LNP elicits potent antitumor immune responses, characterized by enhanced CD8+ T-cell infiltration and reduced immunosuppressive cell populations, leading to significant tumor growth inhibition without observable systemic toxicity. Collectively, this study presents a nanobiotechnology strategy that combines charge-independent peptide loading with metal-ion-mediated immune activation, providing a versatile and potentially translatable platform for therapeutic vaccination against HPV-associated malignancies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the limitations of therapeutic peptide vaccines against HPV-associated cancers, specifically insufficient immunogenicity and inefficient intracellular delivery, by developing a novel nanomedicine platform.
Design and population
The study developed a phenolic hydroxyl-containing lipid nanoparticle (EMT@LNP) as a nanomedicine platform for the coordinated codelivery of HPV16 E6/E7 peptide antigens and manganese (Mn2+) adjuvant. The platform was tested in a therapeutic TC-1 tumor model.
Core methods
EMT@LNP was designed to exploit hydrogen-bonding interactions between phenolic hydroxyl groups and peptide antigens, enabling charge-independent loading of peptides with diverse physicochemical properties. Coordination interactions between phenolic moieties and Mn2+ allowed the simultaneous incorporation of immunostimulatory adjuvants.
Main results
Functionally, EMT@LNP enhanced antigen uptake and promoted dendritic cell maturation, accompanied by activation of STING-associated signaling, as indicated by increased phosphorylation of STING, TBK1, and IRF3. In vivo, it facilitated efficient delivery to draining lymph nodes and prolonged antigen retention following intramuscular administration.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence of a novel nanobiotechnology strategy that combines charge-independent peptide loading with metal-ion-mediated immune activation, offering a versatile platform for therapeutic vaccination against HPV-associated malignancies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that EMT@LNP could inform the development of more effective therapeutic vaccines for HPV-associated cancers, potentially improving immunogenicity and treatment outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, and the evidence is based on in vitro and in vivo models, which may not fully represent clinical scenarios. The evidence strength is moderate.

Journal: PLOS One  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-26  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.pone.0359273  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 21, 10, e0359273
C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

在资源匮乏的环境中应对 HPV 疫苗接种 挑战:估计失学女孩体型的实用策略。

Navigating HPV vaccination challenges in low-resource settings: Practical strategies for estimating the size of out-of-school girls.
作者： Soim Park, Erica N. Rosser, Leanne Zhang, Megan D. Wysong, Arman Majidulla, Marina Terada, Pamela J. Surkan, Joseph G. Rosen, Rupali Jayant Limaye
摘要中文翻译背景:要实现 2030 年 15 岁女孩 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 覆盖率达到 90% 的目标,需要有效覆盖失学 (OOS) 女孩,特别是在大多数 疫苗 分娩发生在学校环境中的低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC)。本研究探讨了各国对 OOS 女孩的定义以及估计该人口规模的策略。方法:2024 年 5 月至 9 月期间,我们对 6 个已建立 HPV 疫苗接种 项目的非洲和亚洲中低收入国家的 32 名 HPV 疫苗 项目利益相关者进行了半结构化深度访谈:柬埔寨、喀麦隆、肯尼亚、马拉维、莫桑比克和乌干达。通过混合归纳演绎主题分析,我们确定了各国定义 OOS 女孩以及计算或估计人口规模的方法。结果:在行政上,各国通常将失学女童定义为未在教育部管辖范围内的学校就读的女童,但在实践中,不同背景下的定义有所不同。国家层面的数据(例如人口普查、入学率)和家庭层面的方法(例如人数)通常用于统计在校女童和离校女童。然而,在 HPV 疫苗接种 实施和评估过程中,在校女孩和 OOS 女孩之间的区别往往很模糊,限制了这些方法的操作效用。为了应对这些挑战,一些国家采取了替代方法,包括使用历史或近似数据进行基于代理的人口估计,以及优先考虑被认为存在离校女童较多的地区。这些替代方法被认为是准确性、可行性和资源限制之间的务实妥协。结论:准确计数 OOS 女孩对于 HPV 疫苗接种 很重要,但在资源有限的环境中可能不可行。务实、针对具体情况的估计方法可以提供一种可行的方法,通过 HPV 疫苗接种 服务更有效地识别和接触 OOS 女孩。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨各国对失学 (OOS) 女童的定义以及估算中低收入国家 (LMIC) 女童人口规模的策略,以应对在 2030 年实现 15 岁女童 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率 90% 的目标方面所面临的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究于 2024 年 5 月至 9 月期间对来自 6 个非洲和亚洲中低收入国家(柬埔寨、喀麦隆、肯尼亚、马拉维、莫桑比克和乌干达)的 32 名 HPV 疫苗 项目利益相关者进行了半结构化深入访谈。
核心方法
研究人员使用半结构化深入访谈和混合归纳演绎主题分析来确定各国定义 OOS 女孩并估计其人口规模的方法。
主要结果
研究发现,各国通常在行政上将失学女童定义为未在教育部管辖范围内的学校就读的女童,但实践中的定义各不相同。使用国家层面的数据和家庭层面的方法来统计在校女童和失学女童,但在 HPV 疫苗接种 实施和评估过程中,在校女童和失学女童之间的区别常常变得模糊。
解释与创新
该研究强调需要采用务实、针对具体情况的估计方法,以通过 HPV 疫苗接种 服务准确识别和更有效地接触 OOS 女孩。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为提高 OOS 女孩计数方法的操作效用提供策略,并在资源有限的环境中扩大 HPV 疫苗接种 计划的覆盖范围。
局限与证据强度
该研究对访谈和主题分析的依赖限制了研究结果的普遍性。在校女童和失学女童之间的区别往往很模糊,这可能会影响人口估计的准确性。

Navigating HPV vaccination challenges in low-resource settings: Practical strategies for estimating the size of out-of-school girls.

Authors: Soim Park, Erica N. Rosser, Leanne Zhang, Megan D. Wysong, Arman Majidulla, Marina Terada, Pamela J. Surkan, Joseph G. Rosen, Rupali Jayant Limaye
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Achieving the 2030 target of 90% human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination coverage among girls by age 15 requires effectively reaching out-of-school (OOS) girls, particularly in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) where most vaccine delivery occurs in school settings. This study explored country definitions of OOS girls and strategies for estimating the size of this population. METHODS: Between May and September 2024, we conducted semi-structured in-depth interviews with 32 HPV vaccine program stakeholders across six African and Asian LMICs with established HPV vaccination programs: Cambodia, Cameroon, Kenya, Malawi, Mozambique, and Uganda. Using hybrid inductive-deductive thematic analyses, we identified country approaches to defining OOS girls and to enumerating or estimating population size. RESULTS: Administratively, countries commonly defined OOS girls as those not enrolled in schools under the Ministry of Education's purview, but in practice, definitions varied across contexts. National-level data (e.g., census, school enrollment rates) and household-level approaches (e.g., headcounts) were commonly used to enumerate in-school and OOS girls. However, the distinction between in-school and OOS girls was often blurred during HPV vaccination implementation and evaluation, limiting the operational utility of these methods. To address these challenges, some countries adopted alternative approaches, including proxy-based population estimates using historical or approximate data and the prioritization of areas perceived to have a higher presence of OOS girls. These alternative approaches were perceived as pragmatic compromises between accuracy, feasibility, and resource constraints. CONCLUSION: Accurate enumeration of OOS girls is important for HPV vaccination but may not be feasible in resource-limited settings. Pragmatic, context-specific estimation methods can offer a feasible way to identify and reach OOS girls more effectively with HPV vaccination services.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore country definitions of out-of-school (OOS) girls and strategies for estimating their population size in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) to address challenges in achieving the 2030 target of 90% HPV vaccination coverage among girls by age 15.
Design and population
The study involved semi-structured in-depth interviews with 32 HPV vaccine program stakeholders from six African and Asian LMICs: Cambodia, Cameroon, Kenya, Malawi, Mozambique, and Uganda, conducted between May and September 2024.
Core methods
The researchers used semi-structured in-depth interviews and hybrid inductive-deductive thematic analyses to identify country approaches to defining OOS girls and estimating their population size.
Main results
The study found that countries commonly defined OOS girls administratively as those not enrolled in schools under the Ministry of Education's purview, but definitions varied in practice. National-level data and household-level approaches were used to enumerate in-school and OOS girls, but the distinction between in-school and OOS girls was often blurred during HPV vaccination implementation and evaluation.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlighted the need for pragmatic, context-specific estimation methods to accurately identify and reach OOS girls more effectively with HPV vaccination services.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform strategies for improving the operational utility of methods to enumerate OOS girls and enhance the reach of HPV vaccination programs in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's reliance on interviews and thematic analyses limits the generalizability of the findings. The distinction between in-school and OOS girls was often blurred, which may affect the accuracy of population estimates.

HPV 感染女性自然杀伤细胞活性的评估——一项横断面研究。

Assessment of natural killer cell activity in HPV-infected women - a cross-sectional study.
作者： Murat Gunenc, Yagmur Soykan, Neslihan Bayramoglu Tepe, Ibrahim Taskum, Seyithan Taysi, Murat Günenç, İbrahim Taşkum, Seyithan Tayşi
摘要中文翻译目的:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染,尤其是高风险致癌类型,与宫颈癌和其他肛门生殖器癌有关。自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞在先天 免疫应答 中发挥着至关重要的作用,无需事先致敏即可识别并消除病毒感染的细胞。材料和方法:本研究比较了致癌 HPV 感染女性和 HPV 阴性对照女性的血清 NK 细胞活性。了解 NK 细胞活性的差异可能有助于深入了解 HPV 清除机制和潜在的免疫治疗靶点。方法:在加济安泰普大学医院进行了一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究,招募了超过 12 个月的患者。根据 HPV 状态对参与者进行分类,并使用基于 ELISA 的检测分析血液样本以定量评估血清 NK 细胞活性。结果:在 82 名女性中,20 名 HPV 16/18 检测呈阳性,35 名患有其他高危致癌 HPV 类型,27 名 HPV 阴性。HPV 16/18 和其他 高危型HPV 组之间的 NK 细胞活性没有显着差异 (p = 0.544)。然而,HPV 阴性组的 NK 细胞活性显着高于 HPV 阳性组(p = 0.001 和 p = 0.002)。结论:NK 细胞活性降低可能导致持续致癌 HPV 感染。这些发现表明 NK 细胞调节疗法在增强 HPV 清除方面具有潜在作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞在清除致癌性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染中的作用,弥补了解 免疫应答 与 HPV 的知识差距。
设计与对象
加济安泰普大学医院进行了一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究,该研究涉及 82 名女性,为期 12 个月,根据 HPV 状态进行分类。
核心方法
使用基于 ELISA 的测定法对致癌 HPV 感染女性和 HPV 阴性对照的血清 NK 细胞活性进行定量评估。
主要结果
在 82 名女性中,20 人 HPV 16/18 检测呈阳性,35 人检测出其他高危致癌 HPV 类型,27 人 HPV 阴性。HPV 16/18 组和其他 高危型HPV 组之间的 NK 细胞活性没有显着差异 (p = 0.544),但 HPV 阴性组中的 NK 细胞活性显着较高 (p = 0.001 和 p = 0.002)。
解释与创新
这些发现表明 NK 细胞调节疗法在增强 HPV 清除方面具有潜在作用,强调了 NK 细胞活性在 HPV 感染结果中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究提供了对 HPV 清除机制和潜在免疫治疗靶点的见解,这可以为未来针对 HPV 相关疾病的 治疗 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其前瞻性 队列研究 设计和小样本量而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。研究结果表明 NK 细胞调节疗法具有潜在作用,但需要进一步研究来证实这些结果。

Assessment of natural killer cell activity in HPV-infected women - a cross-sectional study.

Authors: Murat Gunenc, Yagmur Soykan, Neslihan Bayramoglu Tepe, Ibrahim Taskum, Seyithan Taysi, Murat Günenç, İbrahim Taşkum, Seyithan Tayşi
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: Human papillomavirus (HPV) infections, particularly high-risk oncogenic types, are linked to cervical and other anogenital cancers. Natural killer (NK) cells play a vital role in the innate immune response, identifying and eliminating virus-infected cells without prior sensitization. Material and methods: This study compares serum NK cell activity in women with oncogenic HPV infections and HPV-negative controls. Understanding differences in NK cell activity may provide insights into HPV clearance mechanisms and potential immunotherapeutic targets. Methods: A prospective cohort study was conducted at Gaziantep University Hospital, enrolling patients over 12 months. Participants were categorized based on HPV status, and blood samples were analyzed to quantitatively assess serum NK cell activity using an ELISA-based assay. Results: Among 82 women, 20 tested positive for HPV 16/18, 35 had other high-risk oncogenic HPV types, and 27 were HPV negative. NK cell activity did not significantly differ between HPV 16/18 and other high-risk HPV groups (p = 0.544). However, the HPV-negative group had significantly higher NK cell activity than both HPV-positive groups (p = 0.001 and p = 0.002). Conclusions: Reduced NK cell activity may contribute to persistent oncogenic HPV infections. These findings suggest a potential role for NK cell-modulating therapies to enhance HPV clearance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the role of natural killer (NK) cells in the clearance of oncogenic human papillomavirus (HPV) infections, addressing the knowledge gap in understanding immune responses to HPV.
Design and population
A prospective cohort study was conducted at Gaziantep University Hospital, involving 82 women over 12 months, categorized based on HPV status.
Core methods
Serum NK cell activity was quantitatively assessed using an ELISA-based assay in women with oncogenic HPV infections and HPV-negative controls.
Main results
Among 82 women, 20 tested positive for HPV 16/18, 35 had other high-risk oncogenic HPV types, and 27 were HPV negative. NK cell activity did not significantly differ between HPV 16/18 and other high-risk HPV groups (p = 0.544), but was significantly higher in the HPV-negative group (p = 0.001 and p = 0.002).
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest a potential role for NK cell-modulating therapies to enhance HPV clearance, highlighting the importance of NK cell activity in HPV infection outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the mechanisms of HPV clearance and potential immunotherapeutic targets, which could inform future treatment strategies for HPV-associated diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its prospective cohort design and small sample size, which may affect generalizability. The findings suggest a potential role for NK cell-modulating therapies, but further research is needed to confirm these results.

多方面的 HPV16 E2 作为 宫颈癌 及其他药物的潜在治疗靶点

Multifaceted HPV16 E2 as a potential therapeutic target for cervical cancer and beyond
作者： Xing-Yu Mo, Han-Meng Wang, Zhen-Lei Wang, Yuan-Yuan Xiao, Shuang-Yang Tang, Le Li
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)可引起多种上皮病变,包括宿主细胞甚至宫颈癌的恶性增殖。HPV E2可以在调节病毒基因表达、病毒复制和转录方面发挥重要作用,也可以参与细胞分裂过程中病毒基因组的分离。HPV16 E2蛋白在低分化宫颈病变细胞中高表达,但随着宫颈病变级别的增加而降低或消失。作为DNA复制的起始蛋白和HPV16基因表达的主要转录调节蛋白,E2蛋白可能在病毒生命周期中发挥关键作用。HPV16 E2蛋白作为序列特异性DNA结合蛋白,能够与E6和E7癌基因启动子上的特异性DNA识别序列结合,进而抑制E6和E7癌基因的转录和表达,从而诱导细胞凋亡,抑制肿瘤形成。因此,旨在恢复或增强 HPV16 E2 功能的治疗策略可能有望用于预防和 治疗 HPV 相关癌症,包括(i)基因治疗介导的 E2 恢复;(ii)直接递送E2蛋白;(iii) 通过调节相互作用途径(例如 TopBP1、Brd4)来稳定 E2;(iv) 基于 E2 的治疗剂 疫苗。这些方法可能通过抑制E6/E7癌基因、调节细胞周期、抑制细胞增殖和诱导细胞凋亡来发挥抗肿瘤作用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),特别是 HPV16,及其在 宫颈癌 和其他 HPV 相关恶性肿瘤中的作用。核心问题是HPV16 E2是否可以作为这些疾病的预防和治疗的治疗靶点。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 HPV16 E2 生物学功能的叙述性分析。它不报告系统评价方法、数据库或荟萃分析。证据源自 HPV16 E2 在病毒复制、转录和肿瘤发生中的作用的一般描述。
共识与结论
综述得出的结论是,HPV16 E2是病毒生命周期中的关键调节蛋白,在DNA复制和基因表达中发挥着核心作用。还值得注意的是,HPV16 E2在低分化宫颈病变细胞中高表达,可能通过抑制E6和E7癌基因来抑制肿瘤形成。
争议与缺口
该综述强调,HPV16 E2 的表达随着宫颈病变级别的增加而降低,但没有提供这种变化背后的机制或其临床意义的证据。没有提及与 E2 治疗潜力相关的相互矛盾的发现或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,针对 HPV16 E2 的治疗策略,例如基因治疗、蛋白质递送、通路调节和 疫苗,可能为 HPV 相关癌症的预防和 治疗 提供有希望的途径。这些方法可能会抑制 E6/E7 癌基因、调节细胞周期、抑制增殖并诱导细胞凋亡。

Multifaceted HPV16 E2 as a potential therapeutic target for cervical cancer and beyond

Authors: Xing-Yu Mo, Han-Meng Wang, Zhen-Lei Wang, Yuan-Yuan Xiao, Shuang-Yang Tang, Le Li
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) can cause a variety of epithelial lesions, including the malignant proliferation of host cells and even cervical cancer. HPV E2 can play an important role in regulating viral gene expression, viral replication and transcription, and also can participate in the isolation of viral genome during cell division. HPV16 E2 protein is highly expressed in low-differentiated cervical lesion cells, but decreased or disappeared with the increase of cervical lesion grade. As both the initiation protein for DNA replication and the major transcriptional regulator for HPV16 gene expression, E2 protein may play a key role in the viral life cycle. HPV16 E2 protein as a sequence specific DNA binding protein, can bind with the specific DNA recognition sequence on the promoters of E6 and E7 oncogenes, and then inhibit the transcription and expression of E6 and E7 oncogenes, thus inducing cell apoptosis and suppressing tumor formation. Therefore, therapeutic strategies aimed at restoring or enhancing HPV16 E2 function may hold promise for the prevention and treatment of HPV-related cancers, including (i) gene-therapy-mediated E2 restoration; (ii) direct delivery of E2 protein; (iii) E2 stabilization through modulation of interacting pathways (e.g., TopBP1, Brd4); and (iv) E2-based therapeutic vaccines. These approaches may exert antitumor effects by repressing E6/E7 oncogenes, regulating the cell cycle, inhibiting cell proliferation, and inducing apoptosis.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on Human papillomaviruses (HPV), specifically HPV16, and its role in cervical cancer and other HPV-associated malignancies. The central question is whether HPV16 E2 can serve as a therapeutic target for the prevention and treatment of these diseases.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative analysis of the biological functions of HPV16 E2. It does not report on systematic review methods, databases, or meta-analysis. The evidence is derived from general descriptions of HPV16 E2's role in viral replication, transcription, and oncogenesis.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that HPV16 E2 is a key regulatory protein in the viral life cycle, playing a central role in DNA replication and gene expression. It is also noted that HPV16 E2 is highly expressed in low-differentiated cervical lesion cells and may suppress tumor formation by inhibiting E6 and E7 oncogenes.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that the expression of HPV16 E2 decreases with increasing cervical lesion grade, but it does not provide evidence on the mechanisms behind this change or its clinical implications. There is no mention of conflicting findings or evidence gaps related to the therapeutic potential of E2.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that therapeutic strategies targeting HPV16 E2, such as gene therapy, protein delivery, pathway modulation, and vaccines, may offer promising avenues for the prevention and treatment of HPV-related cancers. These approaches could potentially repress E6/E7 oncogenes, regulate the cell cycle, inhibit proliferation, and induce apoptosis.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

超越分析能力:中国国家外部质量评估网络中 人乳头瘤病毒 测试实验室的信息连续性差距。

Beyond analytical proficiency: information continuity gaps in human papillomavirus testing laboratories within China's national external quality assessment network.
作者： Guigao Lin, Kuo Zhang
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
此次审查重点关注人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),并评估中国国家外部质量评估(EQA)网络内HPV检测实验室信息的连续性。核心问题是实验室是否能够有效地将 HPV 检测结果与细胞学、阴道镜检查或组织病理学等下游临床过程联系起来,以及这些能力是否可以作为整个检测过程的质量指标。
证据基础与方法
该审查使用了嵌入 2026 年国家临床实验室中心 HPV16/18 EQA 网络中的自愿机构级网络调查。它收集了 2025 年的运营数据,并将调查记录与 EQA 结果联系起来。使用预先指定的 Firth 逻辑回归以及序数和窄暴露敏感性分析来分析关联性。
共识与结论
Among 957 unique invitation-frame laboratories, 111 valid responses were obtained, with 109 matching the frame (response rate 11%). The review found that 88% of respondents had fully correct HPV16/18 results. However, only 59% of laboratories could retrieve any downstream information, 13% had near-complete access, and 37% could fully de-duplicate repeated tests from the same woman. Clinical service integration was associated with downstream information availability (OR 4.01, 95% CI 1.71–9.84; p=0.001), and this association remained significant when exposure was restricted to screening/clinical-reporting functions (OR 3.30, 95% CI 1.40–8.20; p=0.006).
争议与缺口
该审查强调了 HPV 检测实验室检索下游临床信息和消除重复检测能力的异质性。它指出,只有 59% 的实验室可以检索任何下游信息,37% 的实验室可以完全消除重复测试。这些发现表明信息连续性方面尚未解决的差距以及需要进一步标准化和验证这些能力作为质量指标。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调需要进一步标准化和验证患者身份解析和下游结果可检索性,作为分子诊断和 EQA 相关质量改进的候选质量指标。它建议这些过程应优先用于研究和实践,以提高临床环境中 HPV 检测的整体质量和实用性。

Beyond analytical proficiency: information continuity gaps in human papillomavirus testing laboratories within China's national external quality assessment network.

Authors: Guigao Lin, Kuo Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objectives External quality assessment (EQA) emphasizes analytical proficiency, but actionable molecular testing also depends on extra-analytical processes that link results to unique patients and subsequent care. We assessed patient de-duplication and downstream-result retrievability in HPV testing laboratories, using EQA performance as an analytical-quality anchor, and explored these capabilities as candidate quality indicators for the total testing process. Methods A voluntary institution-level web survey was embedded in the 2026 National Center for Clinical Laboratories HPV16/18 EQA network and collected 2025 operational data. Survey records were linked to 2025 EQA results. The primary outcome was any laboratory access to downstream cytology, colposcopy, or histopathology information after an HPV-positive result. Associations were examined using prespecified Firth logistic regression, with ordinal and narrower-exposure sensitivity analyses. Results Among 957 unique invitation-frame laboratories, 111 valid responses were obtained; 109 matched the frame (response rate 11 %). Among 89 respondents with 2025 EQA records, 88 (99 %) had fully correct HPV16/18 results. In contrast, 65/111 (59 %) laboratories could retrieve any downstream information, 14/111 (13 %) reported near-complete access, and 41/111 (37 %) could fully de-duplicate repeated tests from the same woman. Clinical service integration was associated with downstream information availability (OR 4.01, 95 % CI 1.71–9.84; p=0.001); the association persisted when exposure was restricted to screening/clinical-reporting functions (OR 3.30, 95 % CI 1.40–8.20; p=0.006). Conclusions High analytical proficiency did not imply closure of the extra-analytical testing loop. Patient identity resolution and downstream-result retrievability merit further standardization and validation as candidate quality indicators for molecular diagnostics and EQA-linked quality improvement.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on human papillomaviruses (HPV) and evaluates the continuity of information in HPV testing laboratories within China's national external quality assessment (EQA) network. The central question is whether laboratories can effectively link HPV test results to downstream clinical processes such as cytology, colposcopy, or histopathology, and whether these capabilities can serve as quality indicators for the total testing process.
Evidence base and method
The review used a voluntary institution-level web survey embedded in the 2026 National Center for Clinical Laboratories HPV16/18 EQA network. It collected 2025 operational data and linked survey records to EQA results. Associations were analyzed using prespecified Firth logistic regression with ordinal and narrower-exposure sensitivity analyses.
Consensus and conclusions
Among 957 unique invitation-frame laboratories, 111 valid responses were obtained, with 109 matching the frame (response rate 11%). The review found that 88% of respondents had fully correct HPV16/18 results. However, only 59% of laboratories could retrieve any downstream information, 13% had near-complete access, and 37% could fully de-duplicate repeated tests from the same woman. Clinical service integration was associated with downstream information availability (OR 4.01, 95% CI 1.71–9.84; p=0.001), and this association remained significant when exposure was restricted to screening/clinical-reporting functions (OR 3.30, 95% CI 1.40–8.20; p=0.006).
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights heterogeneity in the ability of HPV testing laboratories to retrieve downstream clinical information and de-duplicate repeated tests. It notes that only 59% of laboratories could retrieve any downstream information, and 37% could fully de-duplicate repeated tests. These findings suggest unresolved gaps in information continuity and the need for further standardization and validation of these capabilities as quality indicators.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need for further standardization and validation of patient identity resolution and downstream-result retrievability as candidate quality indicators for molecular diagnostics and EQA-linked quality improvement. It suggests that these processes should be prioritized for research and practice to enhance the overall quality and utility of HPV testing in clinical settings.

Neues zu humanen Papillomaviren in der Kopf-Hals-Onkologie – ASCO-2026 年年度亮点 人乳头瘤病毒 在头颈肿瘤学领域的最新进展 - 2026 年 ASCO 年会亮点

Neues zu humanen Papillomaviren in der Kopf-Hals-Onkologie – Highlights der ASCO-Jahrestagung 2026 Latest developments in human papillomavirus in head and neck oncology—highlights from the 2026 ASCO Annual Meeting
作者： S. J. Sharma, M. C. Suchan, N. J. Galow, M. Mayer, S. Kourou, A. Charpentier, H. Abing, A. Reisewitz, H. N. C. Eckel, J. P. Klussmann
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关口咽癌仍然是头颈肿瘤学中最具活力的研究领域之一。虽然经典的降级策略一直是近年来研究的主要焦点,但 2026 年美国临床肿瘤学会 (ASCO) 年会揭示了向生物学和免疫学驱动的 治疗 方法的重大转变。重点是有关治疗性免疫疗法、治疗性 HPV 疫苗、创新生物标志物和适应性降级策略的新数据。特别值得注意的是随机 II 期 KEYCHAIN 试验的结果,其中顺铂全身治疗被免疫治疗取代,作为晚期 p16 阳性头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 的主要 治疗 的一部分。这项研究首次提供了支持 PD-1 抑制剂无进展生存期主要终点的积极证据,同时也证明了较低的毒性。这与 HN.9 研究的阴性结果形成鲜明对比,其中主要放射免疫治疗未能达到无事件生存的主要终点。此外,还提出了基于免疫评分的风险模型和治疗 疫苗接种 策略的有希望的数据。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关口咽癌仍然是头颈肿瘤学中最具活力的研究领域之一。
证据基础与方法
虽然经典的降级策略一直是近年来研究的主要焦点,但 2026 年美国临床肿瘤学会 (ASCO) 年会揭示了向生物学和免疫学驱动的 治疗 方法的重大转变。
共识与结论
重点是有关治疗性免疫疗法、治疗性 HPV 疫苗、创新生物标志物和适应性降级策略的新数据。
争议与缺口
特别值得注意的是随机 II 期 KEYCHAIN 试验的结果,其中顺铂全身治疗被免疫治疗取代,作为晚期 p16 阳性头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 的主要 治疗 的一部分。这项研究首次提供了支持 PD-1 抑制剂无进展生存期主要终点的积极证据,同时也证明了较低的毒性。这与 HN.9 研究的阴性结果形成鲜明对比,其中主要放射免疫治疗未能达到无事件生存的主要终点。
科研与实践启示
此外,还提出了基于免疫评分的风险模型和治疗 疫苗接种 策略的有希望的数据。

Neues zu humanen Papillomaviren in der Kopf-Hals-Onkologie – Highlights der ASCO-Jahrestagung 2026 Latest developments in human papillomavirus in head and neck oncology—highlights from the 2026 ASCO Annual Meeting

Authors: S. J. Sharma, M. C. Suchan, N. J. Galow, M. Mayer, S. Kourou, A. Charpentier, H. Abing, A. Reisewitz, H. N. C. Eckel, J. P. Klussmann
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated oropharyngeal cancer remains one of the most dynamic areas of research in head and neck oncology. While classical de-escalation strategies have been the primary focus of research in recent years, the 2026 American Society of Clinical Oncology (ASCO) Annual Meeting revealed a significant shift toward biologically and immunologically driven treatment approaches. The focus was on new data regarding curative-intent immunotherapy, therapeutic HPV vaccines, innovative biomarkers, and adaptive de-escalation strategies. Of particular note are the results of the randomized phase II KEYCHAIN trial, in which systemic therapy with cisplatin was replaced by immunotherapy as part of the primary treatment for advanced p16-positive head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC). This study provided the first positive evidence in favor of a PD‐1 inhibitor with regard to the primary endpoint of progression-free survival, while also demonstrating lower toxicity. This contrasted with the negative results of the HN.9 study, in which primary radioimmunotherapy failed to meet the primary endpoint of event-free survival. Furthermore, promising data on Immunoscore-based risk models and therapeutic vaccination strategies were presented.

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps

This study provided the first positive evidence in favor of a PD‐1 inhibitor with regard to the primary endpoint of progression-free survival, while also demonstrating lower toxicity. This contrasted with the negative results of the HN.9 study, in which primary radioimmunotherapy failed to meet the primary endpoint of event-free survival.
Research and practice implications
Furthermore, promising data on Immunoscore-based risk models and therapeutic vaccination strategies were presented.

撒哈拉以南非洲地区的 HPV 感染:循环基因型、疫苗接种 策略(单剂与两剂)以及 HIV 合并感染的影响

HPV Infection in Sub-Saharan Africa: Circulating Genotypes, Vaccination Strategies (Single-Dose Versus Two-Dose), and Implications of HIV Co-Infection
作者： Margaret Gurumani, Vinie Kouamou, Azure Tariro Makadzange, Tariro Makadzange
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌 是撒哈拉以南非洲地区妇女发病和 死亡率 的主要原因,该地区的 疫苗接种 覆盖率仍低于世界卫生组织目标,即 90% 的女孩在 15 岁时完全接种疫苗。这篇叙述性综述综合了有关 HPV 基因型分布、单剂量与两剂量疫苗接种以及 HIV 合并感染影响的证据。尽管 HPV16 和 HPV18 仍然是主要的致癌类型,但 HPV35、HPV52、HPV45、HPV33 和 HPV58 在撒哈拉以南非洲人群中也经常被报道。因此,一些高风险感染不在二价和四价 疫苗 覆盖范围内,而一些检测到的类型仍然在非无价 疫苗 覆盖范围之外。坦桑尼亚和肯尼亚的随机试验分别证明了 60 个月内的持久单剂量免疫原性和 54 个月内针对持续性 疫苗 型感染的疫苗功效超过 98%。哥斯达黎加的 ESCUDDO 试验提供了直接的五年随机比较,显示一剂与两剂的非劣效性。对于艾滋病毒感染者来说,尚无等效的单剂量疗效和持久性证据。这些发现支持在免疫功能正常的人群中扩大单剂量 疫苗接种,同时为艾滋病毒感染者提供多剂量途径、基因型特异性监测和持续宫颈筛查。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
宫颈癌 是撒哈拉以南非洲地区妇女发病和 死亡率 的主要原因,该地区的 疫苗接种 覆盖率仍低于世界卫生组织设定的 90% 女孩在 15 岁之前完全接种疫苗的目标。这篇叙述性综述综合了 HPV 基因型分布、单剂量与两剂量 疫苗接种 以及 HIV 合并感染的影响的证据。
证据基础与方法
坦桑尼亚和肯尼亚的随机试验分别证明了 60 个月内的持久单剂量免疫原性,以及 54 个月内 疫苗 对持续 疫苗 型感染的功效分别高于 98%。哥斯达黎加的 ESCUDDO 试验提供了直接的五年随机比较,显示一剂与两剂的非劣效性。
共识与结论
尽管 HPV16 和 HPV18 仍然是主要的致癌类型,但 HPV35、HPV52、HPV45、HPV33 和 HPV58 在撒哈拉以南非洲人群中也经常报告。对于艾滋病毒感染者来说,尚无等效的单剂量疗效和持久性证据。
争议与缺口
因此,一些高风险感染不在二价和四价 疫苗 覆盖范围内,而一些检测到的类型仍然在非无价 疫苗 覆盖范围之外。
科研与实践启示
这些发现支持在免疫功能正常人群中扩大单剂量 疫苗接种,以及针对 HIV 感染者的多剂量途径、基因型特异性 监测 以及持续的宫颈筛查。

HPV Infection in Sub-Saharan Africa: Circulating Genotypes, Vaccination Strategies (Single-Dose Versus Two-Dose), and Implications of HIV Co-Infection

Authors: Margaret Gurumani, Vinie Kouamou, Azure Tariro Makadzange, Tariro Makadzange
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer is a major cause of morbidity and mortality among women in sub-Saharan Africa, where vaccination coverage remains below the World Health Or-ganization target of 90% of girls fully vaccinated by 15 years of age. This narrative review synthesizes evidence on HPV genotype distribution, single-dose versus two-dose vac-cination, and the implications of HIV co-infection. Although HPV16 and HPV18 remain the predominant oncogenic types, HPV35, HPV52, HPV45, HPV33, and HPV58 are re-ported frequently in sub-Saharan African populations. Consequently, some high-risk infections fall outside bivalent and quadrivalent vaccine coverage, and some detected types remain outside nonavalent vaccine coverage. Randomized trials in Tanzania and Kenya demonstrate durable single-dose immunogenicity through 60 months and vac-cine efficacy above 98% against persistent vaccine-type infection through 54 months, respectively. The ESCUDDO trial in Costa Rica provides a direct five-year randomized comparison showing noninferiority of one dose to two doses. Equivalent single-dose efficacy and durability evidence for people living with HIV remains unavailable. These findings support expanded single-dose vaccination in immunocompetent populations alongside multidose pathways for people living with HIV, genotype-specific surveil-lance, and continued cervical screening.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Cervical cancer is a major cause of morbidity and mortality among women in sub-Saharan Africa, where vaccination coverage remains below the World Health Organization target of 90% of girls fully vaccinated by 15 years of age. This narrative review synthesizes evidence on HPV genotype distribution, single-dose versus two-dose vaccination, and the implications of HIV co-infection.
Evidence base and method
Randomized trials in Tanzania and Kenya demonstrate durable single-dose immunogenicity through 60 months and vaccine efficacy above 98% against persistent vaccine-type infection through 54 months, respectively. The ESCUDDO trial in Costa Rica provides a direct five-year randomized comparison showing noninferiority of one dose to two doses.
Consensus and conclusions
Although HPV16 and HPV18 remain the predominant oncogenic types, HPV35, HPV52, HPV45, HPV33, and HPV58 are reported frequently in sub-Saharan African populations. Equivalent single-dose efficacy and durability evidence for people living with HIV remains unavailable.
Controversies and gaps
Consequently, some high-risk infections fall outside bivalent and quadrivalent vaccine coverage, and some detected types remain outside nonavalent vaccine coverage.
Research and practice implications
These findings support expanded single-dose vaccination in immunocompetent populations alongside multidose pathways for people living with HIV, genotype-specific surveillance, and continued cervical screening.

尿液和经血作为 人乳头瘤病毒 检测的无创替代品:文献综述

Urine and menstrual blood as noninvasive alternatives for human papillomavirus detection: a literature review
作者： Renatta Pitol-Martínez, Karla A. Arellano-Jaras, Alfonso Ruiz-Romero, Karen Gutiérrez-Calderón, Ximena S. Nava-Torres, Juan P. de Ita-Bonilla, Osman J. Gudiño-Flores, Alejandra Hernández-Padilla, Priscila B. López-Cebreros, Diego Coutiño-Borraz
摘要中文翻译目前,包括墨西哥在内的中低收入国家仍在使用宫颈涂片或巴氏涂片作为早期宫颈癌及其前驱病变检测的首选筛查测试。此外,还有一级预防计划,包括针对人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的疫苗接种,针对女孩和男孩以及某些弱势群体。作为解决和消除这一健康问题的努力的一部分,各个发达国家的研究网络正在重点研究有助于解决这一问题的新程序,除了巴氏涂片检测外,他们已经在常规地进行分子 HPV 检测。然而,分子或生化检测的用品和训练有素的人员的可用性以及心理社会障碍可能会带来一些挑战。因此,有必要在具有不同文化背景的低收入国家实施采集人体体液样本和进行分子 HPV 检测的新方法。在这种情况下,非侵入性自我采集样本可能是检测主要病因的可行、负担得起且准确的解决方案。宫颈病变;因此,早期发现这些病症可能会产生全球影响。本次审查提供了支持和证明现有替代方案的数据;它不是一种时尚;这是必要的。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 及其与 宫颈癌 及相关疾病的关联。核心问题是尿液和经血是否可以作为 HPV 检测的非侵入性替代方案,特别是在仍广泛使用巴氏试验等传统方法的低收入​​和中等收入国家。主题范围仅限于 HPV 类型和明确与 HPV 相关的疾病,不包括一般癌症,除非它们与 HPV 特别相关。
证据基础与方法
该综述是一篇文献综述,提供了支持使用非侵入性样本采集方法进行 HPV 检测的数据。它没有明确报告所使用的数据库、检索日期或纳入研究的资格标准。审查方法是叙述性的,没有提及荟萃分析或系统审查技术。
共识与结论
尿液和经血等非侵入性自我采集方法可能是低收入国家检测 HPV 的可行、负担得起且准确的解决方案。它强调了这些方法在改善宫颈病变的早期检测和减少与传统巴氏试验相关的障碍方面的重要性。
争议与缺口
该审查承认,分子 HPV 检测的用品和训练有素的人员的可用性仍然是低收入国家的一个挑战。此外,它指出社会心理障碍可能会阻碍这些新方法的采用。没有明确提及相互矛盾的发现或未解决的机制,但该评论强调需要进一步研究来解决这些局限性。
科研与实践启示
审查表明,在低收入国家实施样本采集和分子 HPV 检测的新方法可能会对全球产生重大影响。这意味着需要进行研究和开发,以提高这些测试的可及性和可负担性,并解决采用这些测试的社会心理障碍。

Urine and menstrual blood as noninvasive alternatives for human papillomavirus detection: a literature review

Authors: Renatta Pitol-Martínez, Karla A. Arellano-Jaras, Alfonso Ruiz-Romero, Karen Gutiérrez-Calderón, Ximena S. Nava-Torres, Juan P. de Ita-Bonilla, Osman J. Gudiño-Flores, Alejandra Hernández-Padilla, Priscila B. López-Cebreros, Diego Coutiño-Borraz
Original abstract (English)
At present, middle-and low-income countries including Mexico, are still using cervical smear or Pap as the first screening test for early cervical cancer and its precursor lesions detection.In addition, there are primary prevention programs that include vaccination against human papillomavirus (HPV), targeting girls and boys, as well as certain vulnerable groups.As part of the efforts to address and eliminate this health problem, research networks in various developed countries are focusing on new procedures that can help resolve this issue, and, in addition to the Pap test, they are already routinely performing molecular HPV testing.However, the availability of supplies and trained personnel for molecular or biochemical tests, as well as the psychosocial obstacles could be some challenges.Therefore, it is essential to implement novel methods for the collection of human fluids sample and for conducting molecular HPV tests in low-income countries with diverse cultural contexts.In this context, non-invasive self-collection of the sample could be a viable, affordable, and accurate solution for detecting the primary etiological factor of cervical lesions; thus, the early detection of those pathologies would potentially have a global impact.This review presents data supporting and justifying the present alternatives; it is not a fashion; it is a necessity.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on human papillomaviruses (HPV) and their association with cervical cancer and related diseases. The central question is whether urine and menstrual blood can serve as non-invasive alternatives for HPV detection, particularly in low- and middle-income countries where traditional methods like Pap tests are still widely used. The topic boundary is restricted to HPV types and explicitly HPV-associated diseases, excluding generic cancers unless they are specifically linked to HPV.
Evidence base and method
The review is a literature review that presents data supporting the use of non-invasive sample collection methods for HPV detection. It does not explicitly report on the databases used, search dates, or eligibility criteria for included studies. The review method is narrative, with no mention of meta-analysis or systematic review techniques.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that non-invasive self-collection methods, such as urine and menstrual blood, could be a viable, affordable, and accurate solution for detecting HPV in low-income countries. It emphasizes the importance of these methods in improving early detection of cervical lesions and reducing the barriers associated with traditional Pap tests.
Controversies and gaps
The review acknowledges that the availability of supplies and trained personnel for molecular HPV testing remains a challenge in low-income countries. Additionally, it notes that psychosocial obstacles may hinder the adoption of these new methods. There is no explicit mention of conflicting findings or unresolved mechanisms, but the review highlights the need for further research to address these limitations.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that implementing novel methods for sample collection and molecular HPV testing in low-income countries could have a significant global impact. It implies the need for research and development to improve accessibility and affordability of these tests, as well as to address psychosocial barriers to their adoption.

HIV 感染者和 HIV 感染风险增加人群中与口腔 HPV 感染和清除相关的因素:系统评价和荟萃分析

Factors associated with oral HPV infection and clearance in people living with HIV and populations at increased risk of HIV acquisition: a systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Mengjie Lin, Haibo Jiang, Lixia Ye, Shiwen Tan, Kun Chu, Zehao Ye, Pingping Xu, Yiya Wang, Yi Chen, Zhixin Zhu
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
背景 口腔 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染先于大多数 HPV 相关口咽癌,但 HIV 感染者 (PLWH) 和 HIV 感染风险增加人群中口腔 HPV 患病率 和 发病率 的决定因素仍不清楚。
证据基础与方法
结果 纳入了 27 项研究,累计报告样本量为 13,625 名参与者,其中 25 项参与了荟萃分析。
共识与结论
For oral HPV incidence, more recent oral-sex partners (n = 3; HR, 1.88; 95% CI, 1.41–2.49), more lifetime oral-sex partners (n = 3; HR, 2.19; 95% CI, 1.03–4.68), HIV infection (n = 2; HR, 1.90; 95% CI, 1.20–3.02), lower CD4 count (n = 4; HR, 1.85; 95% CI, 1.43–2.40), and higher HIV viral load (n = 2; HR, 2.51; 95% CI, 1.28–4.93) were significantly associated with increased incidence, whereas smoking and alcohol were not.
争议与缺口
证据的质量从低到极低不等。
科研与实践启示
提供的证据没有明确说明具体的研究或实践意义。

Factors associated with oral HPV infection and clearance in people living with HIV and populations at increased risk of HIV acquisition: a systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Mengjie Lin, Haibo Jiang, Lixia Ye, Shiwen Tan, Kun Chu, Zehao Ye, Pingping Xu, Yiya Wang, Yi Chen, Zhixin Zhu
Original abstract (English)
Background Oral human papillomavirus (HPV) infection precedes most HPV-related oropharyngeal cancers, but determinants of oral HPV prevalence and incidence in people living with HIV (PLWH) and populations at increased risk of HIV acquisition remain unclear. Methods PubMed, Embase, and the Cochrane Library were searched through July 10, 2026, for observational studies of laboratory-confirmed oral HPV prevalence, incidence, or clearance in adults with or at risk for HIV. Adjusted estimates were pooled by random-effects meta-analysis and assessed with GRADE. Results Twenty-seven studies with a cumulative reported sample size of 13,625 participants were included, of which 25 contributed to the meta-analysis. For oral HPV prevalence, smoking (n = 8; OR, 2.11; 95% CI, 1.58–2.83), HIV infection (n = 6; OR, 2.44; 95% CI, 1.69–3.52), and lower CD4 count (n = 8; OR, 3.02; 95% CI, 2.10–4.35) were significantly associated with higher odds, whereas alcohol, recent oral-sex partners, and HIV viral load were not. For oral HPV incidence, more recent oral-sex partners (n = 3; HR, 1.88; 95% CI, 1.41–2.49), more lifetime oral-sex partners (n = 3; HR, 2.19; 95% CI, 1.03–4.68), HIV infection (n = 2; HR, 1.90; 95% CI, 1.20–3.02), lower CD4 count (n = 4; HR, 1.85; 95% CI, 1.43–2.40), and higher HIV viral load (n = 2; HR, 2.51; 95% CI, 1.28–4.93) were significantly associated with increased incidence, whereas smoking and alcohol were not. HIV infection showed a borderline association with reduced clearance (n = 3; HR, 0.43; 95% CI, 0.19–1.00). Certainty of evidence ranged from low to very low. Conclusions Smoking was associated with oral HPV prevalence, whereas greater oral-sex exposure and higher HIV viral load were more clearly associated with incidence. HIV infection and lower CD4 counts were associated with both outcomes, while evidence for clearance remained inconclusive. Further prospective studies are needed.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Background Oral human papillomavirus (HPV) infection precedes most HPV-related oropharyngeal cancers, but determinants of oral HPV prevalence and incidence in people living with HIV (PLWH) and populations at increased risk of HIV acquisition remain unclear.
Evidence base and method
Results Twenty-seven studies with a cumulative reported sample size of 13,625 participants were included, of which 25 contributed to the meta-analysis.
Consensus and conclusions
For oral HPV incidence, more recent oral-sex partners (n = 3; HR, 1.88; 95% CI, 1.41–2.49), more lifetime oral-sex partners (n = 3; HR, 2.19; 95% CI, 1.03–4.68), HIV infection (n = 2; HR, 1.90; 95% CI, 1.20–3.02), lower CD4 count (n = 4; HR, 1.85; 95% CI, 1.43–2.40), and higher HIV viral load (n = 2; HR, 2.51; 95% CI, 1.28–4.93) were significantly associated with increased incidence, whereas smoking and alcohol were not.
Controversies and gaps
Certainty of evidence ranged from low to very low.
Research and practice implications
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a specific research or practice implication.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

病毒入侵:揭示 人乳头瘤病毒 在原发性肺癌中的作用——系统评价和荟萃分析

Viral Intrusion: Unveiling the Role of Human Papillomavirus in Primary Lung Cancer – A Systematic Review and Meta-analysis
作者： Benedikta Alverin, Muhammad Nur Imaduddin Suma, Aulia Fitri Rhamadianti Rhamadianti, Dian Eurike Septyaningtrias, Erlina Burhan
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 在肺癌发生中的作用仍存在争议。这项系统回顾和荟萃分析评估了 HPV 感染与肺癌风险之间的关联及其潜在的预后影响。方法:对 PubMed、ScienceDirect 和 Google Scholar (2015-2025) 进行系统检索,确定了报告肺癌病例和非癌症对照中 HPV 状态的观察性研究。使用随机效应模型计算具有 95% 置信区间 (CI) 的合并比值比 (OR)。使用 I2 统计量评估异质性,并使用漏斗图和 Egger 检验检查发表偏倚。对缺乏对照组或效应大小的研究进行了叙述性总结。结果:荟萃分析纳入了 9 项研究(n = 3,871,842),显示汇总 OR 为 2.62(95% CI:1.86–3.68),表明 HPV 感染者患肺癌的几率较高。观察到显着的异质性(I2 = 83.3%,p < 0.001),并检测到发表偏倚(Egger 检验 p = 0.015)。另外还叙述性地综合了 26 项研究,其中许多研究检测肺部肿瘤中的 HPV DNA(主要是 16 型和 18 型),特别是在非吸烟者、女性和 EGFR 突变的腺癌中。还观察到地理起源、组织学、检测方法和标本类型的显着差异。结论:HPV 感染与肺癌风险增加显着相关,表明其可能作为风险和预后生物标志物。然而,发表偏倚和异质性的存在强调需要进一步进行大规模标准化研究来验证这些发现。

范围与问题

HPV 感染与肺癌风险增加显着相关,表明其可能作为风险和预后生物标志物。

Viral Intrusion: Unveiling the Role of Human Papillomavirus in Primary Lung Cancer – A Systematic Review and Meta-analysis

Authors: Benedikta Alverin, Muhammad Nur Imaduddin Suma, Aulia Fitri Rhamadianti Rhamadianti, Dian Eurike Septyaningtrias, Erlina Burhan
Original abstract (English)
Background : The role of human papillomavirus (HPV) in lung carcinogenesis remains debated. This systematic review and meta-analysis evaluated the association between HPV infection and lung cancer risk and its potential prognostic implications. Methods: A systematic search of PubMed, ScienceDirect, and Google Scholar (2015–2025) identified observational studies reporting HPV status in both lung cancer cases and non-cancer controls. Pooled odds ratios (ORs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were calculated using a random-effects model. Heterogeneity was assessed with the I2 statistic, and publication bias was examined using funnel plot and Egger’s test. Studies lacking control groups or effect sizes were narratively summarized. Results: Nine studies (n = 3,871,842) were included in the meta-analysis, showing a pooled OR of 2.62 (95% CI: 1.86–3.68), indicating higher odds of lung cancer among HPV-infected individuals. Substantial heterogeneity was observed (I2 = 83.3%, p < 0.001) and publication bias was detected (Egger’s test p = 0.015). An additional 26 studies were narratively synthesized, many detecting HPV DNA mainly types 16 and 18 in lung tumors, particularly among non-smokers, females, and EGFR-mutated adenocarcinomas. Considerable variation in geographic origin, histology, detection method, and specimen type was also observed. Conclusion: HPV infection is significantly associated with an increased risk of lung cancer, suggesting its possible role as a risk and prognostic biomarker. However, the presence of publication bias and heterogeneity underscores the need for further large-scale, standardized studies to validate these findings.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The role of human papillomavirus (HPV) in lung carcinogenesis remains debated.
Evidence base and method
This systematic review and meta-analysis evaluated the association between HPV infection and lung cancer risk and its potential prognostic implications. A systematic search of PubMed, ScienceDirect, and Google Scholar (2015–2025) identified observational studies reporting HPV status in both lung cancer cases and non-cancer controls. Pooled odds ratios (ORs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were calculated using a random-effects model. Heterogeneity was assessed with the I2 statistic, and publication bias was examined using funnel plot and Egger’s test. Studies lacking control groups or effect sizes were narratively summarized.
Consensus and conclusions
Nine studies (n = 3,871,842) were included in the meta-analysis, showing a pooled OR of 2.62 (95% CI: 1.86–3.68), indicating higher odds of lung cancer among HPV-infected individuals. Considerable variation in geographic origin, histology, detection method, and specimen type was also observed.
Controversies and gaps
Substantial heterogeneity was observed (I2 = 83.3%, p < 0.001) and publication bias was detected (Egger’s test p = 0.015). The presence of publication bias and heterogeneity underscores the need for further large-scale, standardized studies to validate these findings.
Research and practice implications
HPV infection is significantly associated with an increased risk of lung cancer, suggesting its possible role as a risk and prognostic biomarker.

人乳头瘤病毒 相关多表型鼻窦癌:系统评价

Human Papillomavirus-Related Multiphenotypic Sinonasal Carcinoma: A Systematic Review
作者： Leong Rachel Wen Ge, Guinevere Renee Jiezi Tan, Siow Jin Keat, Bundele Manish Mahadeorao, Jian Li Tan, L. Wenge

查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 人乳头瘤病毒 相关的多表型鼻窦癌 (HMSC),并评估此类肿瘤的结果,以及切除后辅助 治疗 的适应症,以确定最佳治疗策略。核心问题是关于 HMSC 的临床行为和 治疗 方法。
证据基础与方法
该综述使用“鼻旁窦肿瘤”和“乳头瘤病毒感染”等关键词搜索了 PubMed、Embase 和 Ovid (MEDLINE) 数据库。它包括 病例报告、病例系列、文献综述和手术报告。总共分析了 31 篇文章,报告了 119 例 HMSC 病例。
共识与结论
大多数 HMSC 病例在最后一次随访期间没有表现出疾病证据。内窥镜手术仍然是 治疗 的支柱,无论 HMSC 分期如何,辅助 治疗 术后切除的比例相似。尽管具有高级组织学特征,但明确的 治疗 后的结果是有利的。
争议与缺口
佐剂 治疗 在降低复发率方面的长期作用尚未讨论。对于辅助 治疗 的适应症没有明确的指导,决策基于是否存在高风险临床特征。审查还指出,证据有限,需要进一步调查。
科研与实践启示
需要关于 HMSC 辅助 治疗 适应症的明确指南。需要进一步的研究来了解佐剂 治疗 在降低复发率方面的长期作用。个体化的 治疗 决策应基于切缘、侵袭和患者因素。

Human Papillomavirus-Related Multiphenotypic Sinonasal Carcinoma: A Systematic Review

Authors: Leong Rachel Wen Ge, Guinevere Renee Jiezi Tan, Siow Jin Keat, Bundele Manish Mahadeorao, Jian Li Tan, L. Wenge
Original abstract (English)
A systematic review was conducted. https://doi.org/10.7759/cureus.40413 - Miyamaru S, Sanuki T, Miyamoto Y et al (2023) Human papillomavirus-related multiphenotypic sinonasal carcinoma: a report of two patients and review of the literature. https://doi.org/10.1111/neup.12762 - Ward ML, Kernig M, Willson TJ (2021) HPV-related multiphenotypic sinonasal carcinoma: a case report and literature review. A case report and literature review. https://doi.org/10.1007/s12105-019-01079-1 - Adamane SA, Mittal N, Teni T, Pawar S, Waghole R, Bal M (2019) Human papillomavirus-related multiphenotypic sinonasal carcinoma with unique HPV type 52 association: a case report with review of literature. https://doi.org/10.1016/j.otorri.2018.02.005 - Ruangritchankul K, Jitpasutham T, Kitkumthorn N, Thorner PS, Keelawat S (2018) Human papillomavirus-related multiphenotypic sinonasal carcinoma: first case report associated with an intermediate-risk HPV type and literature review. Abstract To establish outcomes of Human Papillomavirus-Related Multiphenotypic Sinonasal Carcinoma and evaluate indications for adjuvant treatment after resection to determine optimal management of such tumors. The databases searched were PubMed, Embase, Ovid (MEDLINE), and Cochrane for articles up to June 19, 2026. PubMed, Embase, and Ovid (MEDLINE) databases were searched, utilizing keywords such as “Paranasal Sinus Neoplasms” and “Papillomavirus infections”. Reference lists were searched for identification of relevant studies. Case reports, case series, literature reviews, and surgical reports were included. Adjuvant treatment post-surgical resection...
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on Human Papillomavirus-Related Multiphenotypic Sinonasal Carcinoma (HMSC) and evaluates the outcomes of such tumors, as well as indications for adjuvant treatment after resection to determine optimal management strategies. The central question is about the clinical behavior and treatment approaches for HMSC.
Evidence base and method
The review searched PubMed, Embase, and Ovid (MEDLINE) databases using keywords like 'Paranasal Sinus Neoplasms' and 'Papillomavirus infections'. It included case reports, case series, literature reviews, and surgical reports. A total of 31 articles were analyzed, with 119 HMSC cases reported.
Consensus and conclusions
The majority of HMSC cases showed no evidence of disease during their last follow-up. Endoscopic surgery remains the mainstay of treatment, and adjuvant treatment post-surgical resection was administered at similar rates regardless of HMSC staging. Despite high-grade histological features, outcomes following definitive treatment are favorable.
Controversies and gaps
The long-term role of adjuvant treatment in reducing recurrence rates has not been discussed. There is no clear guidance on indications for adjuvant treatment, and decisions are based on the presence of high-risk clinical features. The review also notes that the evidence is limited and requires further investigation.
Research and practice implications
There is a need for clear guidelines on adjuvant treatment indications for HMSC. Further research is required to understand the long-term role of adjuvant treatment in reducing recurrence rates. Individualized treatment decisions should be based on margins, invasion, and patient factors.

针对 HPV 相关宫颈阴道菌群失调的序贯微生物组疗法。

Towards a sequential microbiome therapy for HPV-associated cervicovaginal dysbiosis.
作者： Mariano A. Molina, Yi-Chan Zhang, Renske D. M. Steenbergen, Willem J. G. Melchers, Juan Du
摘要中文翻译摘要 持续高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,并且与宫颈阴道微生物群的生态不稳定越来越相关。多组学研究表明,hrHPV 的持续存在与乳杆菌优势减少、厌氧类群增加以及可能损害上皮完整性和 抗病毒药物 免疫力的代谢改变有关。在这篇观点文章中,我们介绍了一种序贯微生物组疗法(SMT)框架,旨在恢复宫颈阴道生态平衡并支持病毒清除。SMT 是一个两阶段的应变解决策略。第一阶段针对生态失调群落,使用抗菌药物、生物膜破坏或微生物群调节方法,为生态重置创造一个窗口。第二阶段引入了与健康相关的乳酸菌物种,这些乳酸菌物种因其酸化能力、竞争适应性和稳定粘膜环境的能力而被选择。我们概述了支持 SMT 的生态原则,并讨论了其作为一种潜在可扩展的微生物组导向策略的发展,用于在现有的 宫颈癌 预防框架内恢复宫颈阴道健康和管理 hrHPV 相关疾病。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
持续的高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,并且与宫颈阴道微生物群的生态不稳定越来越相关。
证据基础与方法
多组学研究表明,hrHPV 的持续存在与乳杆菌优势减少、厌氧类群增加以及可能损害上皮完整性和 抗病毒药物 免疫力的代谢改变有关。
共识与结论
在这篇观点文章中,我们介绍了一种序贯微生物组疗法(SMT)框架,旨在恢复宫颈阴道生态平衡并支持病毒清除。
争议与缺口
SMT 是一个两阶段的应变解决策略。第一阶段针对生态失调群落,使用抗菌药物、生物膜破坏或微生物群调节方法,为生态重置创造一个窗口。
科研与实践启示
第二阶段引入了与健康相关的乳酸菌物种,这些乳酸菌物种因其酸化能力、竞争适应性和稳定粘膜环境的能力而被选择。

Towards a sequential microbiome therapy for HPV-associated cervicovaginal dysbiosis.

Authors: Mariano A. Molina, Yi-Chan Zhang, Renske D. M. Steenbergen, Willem J. G. Melchers, Juan Du
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) infections are the principal cause of cervical cancer, and are increasingly associated with ecological instability of the cervicovaginal microbiome. Multiomics studies indicate that hrHPV persistence is linked to reduced Lactobacillus dominance, increased anaerobic taxa, and metabolic alterations that may compromise epithelial integrity and antiviral immunity. In this Perspective article, we introduce a sequential microbiome therapy (SMT) framework designed to restore cervicovaginal ecological balance and support viral clearance. SMT is a two-phase, strain-resolved strategy. The first phase targets dysbiotic communities using antimicrobials, biofilm-disrupting or microbiota-modulating approaches to create a window for ecological reset. The second phase introduces health-associated Lactobacillus species selected for their acidification capacity, competitive fitness, and ability to stabilize the mucosal environment. We outline the ecological principles underpinning SMT and discuss its development as a potentially scalable microbiome-directed strategy for restoring cervicovaginal health and managing hrHPV-associated disease within existing cervical cancer prevention frameworks.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) infections are the principal cause of cervical cancer, and are increasingly associated with ecological instability of the cervicovaginal microbiome.
Evidence base and method
Multiomics studies indicate that hrHPV persistence is linked to reduced Lactobacillus dominance, increased anaerobic taxa, and metabolic alterations that may compromise epithelial integrity and antiviral immunity.
Consensus and conclusions
In this Perspective article, we introduce a sequential microbiome therapy (SMT) framework designed to restore cervicovaginal ecological balance and support viral clearance.
Controversies and gaps
SMT is a two-phase, strain-resolved strategy. The first phase targets dysbiotic communities using antimicrobials, biofilm-disrupting or microbiota-modulating approaches to create a window for ecological reset.
Research and practice implications
The second phase introduces health-associated Lactobacillus species selected for their acidification capacity, competitive fitness, and ability to stabilize the mucosal environment.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

[头颈癌免疫治疗的最新进展:2026 年 ASCO 年会的新方面]。

[Update on immunotherapy in head and neck cancer : New aspects from the ASCO annual meeting 2026].
作者： Matthias Brand, Thomas Karl Hoffmann, Simon A. Laban
摘要中文翻译背景:针对 PD-1/PD-L1 的免疫检查点抑制剂 (ICIs) 重塑了头颈鳞状细胞癌的 治疗,但持久益处仍然仅限于选定的亚组。新的围手术期和姑息治疗策略,包括双特异性和基于 T 细胞受体 (TCR) 的方法,旨在克服免疫抑制障碍并改善长期控制。目的:系统回顾了 2026 年美国临床肿瘤学会 (ASCO) 年会上提出的头颈癌免疫治疗试验,并分析了它们对一线和复发/转移环境中当前标准和未来策略的影响。材料和方法:根据 ASCO 2026 上的摘要和演讲,对基于 PD-1/PD-L1 的疗法、双特异性抗体以及头颈癌治疗和姑息性创新策略的临床试验进行了回顾。结果:在治疗环境中,基于帕博利珠单抗的围手术期和辅助 PD-1 策略提供了一致的生存获益,并减少了病理高风险特征,同时保留了手术根治性。新辅助双特异性 PD-1/血管内皮生长因子 (VEGF) 阻断联合化疗实现了高缓解率和器官保留切除,而辅助 CD40 靶向 疫苗接种 在 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)16 阳性口咽头颈鳞状细胞癌中诱导了强大、持久的 T 细胞反应。在复发/转移性疾病中,基于 PD-1 的靶向药物组合可改善肿瘤反应、疾病控制和生存。双特异性表皮生长因子受体 (EGFR) 导向抗体和基于 TCR 的双特异性 T 细胞接合受体 (TCER) IMA401 在 PD-1 失败后显示出有意义的活性。结论:这些数据支持将免疫肿瘤学概念扩展到围手术期和姑息治疗环境中。双特异性和基于 TCR 的策略标志着从传统 ICI 单一疗法向更加差异化、机制驱动的联合疗法的转变。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
靶向 PD-1/PD-L1 的免疫检查点抑制剂 (ICIs) 重塑了头颈鳞状细胞癌的 治疗,但持久益处仍然仅限于选定的亚组。
证据基础与方法
根据 ASCO 2026 上的摘要和演示,对基于 PD-1/PD-L1 的疗法、双特异性抗体以及头颈癌治疗和姑息性创新策略的临床试验进行了回顾。
共识与结论
在治疗环境中,围手术期和基于 PD-1 的派姆单抗辅助策略提供了一致的生存获益并减少了病理高风险特征,同时保留了手术根治性。在复发/转移性疾病中,基于 PD-1 的靶向药物组合可改善肿瘤反应、疾病控制和生存。双特异性表皮生长因子受体 (EGFR) 导向抗体和基于 TCR 的双特异性 T 细胞接合受体 (TCER) IMA401 在 PD-1 失败后显示出有意义的活性。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据并未报告与 人乳头瘤病毒 相关的争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
这些数据支持将免疫肿瘤学概念扩展到围手术期和姑息治疗环境中。双特异性和基于 TCR 的策略标志着从传统 ICI 单一疗法向更加差异化、机制驱动的联合疗法的转变。

[Update on immunotherapy in head and neck cancer : New aspects from the ASCO annual meeting 2026].

Authors: Matthias Brand, Thomas Karl Hoffmann, Simon A. Laban
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICIs) targeting PD-1/PD-L1 have reshaped the treatment of head and neck squamous cell carcinoma, but durable benefit remains limited to selected subgroups. New perioperative and palliative strategies, including bispecific and T‐cell receptor (TCR)-based approaches, aim to overcome immunosuppressive barriers and improve long-term control. OBJECTIVE: Immunotherapy trials in head and neck cancer presented at the 2026 American Society of Clinical Oncology (ASCO) Annual Meeting were systematically reviewed and their impact on current standards and future strategies in frontline and recurrent/metastatic settings was analyzed. MATERIALS AND METHODS: Clinical trials of PD-1/PD-L1-based therapies, bispecific antibodies, and innovative strategies in curative and palliative head and neck cancer were reviewed, based on abstracts and presentations at ASCO 2026. RESULTS: In the curative setting, a perioperative and adjuvant PD-1-based strategy with pembrolizumab provided consistent survival benefits and reduced pathological high-risk features while preserving surgical radicality. Neoadjuvant bispecific PD-1/vascular endothelial growth factor (VEGF) blockade combined with chemotherapy achieved high response rates and organ-preserving resections, whereas adjuvant CD40-targeted vaccination in human papillomavirus (HPV)16-positive oropharyngeal head and neck squamous cell carcinoma induced robust, durable T‐cell responses. In the recurrent/metastatic setting, PD-1-based combinations with targeted agents improved tumor response, disease control, and survival. Bispecific epidermal growth factor receptor (EGFR)-directed antibodies and the TCR-based bispecific T‐cell engaging receptor (TCER) IMA401 showed meaningful activity after PD‐1 failure. CONCLUSION: These data support the expansion of immuno-oncologic concepts into perioperative and palliative settings. Bispecific and TCR-based strategies signal a shift from conventional ICI monotherapy toward more differentiated, mechanism-driven combination approaches.

HPV相关细胞外囊泡和肿瘤微环境:免疫调节机制和转化意义

HPV-associated extracellular vesicles and the tumor microenvironment: mechanisms of immune modulation and translational implications
作者： Masoud Goodarzi, Parisa Shiri Aghbash, Hossein Bannazadeh Baghi
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,也与多种肛门生殖器和口咽恶性肿瘤有关。高危型HPV 类型的持续感染和病毒癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 的持续表达有助于恶性转化。细胞外囊泡越来越被认为是细胞间通讯的重要介质,可以在细胞之间转移蛋白质、核酸、脂质和其他调节分子。EV 货物中与 HPV 相关的变化(例如病毒转录本)可能会影响肿瘤微环境中的免疫细胞和基质细胞。实验研究表明,HPV 相关的 EV 参与树突状细胞功能、巨噬细胞极化、T 细胞反应、免疫检查点信号传导、慢性炎症以及与病毒持久性和免疫逃避相关的机制的调节。基于细胞和临床前的研究还表明,HPV 阳性肿瘤细胞释放的 EV 可能导致相关疾病,包括血管生成和转移进展。另一方面,人类研究主要提供了将 EV 相关分子谱与 HPV 状态或疾病特征联系起来的观察证据。除了这些机制见解之外,越来越多的工作探索了 EV 的转化潜力,包括它们作为液体活检生物标志物和作为工程递送平台的用途,例如携带 HPV16 E7 的基于 Nefmut 的囊泡,可在临床前模型中引发保护性 CD8 + T 细胞反应。这篇叙述性综述的重点是肿瘤-免疫相互作用,还讨论了新兴的诊断和治疗应用,以及在基于 EV 的策略转化为常规实践之前必须解决的方法学和临床障碍。

范围与问题
人乳头瘤病毒 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,也与多种肛门生殖器和口咽恶性肿瘤有关。高危型HPV 类型的持续感染和病毒癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 的持续表达有助于恶性转化。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述的重点是肿瘤-免疫相互作用,还讨论了新兴的诊断和治疗应用,以及在基于 EV 的策略转化为常规实践之前必须解决的方法学和临床障碍。
共识与结论
细胞外囊泡越来越被认为是细胞间通讯的重要介质,可以在细胞之间转移蛋白质、核酸、脂质和其他调节分子。EV 货物中与 HPV 相关的变化(例如病毒转录本)可能会影响肿瘤微环境中的免疫细胞和基质细胞。实验研究表明,HPV 相关的 EV 参与树突状细胞功能、巨噬细胞极化、T 细胞反应、免疫检查点信号传导、慢性炎症以及与病毒持久性和免疫逃避相关的机制的调节。
争议与缺口
人类研究主要提供了将 EV 相关分子谱与 HPV 状态或疾病特征联系起来的观察证据。基于细胞和临床前的研究还表明,HPV 阳性肿瘤细胞释放的 EV 可能导致相关疾病,包括血管生成和转移进展。
科研与实践启示
除了这些机制见解之外,越来越多的工作探索了 EV 的转化潜力,包括它们作为液体活检生物标志物和作为工程递送平台的用途,例如携带 HPV16 E7 的基于 Nefmut 的囊泡,可在临床前模型中引发保护性 CD8 + T 细胞反应。

HPV-associated extracellular vesicles and the tumor microenvironment: mechanisms of immune modulation and translational implications

Authors: Masoud Goodarzi, Parisa Shiri Aghbash, Hossein Bannazadeh Baghi
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus infections are a major cause of cervical cancer and are also associated with several anogenital and oropharyngeal malignancies. Persistent infection with high-risk HPV types and sustained expression of viral oncoproteins E6 and E7 contribute to malignant transformation. Extracellular vesicles are increasingly recognized as important mediators of intercellular communication and can transfer proteins, nucleic acids, lipids, and other regulatory molecules between cells. HPV-associated changes in EV cargo, such as viral transcripts, may influence immune and stromal cells within the tumor microenvironment. Experimental studies have implicated HPV-associated EVs in the regulation of dendritic cell function, macrophage polarization, T-cell responses, immune checkpoint signaling, chronic inflammation, and mechanisms related to viral persistence and immune evasion. Cell-based and preclinical studies also suggest that EVs released by HPV-positive tumor cells may contribute to related disorders including angiogenesis and metastatic progression. On the other hand, human studies have primarily provided observational evidence linking EV-associated molecular profiles with HPV status or disease characteristics. Beyond these mechanistic insights, a growing body of work explores the translational potential of EVs, including their use as liquid-biopsy biomarkers and as engineered delivery platforms such as Nefmut-based vesicles carrying HPV16 E7 that elicit protective CD8 + T-cell responses in preclinical models. This narrative review, with emphasis on tumor-immune interactions, and also discusses emerging diagnostic and therapeutic applications together with the methodological and clinical barriers that must be addressed before EV-based strategies can be translated into routine practice.

HPV-associated changes in EV cargo, such as viral transcripts, may influence immune and stromal cells within the tumor microenvironment. Experimental studies have implicated HPV-associated EVs in the regulation of dendritic cell function, macrophage polarization, T-cell responses, immune checkpoint signaling, chronic inflammation, and mechanisms related to viral persistence and immune evasion.
Controversies and gaps
Human studies have primarily provided observational evidence linking EV-associated molecular profiles with HPV status or disease characteristics. Cell-based and preclinical studies also suggest that EVs released by HPV-positive tumor cells may contribute to related disorders including angiogenesis and metastatic progression.
Research and practice implications
Beyond these mechanistic insights, a growing body of work explores the translational potential of EVs, including their use as liquid-biopsy biomarkers and as engineered delivery platforms such as Nefmut-based vesicles carrying HPV16 E7 that elicit protective CD8 + T-cell responses in preclinical models.

头颈鳞状细胞癌西妥昔单抗耐药机制:关注 EGFR-ERK 通路。

Mechanisms of cetuximab resistance in head and neck squamous cell Carcinoma: Focus on the EGFR-ERK pathway.
作者： Liliya Galiyeva, Josipa Grušanović, Jiří Netušil, Vojtěch Melichar, Zuzana Klímová, Michal Kolář, Jan Plzák, Jana Šachová, David Kalfeřt, Tomáš Vomastek
摘要中文翻译头颈鳞状细胞癌(HNSCC)是全球第六大常见癌症。主要危险因素包括吸烟、过量饮酒和感染致癌 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)。尽管 治疗 取得了进展,但 HNSCC 仍然与不良预后相关,特别是对于患有晚期或复发/转移性疾病的患者。表皮生长因子受体 (EGFR) 已被认为是 HNSCC 的关键致癌驱动因素,因为它经常过度表达并促进多种促肿瘤信号通路的激活,包括 ERK 和 PI3K/Akt。西妥昔单抗仍然是唯一被批准针对 HNSCC 肿瘤相关受体的分子靶向药物。然而,其临床益处受到低反应率和频繁产生耐药性的限制,即使在最初有反应的肿瘤中也是如此。因此,了解 EGFR 靶向治疗耐药的分子基础对于开发更有效的药理学策略和提高西妥昔单抗的临床应用至关重要。在这篇综述中,我们总结了 EGFR 改变、平行补偿途径的激活以及下游信号级联(包括 PI3K/Akt 和 Ras/ERK)的重新激活,作为 HNSCC EGFR 靶向治疗耐药的主要机制。我们特别关注 ERK 信号通路作为遗传、表观遗传和适应性抵抗机制的中心整合器。持续的 ERK 活性恢复下游 EGFR 输出,促进反馈配体产生,驱动上皮间质转化和细胞可塑性,并参与平行的致癌途径,从而使肿瘤细胞能够逃避西妥昔单抗介导的 EGFR 阻断。因此,ERK 和 EGFR 的联合靶向可能代表了在选定的分子背景下克服西妥昔单抗耐药性的有前途的策略。

Mechanisms of cetuximab resistance in head and neck squamous cell Carcinoma: Focus on the EGFR-ERK pathway.

Authors: Liliya Galiyeva, Josipa Grušanović, Jiří Netušil, Vojtěch Melichar, Zuzana Klímová, Michal Kolář, Jan Plzák, Jana Šachová, David Kalfeřt, Tomáš Vomastek
Original abstract (English)
Head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC) is the sixth most common cancer worldwide. Major risk factors include tobacco use, excessive alcohol consumption, and infection with oncogenic human papillomaviruses (HPV). Despite advances in treatment, HNSCC remains associated with poor outcomes, particularly in patients with advanced or recurrent/metastatic disease. The epidermal growth factor receptor (EGFR) has been recognized as a key oncogenic driver in HNSCC, as it is frequently overexpressed and promotes activation of multiple pro-tumorigenic signaling pathways, including ERK and PI3K/Akt. Cetuximab remains the only approved molecularly targeted agent directed against a tumor-associated receptor in HNSCC. However, its clinical benefit is limited by low response rates and the frequent development of resistance even in initially responsive tumors. Understanding the molecular basis of resistance to EGFR-targeted therapy is therefore critical for developing more effective pharmacological strategies and improving the clinical utility of cetuximab. In this review, we summarize EGFR alterations, activation of parallel compensatory pathways, and the reactivation of downstream signaling cascades, including PI3K/Akt and Ras/ERK, as major mechanisms underlying resistance to EGFR-targeted therapy in HNSCC. We focus particularly on the ERK signaling pathway as a central integrator of genetic, epigenetic, and adaptive resistance mechanisms. Sustained ERK activity restores downstream EGFR outputs, promotes feedback ligand production, drives epithelial-mesenchymal transition and cellular plasticity, and engages parallel oncogenic pathways, thereby enabling tumor cells to evade cetuximab-mediated EGFR blockade. Combined targeting of ERK and EGFR may therefore represent a promising strategy for overcoming cetuximab resistance in selected molecular contexts.

The supplied evidence does not report controversies or evidence gaps related to human papillomaviruses.

The supplied evidence does not report research or practice implications for human papillomaviruses.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

治疗 后 监测 和经常性 宫颈癌 的管理 - 法国 宫颈癌 指南小组的审查。

POST-TREATMENT SURVEILLANCE AND MANAGEMENT OF RECURRENT CERVICAL CANCER - A REVIEW FROM THE FRENCH CERVICAL CANCER GUIDELINES GROUP.
作者： Aurélie Buffeteau, Pauline Chauvet, Abel Cordoba, Jean Levêque, Pascal Rousset, Isabelle Thomassin, Xavier Carcopino, Jean-Luc Brun, members from the FRENCH CERVICAL CANCER GUIDELINES GROUP
摘要中文翻译目的:回顾 治疗 后随访和 宫颈癌 后复发管理的现有证据。方法:我们对 监测 模式以及盆腔和淋巴结复发治疗的文献进行了叙述性回顾。结果:大多数复发发生在 治疗 治疗后三年内,但症状出现前检测的生存益处尚未得到证实。保留生育能力手术后,HPV 检测对于检测无症状盆腔复发非常敏感。子宫切除术后,大多数复发都是有症状的,临床检查比 HPV 检测或阴道穹窿细胞学检查具有更高的诊断率。同步放化疗(CRT)后,持续性 HPV 感染与复发风险增加相关,但其特异性有限且缺乏基于 HPV 的经过验证的 监测 策略限制了其临床应用。MRI 和 FDG PET/CT 可用于疗效评估和疑似复发调查,但缺乏支持无症状患者常规影像学检查的证据。密集的随访计划并没有改善复发检测或生存率。关于复发管理的证据主要是回顾性的。初次手术后盆腔复发可采用 CRT 治疗,并辅以近距离放射治疗治疗阴道受累。在 CRT 后出现中央盆腔复发的特定患者中,完全手术切除可能会延长生存期,但会带来较高的发病率。先前照射区域外的淋巴结复发已采用 CRT 进行治疗。结论:现有证据支持主要临床、治疗 适应的随访。强化 监测、常规成像或检测无症状复发的生存获益仍不确定。复发管理需要根据疾病程度、无病间隔和既往 治疗 进行个体化多学科评估。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述旨在评估治疗后的随访和复发管理策略,重点关注人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)在复发检测和治疗中的作用。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 监测 方法和复发管理文献的叙述性综合,重点是 HPV 检测和成像技术。它包括有关复发时间、HPV 检测在不同 治疗 背景下的效用以及成像方式的作用的证据。
共识与结论
证据表明,大多数 宫颈癌 复发发生在 治疗 的三年内,但早期检测的生存益处尚未得到证实。在检测子宫切除术后的复发方面,临床检查比 HPV 检测或阴道穹窿细胞学检查更有效,而 HPV 检测对保留生育手术后的无症状盆腔复发敏感。持续的 HPV 感染与 CRT 后复发风险增加有关,但由于特异性低且缺乏经过验证的策略,其临床实用性受到限制。
争议与缺口
对于无症状患者,强化 监测 或常规影像学检查的生存获益尚不确定。证据主要是回顾性的,缺乏可靠的数据支持对无症状个体进行常规影像学检查。此外,由于特异性有限且缺乏经过验证的策略,HPV 在 CRT 后复发管理中的作用尚未完全确定。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步的研究来确定基于 HPV 的 监测 策略的临床效用,并验证成像方式对无症状患者的有效性。实践应侧重于复发管理的个体化、多学科方法,考虑疾病范围、无病间隔和既往 治疗。

POST-TREATMENT SURVEILLANCE AND MANAGEMENT OF RECURRENT CERVICAL CANCER - A REVIEW FROM THE FRENCH CERVICAL CANCER GUIDELINES GROUP.

Authors: Aurélie Buffeteau, Pauline Chauvet, Abel Cordoba, Jean Levêque, Pascal Rousset, Isabelle Thomassin, Xavier Carcopino, Jean-Luc Brun, members from the FRENCH CERVICAL CANCER GUIDELINES GROUP
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To review the available evidence on post-treatment follow-up and recurrence management after cervical cancer. METHODS: We conducted a narrative review of the literature on surveillance modalities, and the management of pelvic and nodal recurrences. RESULTS: Most recurrences occur within three years of treatment, but the survival benefit of detection before symptom onset remains unproven. After fertility-sparing surgery, HPV testing is sensitive for detecting asymptomatic pelvic recurrence. After hysterectomy, most recurrences are symptomatic, and clinical examination has a higher diagnostic yield than HPV testing or vaginal vault cytology. After concurrent chemoradiotherapy (CRT), persistent HPV infection is associated with an increased recurrence risk, but its limited specificity and the absence of a validated HPV-based surveillance strategy restrict its clinical utility. MRI and FDG PET/CT are useful for response assessment and investigation of suspected recurrence, but evidence supporting routine imaging of asymptomatic patients is lacking. Intensive follow-up schedules have not improved recurrence detection or survival. Evidence on recurrence management is predominantly retrospective. Pelvic recurrences after primary surgery have been treated with CRT, supplemented by brachytherapy for vaginal involvement. In selected patients with central pelvic recurrence after CRT, complete surgical resection may provide prolonged survival, but carries substantial morbidity. Nodal recurrences outside previously irradiated fields have been treated with CRT. CONCLUSION: Available evidence supports a predominantly clinical, treatment-adapted follow-up. The survival benefit of intensive surveillance, routine imaging, or detecting asymptomatic recurrence remains uncertain. Recurrence management requires individualized multidisciplinary assessment based on disease extent, disease-free interval, and previous treatments.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review aims to evaluate post-treatment follow-up and recurrence management strategies for cervical cancer, focusing on the role of human papillomaviruses (HPV) in recurrence detection and treatment.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of the literature on surveillance methods and recurrence management, with an emphasis on HPV testing and imaging techniques. It includes evidence on the timing of recurrences, the utility of HPV testing in different treatment contexts, and the role of imaging modalities.
Consensus and conclusions
The evidence suggests that most cervical cancer recurrences occur within three years of treatment, but the survival benefit of early detection remains unproven. Clinical examination is more effective than HPV testing or vaginal vault cytology in detecting recurrences after hysterectomy, while HPV testing is sensitive for asymptomatic pelvic recurrence following fertility-sparing surgery. Persistent HPV infection is linked to increased recurrence risk after CRT, but its clinical utility is limited due to low specificity and lack of a validated strategy.
Controversies and gaps
There is uncertainty regarding the survival benefit of intensive surveillance or routine imaging in asymptomatic patients. The evidence is largely retrospective, and there is a lack of robust data supporting the routine use of imaging for asymptomatic individuals. Additionally, the role of HPV in recurrence management after CRT is not fully established due to limited specificity and absence of a validated strategy.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to determine the clinical utility of HPV-based surveillance strategies and to validate the effectiveness of imaging modalities in asymptomatic patients. Practice should focus on individualized, multidisciplinary approaches for recurrence management, considering disease extent, disease-free interval, and prior treatments.

化学免疫治疗时代的晚期肛门鳞状细胞癌:Retifanlimab、Nivolumab 和 治疗 选择的实用框架。

Advanced Anal Squamous Cell Carcinoma in the Chemoimmunotherapy Era: Retifanlimab, Nivolumab, and a Practical Framework for Treatment Selection.
作者： Tamotsu Sagawa, Masahiro Hirakawa, Hiroyuki Nagashima, Koshi Fujikawa
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
晚期肛门鳞状细胞癌 (ASCC) 是一种罕见的 人乳头瘤病毒 相关恶性肿瘤,历史上用于全身治疗的前瞻性证据有限。
证据基础与方法
关键考虑因素包括体能状态、免疫检查点抑制剂资格、HIV感染、自身免疫风险、神经病变、器官功能、肿瘤负荷、局部症状控制和化学免疫治疗后测序。
共识与结论

争议与缺口
At the final overall-survival analysis, median overall survival was 32.8 versus 22.2 months (HR 0.75, 95% CI 0.55-1.01); the regimen has received regulatory authorization in the United States, the European Union, and Japan.
科研与实践启示
EA2176 正在评估卡铂-紫杉醇联合或不联合纳武单抗,并将阐明基于 PD-1 的一线化学免疫疗法是否代表可重复的类别效应。治疗 决策应将 III 期验证的选择与类别效应外推法区分开来,并在适当时整合多学科局部治疗。

Advanced Anal Squamous Cell Carcinoma in the Chemoimmunotherapy Era: Retifanlimab, Nivolumab, and a Practical Framework for Treatment Selection.

Authors: Tamotsu Sagawa, Masahiro Hirakawa, Hiroyuki Nagashima, Koshi Fujikawa
Original abstract (English)
Advanced anal squamous cell carcinoma (ASCC) is a rare human papillomavirus-associated malignancy with historically limited prospective evidence for systemic therapy. InterAAct established carboplatin-paclitaxel as a clinically favorable chemotherapy backbone compared with cisplatin-fluorouracil. POD1UM-303/InterAACT-2 subsequently showed that adding retifanlimab significantly improved progression-free survival in patients with no prior systemic therapy for advanced disease. At the final overall-survival analysis, median overall survival was 32.8 versus 22.2 months (HR 0.75, 95% CI 0.55-1.01); the regimen has received regulatory authorization in the United States, the European Union, and Japan. EA2176 is evaluating carboplatin-paclitaxel with or without nivolumab and will clarify whether first-line PD-1-based chemoimmunotherapy represents a reproducible class effect. This focused narrative review critically summarizes the evidence for retifanlimab- and nivolumab-based strategies and proposes a practical framework for treatment selection. Key considerations include performance status, immune-checkpoint-inhibitor eligibility, HIV infection, autoimmune risk, neuropathy, organ function, tumor burden, local symptom control, and post-chemoimmunotherapy sequencing. Retifanlimab plus carboplatin-paclitaxel currently has the most mature phase III evidence, whereas nivolumab-based chemoimmunotherapy remains investigational pending EA2176. Treatment decisions should distinguish phase III-validated options from class-effect extrapolation and integrate multidisciplinary local therapy when appropriate.

Scope and question

Evidence base and method

Retifanlimab plus carboplatin-paclitaxel currently has the most mature phase III evidence, whereas nivolumab-based chemoimmunotherapy remains investigational pending EA2176.
Controversies and gaps
At the final overall-survival analysis, median overall survival was 32.8 versus 22.2 months (HR 0.75, 95% CI 0.55-1.01); the regimen has received regulatory authorization in the United States, the European Union, and Japan.
